          const extractedFloor = extractFloorFromCodeOrName(targetUnitCode) || 
                                 extractFloorFromCodeOrName((p as any)?.unit) || 
                                 extractFloorFromCodeOrName(p?.code) || 
                                 extractFloorFromCodeOrName(effectiveFilterCode);
          const targetFloor = (p?.location && p.location !== 'all' && p.location !== 'ALL') 
            ? p.location 
            : (extractedFloor || "all");

          if (targetFloor && targetFloor !== 'all' && targetFloor !== 'ALL') {
            updates.globalLocationFilter = [targetFloor];
          }

          updates.layoutTabFilters = {
            store: p?.store || task?.projectStore || "all",
            location: targetFloor,
            projectCode: targetUnitCode,
            _ts: Date.now(),
          };
          break;
        }
        case "gantt": {
          const targetProjectId = p?.id ? String(p.id) : (p?.code || "");
          updates.ganttTabFilterProjectId = targetProjectId;
          if (effectiveFilterCode) {
            safeSetItemSession("taka_gantt_search", effectiveFilterCode);
          }
          // Requirement: Khi mở gantt từ quick navigation chỉ mở project, ko focus task, ko mở task ở trong project (giữ thu gọn giống trong hình)
          updates.ganttTabFocusTaskId = "";

          // Giữ project ở trạng thái thu gọn (collapsed), không mở rộng tasks con
          if (p?.id) {
            const currentExpanded = useAppStore.getState().expandedProjectIds || [];
            updates.expandedProjectIds = currentExpanded.filter(id => id !== String(p.id));
          }

          // Giữ bộ lọc tầng/location của project (vd: L2 như trong hình)
          if (p?.location && p.location !== 'all' && p.location !== 'ALL') {
            updates.globalLocationFilter = [p.location];
          } else {
            updates.globalLocationFilter = [];
          }

          safeSetItemSession("taka_gantt_status_filter", "all");
          safeSetItemSession("taka_gantt_trigger_today", "true");
          triggerGanttToday();
          if (p?.id) {
            triggerChangeFlash(String(p.id), "projects", "QuickNav");
          }
          break;
        }
        case "detail": {
          // If a specific task was explicitly targeted (e.g. user clicked directly on a subtask in Gantt/Search):
          // focus that task and expand the project.
          // If a PROJECT was targeted (task is null/undefined):
          // DO NOT resolve to or invent any child task! Keep focus empty, filter by project code, and keep project collapsed.
          const isTargetingSpecificTask = Boolean(task && task.id);
          const targetTaskId = isTargetingSpecificTask ? (task?.id || "") : "";
          updates.detailTabFocusTaskId = targetTaskId;

          const effectiveCode = isTargetingSpecificTask && task
            ? (computeTaskCode(projects, tasks, task) || task.taskCodePhu || (p ? `${p.code}#${(task.orderIndex !== undefined ? task.orderIndex : 0) + 1}` : "") || p?.code || "")
            : (p?.code || effectiveFilterCode || "");

          updates.detailTabFilterCode = effectiveCode;
          if (effectiveCode) {
            safeSetItemSession("taka_detail_search", effectiveCode);
          }
          if (p?.store || task?.projectStore) {
            updates.detailTabStoreFilter = p?.store || task?.projectStore || "";
          }

          // Reset location and status filter so target project is never hidden in Task Detail
          updates.globalLocationFilter = [];
          safeSetItemSession("taka_detail_status_filter", "all");

          // Requirement: Khi chuyển qua project qua quick navigation thì chỉ mở project, 
          // chưa expand task con để không kích hoạt tải tasks từ Firestore về.
          // Chỉ mở rộng task con nếu đích đến là một công việc (task) cụ thể.
          if (isTargetingSpecificTask && p?.id) {
            const currentExpanded = useAppStore.getState().expandedProjectIds || [];
            const newExpanded = new Set(currentExpanded);
            newExpanded.add(String(p.id));
            updates.expandedProjectIds = Array.from(newExpanded);
            updates.isTasksRequested = true;
          } else {
            // Khi chọn chuyển tới Project: đảm bảo focus task bị xóa sạch và không kích hoạt useDetailFocusTracking
            updates.detailTabFocusTaskId = "";
            updates.ganttTabFocusTaskId = "";
          }

          if (p?.id) {
            triggerChangeFlash(String(p.id), "projects", "QuickNav");
          }
          break;
        }
        case "budget": {
          updates.budgetTabSearchTerm = effectiveFilterCode || "";
          break;
        }
        case "cost": {
          updates.costTabSearchTerm = effectiveFilterCode || "";
          break;
        }
        default:
          break;
      }

      useAppStore.setState(updates);
    });
  };

  const handleDataUndo = async () => {
    if (activeTab === "detail" && detailTabFocusTaskId) {
      const prev =
        tabHistory.length > 0 ? tabHistory[tabHistory.length - 1] : "gantt";
      setTabHistory((h) => h.slice(0, -1));
      setDetailTabFocusTaskId("");
      setDetailTabFilterCode("");
      setActiveTab(prev);
      return;
    }

    if (activeTab === "taskDelegation") {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("taka_clear_delegation_focus"));
    }

    if (undoStack.length > 0) {
      await undoManager.pop();
    } else if (tabHistory.length > 0) {
      const prev = tabHistory[tabHistory.length - 1];
      setTabHistory((h) => h.slice(0, -1));
      if (activeTab === "detail") {
        setDetailTabFocusTaskId("");
        setDetailTabFilterCode("");
      }
      if (activeTab === "gantt") setGanttTabFocusTaskId("");
      setActiveTab(prev);
    }
  };

  
  const handleGoBack = () => {
    if (activeTab === "detail" && detailTabFocusTaskId) {
      const prev = tabHistory.length > 0 ? tabHistory[tabHistory.length - 1] : "gantt";
      setTabHistory((h) => h.slice(0, -1));
      setDetailTabFocusTaskId("");
      setDetailTabFilterCode("");
      setActiveTab(prev);
      return;
    }
    if (tabHistory.length > 0) {
      const prev = tabHistory[tabHistory.length - 1];
      setTabHistory((h) => h.slice(0, -1));
      if (activeTab === "detail") {
        setDetailTabFocusTaskId("");
        setDetailTabFilterCode("");
      }
      if (activeTab === "gantt") setGanttTabFocusTaskId("");
      setActiveTab(prev);
    }
  };

  const handleGoBackRef = useRef(handleGoBack);
  useEffect(() => {
    handleGoBackRef.current = handleGoBack;
  });

  const handleDataUndoRef = useRef(handleDataUndo);
  useEffect(() => {
    handleDataUndoRef.current = handleDataUndo;
  });

  const handleDataRedo = async () => {
    if (redoStack.length > 0) {
      await undoManager.redo();
    }
  };

  const handleDataRedoRef = useRef(handleDataRedo);
  useEffect(() => {
    handleDataRedoRef.current = handleDataRedo;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const activeElement = document.activeElement;
      const isTyping =
        activeElement &&
        (activeElement.tagName === "INPUT" ||
          activeElement.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
          (activeElement as HTMLElement).isContentEditable);

      // Esc: Close open modals / blur / print preview
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        if (isPrintPreview) {
          setIsPrintPreview(false);
          return;
        }
        if (toast) {
          setToast(null);
        }
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        if (state.isProjectModalOpen) state.setIsProjectModalOpen(false);
        if (state.isTaskModalOpen) state.setIsTaskModalOpen(false);
        if (state.isUserModalOpen) state.setIsUserModalOpen(false);
        if (state.isLoginModalOpen) state.setIsLoginModalOpen(false);
        if (activeElement && typeof (activeElement as HTMLElement).blur === "function") {
          (activeElement as HTMLElement).blur();
        }
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + K / Cmd + K: Command Palette
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        state.setIsCommandPaletteOpen(!state.isCommandPaletteOpen);
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + Z / Cmd + Z: Undo action (when not actively editing text in input)
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "z" && !e.shiftKey) {
        if (!isTyping) {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleDataUndoRef.current?.();
        }
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + Y / Cmd + Y OR Ctrl + Shift + Z / Cmd + Shift + Z: Redo action
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key.toLowerCase() === "y" || (e.key.toLowerCase() === "z" && e.shiftKey))) {
        if (!isTyping) {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleDataRedoRef.current?.();
        }
        return;
      }

      // Shift + F: Toggle Focus Mode
      if (e.shiftKey && e.key.toUpperCase() === "F" && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
        if (!isTyping) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setIsFocusMode(!isFocusMode);
        }
        return;
      }

      // If typing inside an input/textarea or holding modifier keys, ignore single-key shortcuts
      if (isTyping) return;
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;

      // /: Focus global search input (Projects, Tasks, Assignees)
      if (e.key === "/") {
        e.preventDefault();
        const globalSearchInput = document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(
          'input[data-global-search-input="true"]'
        );
        if (globalSearchInput) {
          globalSearchInput.focus();
          globalSearchInput.select?.();
        } else {
          const searchInput = document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(
            'input[placeholder*="Search" i], input[placeholder*="Tìm kiếm" i], input[type="search"]'
          );
          if (searchInput) {
            searchInput.focus();
            searchInput.select?.();
          }
        }
        return;
      }

      // Backspace: Back to previous page / tab history
      if (e.key === "Backspace") {
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        if (state.isProjectModalOpen || state.isTaskModalOpen || state.isUserModalOpen || state.isLoginModalOpen) {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (state.isProjectModalOpen) state.setIsProjectModalOpen(false);
          if (state.isTaskModalOpen) state.setIsTaskModalOpen(false);
          if (state.isUserModalOpen) state.setIsUserModalOpen(false);
          if (state.isLoginModalOpen) state.setIsLoginModalOpen(false);
          return;
        }
        e.preventDefault();
        handleGoBackRef.current?.();
        return;
      }

      // P: New Project
      if (e.key.toLowerCase() === "p") {
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        const userRole = state.currentUser?.role;
        const canWrite = userRole === "admin" || (userRole && state.permissionMatrix[userRole as AppRole]?.Write?.general);
        if (canWrite) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setEditingProject(null);
          setIsReadOnlyModal(false);
          setModalInitialTab("details");
          setIsProjectModalOpen(true);
        }
        return;
      }

      // T: New Task
      if (e.key.toLowerCase() === "t") {
        if (!isAppReadyRef.current) return;
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        const userRole = state.currentUser?.role;
        const canWrite = userRole === "admin" || (userRole && state.permissionMatrix[userRole as AppRole]?.Write?.detail);
        if (canWrite) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setEditingTask(null);
          setIsReadOnlyModal(false);
          setModalInitialTab("details");
          const safeP = projects && projects.length > 0 ? projects[0] : null;
          setPrefillProjectCode(safeP?.code || "");
          setPrefillProjectYear(safeP?.year || null);
          setIsTaskModalOpen(true);
        }
        return;
      }

      // 1 - 9: Switch tabs
      const tabNumberMap: Record<string, TabId> = {
        "1": "general",
        "2": "calendar",
        "3": "gantt",
        "4": "detail",
        "5": "budget",
        "6": "cost",
        "7": "layout",
        "8": "summary",
        "9": "taskDelegation",
      };
      if (tabNumberMap[e.key]) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleTabChange(tabNumberMap[e.key]);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [
    isFocusMode,
    projects,
    handleTabChange,
    setIsFocusMode,
    setEditingProject,
    setEditingTask,
    setIsReadOnlyModal,
    setModalInitialTab,
    setIsProjectModalOpen,
    setIsTaskModalOpen,
    setPrefillProjectCode,
    setPrefillProjectYear,
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let startX = 0;
    let startY = 0;
    let touchTarget: HTMLElement | null = null;
    let isMultiTouch = false;

    const handleTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      if (e.touches.length > 1) {
        isMultiTouch = true;
        return;
      }
      isMultiTouch = false;
      startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
      startY = e.touches[0].clientY;
      touchTarget = e.target as HTMLElement;
    };

    const isScrollableX = (el: HTMLElement | null): boolean => {
      if (!el || el === document.body || el === document.documentElement)
        return false;
      const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      const overflowX = style.overflowX;
      if (overflowX === "auto" || overflowX === "scroll") {
        if (el.scrollWidth > el.clientWidth) {
          return true;
        }
      }
      return isScrollableX(el.parentElement);
    };

    const isScrollableY = (el: HTMLElement | null): boolean => {
      if (!el || el === document.body || el === document.documentElement)
        return false;
      const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      const overflowY = style.overflowY;
      if (overflowY === "auto" || overflowY === "scroll") {
        if (el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight) {
          return true;
        }
      }
      return isScrollableY(el.parentElement);
    };

    const handleTouchEnd = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      if (isMultiTouch) return;

      // Disable swipe-to-tab if the user is zoomed in
      if (window.visualViewport && window.visualViewport.scale > 1) {
        return;
      }

      const endX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
      const endY = e.changedTouches[0].clientY;

      const diffX = startX - endX; // Positive means swiped left
      const diffY = startY - endY;

      // Check if user has selected text
      const selection = window.getSelection();
      if (selection && selection.toString().length > 0) {
        return;
      }

      // Check if target is an input field
      if (
        touchTarget &&
        touchTarget.closest('input, textarea, [contenteditable="true"]')
      ) {
        return;
      }

      // Handle Tab Switch Gestures
      // Handle swipe right to go back
      if (Math.abs(diffX) > 60 && Math.abs(diffX) > Math.abs(diffY) * 1.5) {
        // Swiped right from left edge (always trigger GoBack, overriding scrollable content)
        if (diffX < 0 && startX <= 40) { 
          handleGoBackRef.current();
        } else if (!isScrollableX(touchTarget)) {
          // Other non-edge swipe gestures can go here
        }
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("touchstart", handleTouchStart);
    document.addEventListener("touchend", handleTouchEnd);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("touchstart", handleTouchStart);
      document.removeEventListener("touchend", handleTouchEnd);
    };
  }, [activeTab, visibleTabs]);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    const guest: AppUser = {
      id: "guest",
      username: "Guest",
      role: "guest",
      canSee: false,
      canWrite: false,
    };
    setCurrentUser(guest);
    safeSetItemLocal("taka_auth_v1", JSON.stringify(guest));
    handleTabChange("calendar");
  };

  const handlePrintClick = () => {
    setIsPrintPreview(true);
  };

  const canSee = currentUser.role === "admin" || currentUser.canSee;
  const userRole = (
    currentUser?.role === "admin"
      ? "admin"
      : (currentUser?.role as string)?.toUpperCase() === "CP"
      ? "CP"
      : (currentUser?.role as string)?.toLowerCase() === "manager"
      ? "Manager"
      : (currentUser?.role as string)?.toLowerCase() === "staff"
      ? "Staff"
      : (currentUser?.role as string)?.toLowerCase() === "external"
      ? "External"
      : currentUser?.role
  ) as AppRole;

  const visibleLocations = locations.filter(
    (loc) => locationVisibility[loc] !== false,
  );

  const activeLocations = useMemo(() => {
    const used = new Set<string>();
    enrichedProjects.forEach((p) => {
      if (p.location) used.add(p.location);
    });
    return visibleLocations.filter((loc) => used.has(loc));
  }, [visibleLocations, enrichedProjects]);

  const getPaperDimensions = () => {
    if (printPaperSize === 'A2') return { w: 420, h: 594 };
    if (printPaperSize === 'A3') return { w: 297, h: 420 };
    return { w: 210, h: 297 };
  };
  const { w: paperW, h: paperH } = getPaperDimensions();
  const printWidth = printOrientation === 'landscape' ? paperH : paperW;
  const printHeight = printOrientation === 'landscape' ? paperW : paperH;

  const printFilteredData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isPrintPreview || activeTab !== "gantt") return { sortedProjects: [], tasksByProj: new Map(), expanded: [] };

    const expandedStr = safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_expanded_projects");
    const expanded = expandedStr ? JSON.parse(expandedStr) : [];
      
    const filterLocationStr = safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_filter_location");
    const filterLocation: string[] = filterLocationStr ? JSON.parse(filterLocationStr) : [];
    const filterProjectStatus = safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_filter_project_status") || "active";
    const filterYear = safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_filter_year") || "all";
    const filterStore = safeGetItemLocal(`taka_store_filter_${currentUser?.id}`) || "all";
    const searchTerm = safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_search") || "";

    const tasksByProj = new Map<string, typeof filteredTasks>();
    filteredTasks.forEach(t => {
      const pKeys: string[] = [];
      if (t.party === "All") {
        enrichedProjects.forEach(p => pKeys.push(`${p.code || ""}-${p.year || ""}-${p.store || ""}`));
      } else {
        enrichedProjects.filter(p => p.location === t.party).forEach(p => pKeys.push(`${p.code || ""}-${p.year || ""}-${p.store || ""}`));
      }
      pKeys.forEach(key => {
        if (!tasksByProj.has(key)) tasksByProj.set(key, []);
        tasksByProj.get(key)!.push(t);
      });
    });

    const actuallyVisibleProjects = enrichedProjects.filter((p) => {
      if (filterYear !== "all" && String(p.year) !== filterYear) return false;
      if (filterStore !== "all" && p.store !== filterStore) return false;
      if (filterLocation.length > 0 && !filterLocation.includes(p.location)) return false;

      if (filterProjectStatus && filterProjectStatus !== "all") {
        const pStart = p.computedTakStart || p.start || "9999-12-31";
        const pEnd = p.computedTakCompl || p.handover;
        const pStatus = getStatus(
          pStart,
          pEnd,
          p.computedOpening || p.opening,
          p.code,
        );

        if (filterProjectStatus === "active") {
          if (pStatus !== "On process" && pStatus !== "Upcoming") return false;
        } else if (pStatus !== filterProjectStatus) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      const pKey = `${p.code || ""}-${p.year || ""}-${p.store || ""}`;
      const projectTasks = tasksByProj.get(pKey) || [];

      if (searchTerm) {
        return matchesAdvancedSearch(
          searchTerm,
          [p.code, p.name, p.store, p.location],
          [],
          (term, andTerms) => {
            return projectTasks.some((t) => {
              return andTerms.every((tTerm) => {
                const inProject = [p.code, p.name, p.store, p.location].some(
                  (v) =>
                    String(v || "")
                      .toLowerCase()
                      .includes(tTerm),
                );
                if (inProject) return true;
                return [t.name, t.party].some((v) =>
                  String(v || "")
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .includes(tTerm),
                );
              });
            });
          },
        );
      }
      return true;
    });

    const statusPriority: Record<string, number> = {
      "On process": 1,
      Upcoming: 2,
      Completed: 3,
      Handover: 4,
      Canceled: 5,
    };

    const sortedProjects = [...actuallyVisibleProjects].sort((a, b) => {
      const pinA = a.isPinned ? 1 : 0;
      const pinB = b.isPinned ? 1 : 0;
      if (pinA !== pinB) return pinB - pinA;

      const aStart = a.computedTakStart || a.start || "9999-12-31";
      const aEnd = a.computedTakCompl || a.handover;
      const aStatus = getStatus(aStart, aEnd, a.computedOpening || a.opening, a.code);

      const bStart = b.computedTakStart || b.start || "9999-12-31";
      const bEnd = b.computedTakCompl || b.handover;
      const bStatus = getStatus(bStart, bEnd, b.computedOpening || b.opening, b.code);

      const pA = statusPriority[aStatus] || 99;
      const pB = statusPriority[bStatus] || 99;

      if (pA !== pB) return pA - pB;

      if (aStatus === "On process") {
        const today = new Date();
        today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

        const finishA = parseDateTime(aEnd || a.computedOpening || a.opening);
        const finishB = parseDateTime(bEnd || b.computedOpening || b.opening);

        if (finishA && finishB) {
          const diffA = Math.abs(finishA.getTime() - today.getTime());
          const diffB = Math.abs(finishB.getTime() - today.getTime());
          if (diffA !== diffB) {
            return diffA - diffB;
          }
        }
      }
      return aStart.localeCompare(bStart);
    });

    return { sortedProjects, tasksByProj, expanded };
  }, [isPrintPreview, activeTab, enrichedProjects, filteredTasks, currentUser?.id]);

  const printFilterContext = useMemo(() => {
    let fallbackCode = "";
    if (activeTab === "gantt" && printFilteredData.sortedProjects.length === 1) {
      fallbackCode = printFilteredData.sortedProjects[0].code;
    }
    return getPrintFilterContext({
      activeTab,
      globalStoreFilter,
      globalYearFilter,
      currentUser,
      detailTabFilterCode,
            layoutTabFilters,
      fallbackProjectCode: fallbackCode,
    });
  }, [
    activeTab,
    globalStoreFilter,
    globalYearFilter,
    currentUser,
    detailTabFilterCode,
        layoutTabFilters,
    printFilteredData,
  ]);

  const printDates = useMemo(() => {
    let minDate = new Date();
    let maxDate = new Date();
    let hasDates = false;

    const { sortedProjects, tasksByProj } = printFilteredData;

    if (printRangeType === "custom") {
       if (printStartDate) { minDate = parseDateTime(printStartDate) || new Date(); hasDates = true; }
       if (printEndDate) { maxDate = parseDateTime(printEndDate) || new Date(); hasDates = true; }
    } else {
      const updateMinMax = (dateStr: string | undefined | null) => {
        if (!dateStr) return;
        const d = parseDateTime(dateStr);
        if (d && !isNaN(d.getTime())) {
          if (!hasDates) {
            minDate = d;
            maxDate = d;
            hasDates = true;
          } else {
            if (d < minDate) minDate = d;
            if (d > maxDate) maxDate = d;
          }
        }
      };

      sortedProjects.forEach((p) => {
        updateMinMax(p.computedTakStart || p.start);
        updateMinMax(p.computedTakCompl || p.handover);
        updateMinMax(p.computedOpening || p.opening);
        
        const pKey = `${p.code || ""}-${p.year || ""}-${p.store || ""}`;
        const projectTasks = tasksByProj.get(pKey) || [];
        projectTasks.forEach(t => {
          updateMinMax(t.start);
          updateMinMax(t.finish);
        });
      });
    }

    if (hasDates) {
      if (printRangeType !== "custom") {
         minDate = new Date(minDate.getFullYear(), minDate.getMonth() - 1, 1);
         maxDate = new Date(maxDate.getFullYear(), maxDate.getMonth() + 2, 0);
      }
    } else {
      minDate = new Date();
      minDate = new Date(minDate.getFullYear(), minDate.getMonth() - 1, 1);
      maxDate = new Date();
      maxDate = new Date(maxDate.getFullYear(), maxDate.getMonth() + 2, 0);
    }

    return { minDate, maxDate };
  }, [printFilteredData, printRangeType, printStartDate, printEndDate]);

  const [measuredContentSize, setMeasuredContentSize] = useState<{ width: number; height: number }>({ width: 0, height: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPrintPreview) return;
    const updateSize = () => {
      const wrapper = document.querySelector('.print-content-wrapper') as HTMLElement;
      if (wrapper) {
        const w = Math.max(100, wrapper.scrollWidth || wrapper.offsetWidth);
        const h = Math.max(100, wrapper.scrollHeight || wrapper.offsetHeight);
        setMeasuredContentSize(prev => (prev.width === w && prev.height === h ? prev : { width: w, height: h }));
      }
    };
    const t = setTimeout(updateSize, 80);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [isPrintPreview, activeTab, printOrientation, printPaperSize, ganttViewMode]);

  const printScale = useMemo(() => {
    if (printScaleMode === "default") return 100;
    if (printScaleMode === "custom") return printScaleCustom;

    const targetWidthPx = (printWidth - 20) * 3.779527559;
    const targetHeightPx = (printHeight - 20) * 3.779527559;

    let contentW = measuredContentSize.width;
    let contentH = measuredContentSize.height;

    if (contentW <= 0 || contentH <= 0) {
      if (activeTab === "gantt") {
        const { sortedProjects } = printFilteredData;
        const { minDate, maxDate } = printDates;
        const savedDayWidth = safeGetDeviceItemLocal("taka_gantt_day_width");
        const currentDayWidth = savedDayWidth ? Math.round(parseFloat(savedDayWidth)) : 4;
        const savedSideWidth = safeGetDeviceItemLocal("taka_gantt_side_width");
        const currentSideWidth = savedSideWidth ? parseInt(savedSideWidth, 10) : 300;
        const totalDays = Math.round((maxDate.getTime() - minDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) + 1;
        contentW = currentSideWidth + (totalDays * currentDayWidth);
        contentH = 160 + sortedProjects.length * 44;
      } else {
        contentW = 1200;
        contentH = 800;
      }
    }

    if (printScaleMode === "fit-cols") {
      const scale = Math.floor((targetWidthPx / contentW) * 100);
      return Math.max(10, Math.min(scale, 100));
    }
    
    if (printScaleMode === "fit-rows") {
      const scale = Math.floor((targetHeightPx / contentH) * 100);
      return Math.max(10, Math.min(scale, 100));
    }
    
    if (printScaleMode === "fit-page") {
      const scaleCols = Math.floor((targetWidthPx / contentW) * 100);
      const scaleRows = Math.floor((targetHeightPx / contentH) * 100);
      return Math.max(10, Math.min(scaleCols, scaleRows, 100));
    }

    return 100;
  }, [printScaleMode, printScaleCustom, printWidth, printHeight, measuredContentSize, activeTab, printFilteredData, printDates]);

  const ganttPrintPages = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isPrintPreview || activeTab !== "gantt") return null;
    return [{ dateRange: undefined as any }];
  }, [isPrintPreview, activeTab, printFilteredData, printDates]);

  const handleExportToExcel = async () => {
    if (isExportingPDF || activeTab === 'gantt') return;
    setIsExportingPDF(true);
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 150));
    try {
      await exportToExcel({
        activeTab,
        sortedProjects: printFilteredData.sortedProjects,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setIsExportingPDF(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePrintToPDF = async () => {
    if (isExportingPDF) return;
    if (printTarget === 'browser') {
      window.print();
      return;
    }
    setIsExportingPDF(true);
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
    try {
      await exportToPdf({
        activeTab,
        printOrientation,
        printPaperSize,
        printDpi,
        printScaleMode,
        printScale,
        printWidth,
        printHeight,
        printFilterHeader,
        printFilterContext,
        printDate,
        printWatermark,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Export to PDF failed:", err);
      useAppStore.getState().setToast({ title: "Thông báo", message: String("Xuất PDF thất bại. Vui lòng thử lại.") });
    } finally {
      setIsExportingPDF(false);
    }
  };


  return (
    <div className={`min-h-[100dvh] p-2 lg:p-4 flex flex-col bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#131314] text-slate-800 dark:text-[#f3f3f3] font-sans pt-[env(safe-area-inset-top)] pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] pl-[env(safe-area-inset-left)] pr-[env(safe-area-inset-right)] print:p-0 transition-colors ${isPrintPreview ? 'print-preview-container' : ''}`}>
      <style>{`
        /* Global print overrides for better visibility */
        @media print, screen {
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active {
             /* Darker text */
             --tw-text-opacity: 1;
          }
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .border-slate-200,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .border-b,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .border-r {
             border-color: #475569 !important; /* slate-600 */
          }
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .text-slate-500,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .text-slate-600 {
             color: #1e293b !important; /* slate-800 */
             font-weight: 700 !important;
          }
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .text-slate-700,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .text-slate-800,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .text-slate-900 {
             color: #000000 !important; /* black */
             font-weight: 800 !important;
          }
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active .bg-slate-100 {
             background-color: #e2e8f0 !important; /* slate-200 for better contrast */
          }
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active table,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active th,
          html:not(.dark) .print-preview-content-active td {
             border-color: #334155 !important; /* slate-700 */
          }
        }
        @media print {
          @page {
            size: ${printPaperSize} ${printOrientation};
            margin: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '5mm !important' : '10mm !important'};
          }
          .print-content-wrapper {
            transform: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? 'none' : `scale(${printScale / 100})`};
            transform-origin: top left;
            width: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100% !important' : `${100 / (printScale / 100)}% !important`};
            height: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100% !important' : `${100 / (printScale / 100)}% !important`};
          }
          #live-layout-canvas-container {
            width: 100% !important;
            height: 100% !important;
            display: flex !important;
            align-items: center !important;
            justify-content: center !important;
          }
          #live-layout-canvas-container canvas {
            max-width: 100% !important;
            max-height: 95vh !important;
            object-fit: contain !important;
          }
          .hide-on-actual-print, .print\:hidden {
            display: none !important;
          }
        }
        ${isPrintPreview ? `
          @media screen {
            body.print-preview-active {
              background-color: #94a3b8 !important;
            }
            .print-preview-container {
              padding-top: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '50px !important' : '100px !important'};
              padding-bottom: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '20px !important' : '60px !important'};
              background-color: transparent !important;
              overflow: auto !important;
            }
            .print-paper {
              width: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100% !important' : 'max-content !important'};
              height: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100% !important' : 'max-content !important'};
              min-width: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100%' : `${printWidth}mm`};
              min-height: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '85vh' : `${printHeight}mm`};
              background-color: white !important;
              margin: 0 auto;
              box-shadow: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5) !important;
              padding: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '0 !important' : '10mm !important'};
              position: relative;
              overflow: hidden;
              display: flex;
              flex-direction: column;
            }
            .print-preview-content-active {
              transform: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? 'none' : `scale(${printScale / 100})`};
              transform-origin: top left;
              width: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100% !important' : 'max-content !important'};
              height: ${activeTab === 'layout' ? '100% !important' : 'max-content !important'};
              background-color: transparent !important;
              border: none !important;
              box-shadow: none !important;
              border-radius: 0 !important;
              padding: 0 !important;
              flex-grow: 1 !important;
            }
          }
        ` : ""}
      `}</style>
      
      {isPrintPreview && (
        <PrintOptionsPanel
          activeTab={activeTab}
          onClose={() => setIsPrintPreview(false)}
          onExportToExcel={handleExportToExcel}
          onPrintToPDF={handlePrintToPDF}
        />
      )}

      {ganttPrintPages ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 w-full items-center bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-800 p-8 print:p-0 print:bg-transparent">
          {ganttPrintPages.map((pageData, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="print-paper relative flex flex-col shrink-0" style={{ pageBreakAfter: idx < ganttPrintPages.length - 1 ? 'always' : 'auto' }}>
              {printWatermark !== "none" && (
                <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none flex items-center justify-center z-[150] overflow-hidden" style={{ opacity: 0.15 }}>
                  <span 
                    className="font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest text-center"
                    style={{ 
                      fontSize: printOrientation === 'landscape' ? '12vw' : '15vw',
                      transform: 'rotate(-45deg)'
                    }}
                  >
                    {printWatermark}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
              {printDate && (
                <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 z-[90]">
                  Printed on: {new Date().toLocaleString()} - Page {idx + 1}
                </div>
              )}
              {printFilterHeader && (
                <div className="w-full px-4 pt-3 pb-1 bg-white dark:bg-[#1e1e20] shrink-0">
                  <PrintFilterHeader
                    context={printFilterContext}
                    pageIndex={idx}
                    totalPages={ganttPrintPages.length}
                  />
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="w-full flex-grow flex flex-col bg-white dark:bg-[#1e1e20] p-0 print-content-wrapper print-preview-content-active relative overflow-hidden">
                                  {isPrintPreview && (
                    <Suspense fallback={null}>
                      <PrintPreviewOverlay
                        printScale={printScale}
                        printPaperSize={printPaperSize}
                        printOrientation={printOrientation}
                        printScaleMode={printScaleMode}
                      />
                    </Suspense>
                  )}
                 <Profiler id={`GanttTab-Page-${idx}`} onRender={onRenderCallback}>
                   <GanttTab
                     
                     printDateRangeOverride={pageData.dateRange}
                     
                     
                     
                     
                     
                     initialFocusTaskId={ganttTabFocusTaskId}
                     onClearFocus={() => setGanttTabFocusTaskId("")}
                     onNavigateToLayout={handleNavigateToLayout}
                     onNavigateToTaskDetail={handleNavigateToTaskDetail}
                   />
                 </Profiler>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className={isPrintPreview ? "print-paper relative flex-grow flex flex-col w-full" : "w-full flex-grow flex flex-col"}>
          {isPrintPreview && printWatermark !== "none" && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none flex items-center justify-center z-[150] overflow-hidden" style={{ opacity: 0.15 }}>
              <span 
                className="font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest text-center"
                style={{ 
                  fontSize: printOrientation === 'landscape' ? '12vw' : '15vw',
                  transform: 'rotate(-45deg)'
                }}
              >
                {printWatermark}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          {printDate && isPrintPreview && (
            <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 z-[90]">
              Printed on: {new Date().toLocaleString()}
            </div>
          )}
          {printFilterHeader && isPrintPreview && activeTab !== 'layout' && (
            <div className="w-full px-4 pt-3 pb-1 bg-white dark:bg-[#1e1e20] shrink-0">
              <PrintFilterHeader context={printFilterContext} />
            </div>
          )}

          <div className={`w-full flex-grow flex flex-col bg-white dark:bg-[#1e1e20] ${
            activeTab === 'layout' 
              ? 'p-0 pb-0 border-none shadow-none rounded-none' 
              : activeTab === 'picture_mapping' 
              ? 'p-1.5 sm:p-2.5 pb-2 sm:pb-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#333336]' 
              : 'p-4 lg:p-6 pb-20 lg:pb-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#333336]'
          } transition-colors print:p-0 print:m-0 print:border-none print:shadow-none print:rounded-none print-content-wrapper ${isPrintPreview ? 'print-preview-content-active' : ''}`}>
          {/* Floating Ribbon Display Options button in Full-screen mode */}
          {ribbonDisplayMode === 'fullscreen' && (
            <div className="fixed top-3 right-4 z-[9999] print:hidden">
              <RibbonDisplayMenu variant="floating" />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Header - Only visible when ribbonDisplayMode is 'always_show' */}
          {ribbonDisplayMode === 'always_show' && (
            <div className="print:hidden relative z-[200]">
              <Header
                cloudStatus={cloudStatus}
                onLoginClick={() => setIsLoginModalOpen(true)}
                onLogoutClick={handleLogout}
                onPrintClick={handlePrintClick}
                onTabChange={handleTabChange}
                activeTab={activeTab}
                notificationCount={unreadCount}
                onUndo={handleDataUndo}
                canUndo={undoStack.length > 0 || tabHistory.length > 0}
                hasDataToUndo={undoStack.length > 0}
                undoDescription={
                  undoStack.length > 0
                    ? `Undo: ${undoStack[undoStack.length - 1].description}`
                    : tabHistory.length > 0
                      ? `Go back to previous page`
                      : undefined
                }
                onRedo={handleDataRedo}
                canRedo={redoStack.length > 0}
                hasDataToRedo={redoStack.length > 0}
                redoDescription={
                  redoStack.length > 0
                    ? `Redo: ${redoStack[redoStack.length - 1].description}`
                    : undefined
                }
                onToggleTheme={toggleTheme}
                isFocusMode={isFocusMode}
                onToggleFocusMode={() => setIsFocusMode(!isFocusMode)}
                isPrintPreview={isPrintPreview}
                onPrintPreviewToggle={() => setIsPrintPreview(!isPrintPreview)}
                onDeepLink={handleDeepLinkString}
                onQuickNavigate={handleQuickNavigate}
              />
            </div>
          )}

        {/* Navigation - Visible in 'always_show' and 'tabs_only' modes */}
        {canSee && !isFocusMode && ribbonDisplayMode !== 'fullscreen' && (
          <div className="print:hidden relative z-[150]">
            <Navigation
              activeTab={activeTab}
              onTabChange={(tab) => {
                if (tab === "cost") {
                                  }
                if (tab === "budget") {
                  setBudgetTabSearchTerm("");
                }
                if (tab === "detail") {
                  setDetailTabFilterCode("");
                  setDetailTabStoreFilter("");
                }
                handleTabChange(tab);
              }}
              
              
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {!canSee ? (
          <div className="flex-grow flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-slate-50/50 border-2 border-dashed border-slate-200 rounded-xl m-4 print:hidden">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 text-slate-300 mb-4">
              <svg fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  strokeWidth="1.5"
                  d="M12 15v2m-6 4h12a2 2 0 002-2v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2zm10-10V7a4 4 0 00-8 0v4h8z"
                ></path>
              </svg>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-700">
              Access Restricted
            </h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm max-w-xs text-center mt-2">
              Please log in with a valid account to view and manage project
              data.
            </p>
            <button
              title="Button"
              onClick={() => setIsLoginModalOpen(true)}
              className="mt-6 px-8 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-95"
            >
              Login Now
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <AppTabRouter
            activeTab={activeTab}
            currentUser={currentUser}
            userRole={userRole}
            permissionMatrix={permissionMatrix}
            layoutTabFilters={layoutTabFilters}
            setLayoutTabFilters={setLayoutTabFilters}
            ganttTabFocusTaskId={ganttTabFocusTaskId}
            setGanttTabFocusTaskId={setGanttTabFocusTaskId}
            detailTabFilterCode={detailTabFilterCode}
            setDetailTabFilterCode={setDetailTabFilterCode}
            globalStoreFilter={globalStoreFilter}
            globalYearFilter={globalYearFilter}
            setGlobalStoreFilter={setGlobalStoreFilter}
            setGlobalYearFilter={setGlobalYearFilter}
            detailTabFocusTaskId={detailTabFocusTaskId}
            setDetailTabFocusTaskId={setDetailTabFocusTaskId}
            detailReturnScrollId={detailReturnScrollId}
            setDetailReturnScrollId={setDetailReturnScrollId}
            budgetTabSearchTerm={budgetTabSearchTerm}
            setBudgetTabSearchTerm={setBudgetTabSearchTerm}
            costTabSearchTerm={costTabSearchTerm}
            setCostTabSearchTerm={setCostTabSearchTerm}
            tabHistory={tabHistory}
            setTabHistory={setTabHistory}
            locationVisibility={locationVisibility}
            activeNotifications={activeNotifications}
            readNotifs={readNotifs}
            notificationFilterId={notificationFilterId}
            setNotificationFilterId={setNotificationFilterId}
            unreadCount={unreadCount}
            handleRequestPermission={handleRequestPermission}
            notifActions={notifActions}
            notificationRules={notificationRules}
            handleTabChange={handleTabChange}
            handleNavigateToLayout={handleNavigateToLayout}
            handleNavigateToTaskDetail={handleNavigateToTaskDetail}
            setQuickNavTarget={setQuickNavTarget}
            setEditingProject={setEditingProject}
            setEditingTask={setEditingTask}
            setEditingUser={setEditingUser}
            setIsReadOnlyModal={setIsReadOnlyModal}
            setModalInitialTab={setModalInitialTab}
            setIsProjectModalOpen={setIsProjectModalOpen}
            setIsTaskModalOpen={setIsTaskModalOpen}
            setIsUserModalOpen={setIsUserModalOpen}
            setPrefillProjectCode={setPrefillProjectCode}
            setPrefillProjectYear={setPrefillProjectYear}
            setPrefillProjectStore={setPrefillProjectStore}
            isAppReadyRef={isAppReadyRef}
            executeDeepLinkRef={executeDeepLinkRef}
            onRenderCallback={onRenderCallback}
          />
        )}
      </div>
      </div>
      )}

      <AppModals
        onLogin={handleLogin}
        onTabChange={handleTabChange}
      />

      {quickNavTarget && (
        <ProjectQuickNavModal
          target={quickNavTarget}
          isOpen={!!quickNavTarget}
          onClose={() => setQuickNavTarget(null)}
          onNavigate={handleQuickNavigate}
        />
      )}

      {isCommandPaletteOpen && (
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <CommandPaletteModal
            isOpen={isCommandPaletteOpen}
            onClose={() => setIsCommandPaletteOpen(false)}
            onTabChange={handleTabChange}
            onToggleTheme={toggleTheme}
            onToggleFocusMode={() => setIsFocusMode(!isFocusMode)}
            onPrintPreviewToggle={() => setIsPrintPreview(!isPrintPreview)}
          />
        </Suspense>
      )}

      {toast && (
        <Toast
          title={toast.title}
          message={toast.message}
          onClose={() => setToast(null)}
        />
      )}


      <div className="fixed bottom-1 right-3 text-[10px] text-slate-400 z-50 pointer-events-none">
        copyright by Nam Hung CP
      </div>
          {showTour && (
            <Suspense fallback={null}>
              <AppTour onClose={handleCloseTour} />
            </Suspense>
          )}
      {isExportingPDF && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[99999] bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-white print:hidden hide-on-actual-print">
          <svg className="w-12 h-12 animate-spin text-blue-500 mb-4" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4"></circle>
            <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
          </svg>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">Generating High Quality Document...</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-300 mt-2 text-sm text-center max-w-sm">Please wait while we render your chart. This may take a moment depending on the project size.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/Gantt/useGanttProjectsFilter.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useCallback, useDeferredValue, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Project, Task, AppUser } from '../../types';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { 
  getStatus, 
  getProjectDatesAndStatus,
  matchProjectStatusFilter,
  parseDateTime, 
  getUserAllowedStores, 
  getEffectiveStoreFilter, 
  matchesAdvancedSearch 
} from '../../lib/utils';
import { useLocationOptions } from '../../hooks/useLocationOptions';
import { safeGetItemSession, safeSetItemSession } from '../../lib/storage';

interface UseGanttProjectsFilterParams {
  projects: Project[];
  tasks: Task[];
  delegationGroups: any[];
  currentUser: AppUser;
}

export function useGanttProjectsFilter({
  projects,
  tasks,
  delegationGroups,
  currentUser
}: UseGanttProjectsFilterParams) {
  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setGlobalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalYearFilter);
  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setGlobalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalStoreFilter);
  const effectiveStore = getEffectiveStoreFilter(globalStoreFilter, currentUser);
  const globalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalLocationFilter);
  const setGlobalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalLocationFilter);
  const setExpandedProjects = useAppStore(state => state.setExpandedProjectIds);
  const ganttTabFilterProjectId = useAppStore(state => state.ganttTabFilterProjectId);
  const setGanttTabFilterProjectId = useAppStore(state => state.setGanttTabFilterProjectId);

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState(() => safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_search") || "");
  const deferredSearchTerm = useDeferredValue(searchTerm);

  // Sync QuickNav targeted project filter
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ganttTabFilterProjectId) {
      const targetProj = projects.find(
        p => (p.id && String(p.id) === String(ganttTabFilterProjectId)) ||
             (p.code && String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase() === String(ganttTabFilterProjectId).trim().toUpperCase())
      );
      const codeToDisplay = targetProj?.code || ganttTabFilterProjectId;
      setSearchTerm(codeToDisplay);
      setFilterProjectStatus('all');
    }
  }, [ganttTabFilterProjectId, projects]);

  const [filterProjectStatus, setFilterProjectStatus] = useState<string>(() => {
    if ((safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_search") || "").trim()) return 'all';
    return safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days';
  });

  const lastSelectedStatusFilterRef = useRef<string>(
    safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days'
  );
  const lastSelectedLocationFilterRef = useRef<string[]>(globalLocationFilter);
  const wasSearchingRef = useRef<boolean>(!!(safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_search") || "").trim());

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      safeSetItemSession("taka_gantt_search", searchTerm);
      if (ganttTabFilterProjectId) {
        const targetProj = projects.find(
          p => (p.id && String(p.id) === String(ganttTabFilterProjectId)) ||
               (p.code && String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase() === String(ganttTabFilterProjectId).trim().toUpperCase())
        );
        const expectedCode = targetProj?.code || ganttTabFilterProjectId;
        if (!searchTerm.trim() || searchTerm.trim().toUpperCase() !== expectedCode.trim().toUpperCase()) {
          setGanttTabFilterProjectId('');
        }
      }
    }, 350);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchTerm, ganttTabFilterProjectId, setGanttTabFilterProjectId, projects]);

  const onStatusFilterChange = useCallback((status: string) => {
    setFilterProjectStatus(status);
    if (!searchTerm.trim()) {
      lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current = status;
      safeSetItemSession("taka_gantt_status_filter", status);
    }
  }, [searchTerm]);

  // Status & Location filter preservation during search
  useEffect(() => {
    const isSearching = !!deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!wasSearchingRef.current && isSearching) {
      if (filterProjectStatus !== 'all') {
        lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current = filterProjectStatus;
        safeSetItemSession("taka_gantt_status_filter", filterProjectStatus);
      }
      lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current = globalLocationFilter;
      setFilterProjectStatus('all');
      wasSearchingRef.current = true;
    } else if (wasSearchingRef.current && !isSearching) {
      const restored = lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current || safeGetItemSession("taka_gantt_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days';
      setFilterProjectStatus(restored);
      setGlobalLocationFilter(lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current || []);
      wasSearchingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [deferredSearchTerm]);

  const uniqueYears = useMemo(() => {
    const yearsSet = new Set<string>();
    projects.forEach(p => {
      if (p.year) yearsSet.add(String(p.year));
    });
    ['2026', '2027'].forEach(y => yearsSet.add(y));
    return Array.from(yearsSet).sort();
  }, [projects]);

  const uniqueStores = useMemo(() => getUserAllowedStores(currentUser), [currentUser]);
  const locationOptions = useLocationOptions();

  // Index tasks by project ID, project key, and code
  const tasksByProjectId = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Task[]>();
    tasks.forEach(t => {
      if (t.projectId) {
        const pId = String(t.projectId);
        if (!map.has(pId)) map.set(pId, []);
        map.get(pId)!.push(t);
      }
    });
    return map;
  }, [tasks]);

  const tasksByProject = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Task[]>();
    tasks.forEach(t => {
      const key = `${String(t.projectCode || '').trim().toUpperCase()}_${String(t.projectYear || '').trim()}_${String(t.projectStore || '').trim().toUpperCase()}`;
      if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, []);
      map.get(key)!.push(t);
    });
    return map;
  }, [tasks]);

  const tasksMapByCode = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Task[]>();
    tasks.forEach(t => {
      const code = String(t.projectCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!map.has(code)) map.set(code, []);
      map.get(code)!.push(t);
    });
    return map;
  }, [tasks]);

  const getGroupTasksForProject = useCallback(
    (p: Project): Task[] => {
      const mergedMap = new Map<string, Task>();

      const pStore = p.store ? String(p.store).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';

      // 1. By projectId
      if (p.id && tasksByProjectId.has(String(p.id))) {
        tasksByProjectId.get(String(p.id))!.forEach(t => {
          if (t && (t.id || t.name)) {
            const tStore = t.projectStore ? String(t.projectStore).trim().toUpperCase() : '';
            const tYear = t.projectYear !== undefined && t.projectYear !== null ? String(t.projectYear).trim() : '';
            if (pStore && tStore && pStore !== tStore) return;
            if (pYear && tYear && pYear !== tYear) return;
            mergedMap.set(String(t.id || t.name), t);
          }
        });
      }

      // 2. By project key (CODE_YEAR_STORE)
      const key = `${String(p.code || '').trim().toUpperCase()}_${String(p.year || '').trim()}_${String(p.store || '').trim().toUpperCase()}`;
      const foundByKey = tasksByProject.get(key);
      if (foundByKey && foundByKey.length > 0) {
        foundByKey.forEach(t => {
          if (t && (t.id || t.name)) mergedMap.set(String(t.id || t.name), t);
        });
      }

      // 3. By project code
      const codeKey = String(p.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (codeKey) {
        const foundByCode = tasksMapByCode.get(codeKey);
        if (foundByCode && foundByCode.length > 0) {
          foundByCode.forEach(t => {
            const matchesStore = !t.projectStore || !p.store || String(t.projectStore).trim().toUpperCase() === String(p.store).trim().toUpperCase();
            const matchesYear = !t.projectYear || !p.year || String(t.projectYear).trim() === String(p.year).trim();
            if (matchesStore && matchesYear && t && (t.id || t.name)) {
              mergedMap.set(String(t.id || t.name), t);
            }
          });
        }
      }

      let resultTasks = Array.from(mergedMap.values());

      // Fallback & Merge from taskSummaries if taskSummaries has more items
      if (p.taskSummaries && Array.isArray(p.taskSummaries) && p.taskSummaries.length > resultTasks.length) {
        const existingSet = new Set<string>();
        resultTasks.forEach(t => {
          if (t.id) existingSet.add(String(t.id).toLowerCase());
          if (t.taskCodePhu) existingSet.add(String(t.taskCodePhu).trim().toLowerCase());
          if (t.name) existingSet.add(String(t.name).trim().toLowerCase());
        });

        p.taskSummaries.forEach((ts, idx) => {
          const tsId = ts.id ? String(ts.id).toLowerCase() : '';
          const tsCode = ts.taskCodePhu ? String(ts.taskCodePhu).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
          const tsName = ts.name ? String(ts.name).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
          const hasMatch = (tsId && existingSet.has(tsId)) ||
                           (tsCode && existingSet.has(tsCode)) ||
                           (tsName && existingSet.has(tsName));
          if (!hasMatch) {
            resultTasks.push({
              id: ts.id || `ts_${p.id || p.code}_${p.store || 'HCM'}_${p.year || ''}_${idx}`,
              name: ts.name || `Task ${idx + 1}`,
              start: ts.start || p.computedTakStart || p.start || '',
              finish: ts.finish || p.computedTakCompl || p.handover || '',
              projectCode: p.code || '',
              projectStore: p.store || 'HCM',
              projectYear: p.year ? String(p.year) : '',
              party: ts.party || p.party || '',
              runningStatus: ts.runningStatus || 'On process',
              duration: ts.duration || '',
              parentTaskId: ts.parentTaskId,
              taskCodePhu: ts.taskCodePhu || (p.code ? `${p.code}-${idx + 1}` : undefined),
              orderIndex: ts.orderIndex !== undefined ? ts.orderIndex : idx,
              projectId: p.id,
              ...ts
            } as Task);
          }
        });
      }

      return resultTasks;
    },
    [tasksByProjectId, tasksByProject, tasksMapByCode]
  );

  // Pre-indexed Search Pool Cache to eliminate Garbage Collection pauses on mobile Safari
  const projectSearchIndex = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, { pPool: (string | number | undefined | null)[]; tPools: (string | number | undefined | null)[][] }>();
    projects.forEach(p => {
      const pTasks = getGroupTasksForProject(p);
      const pPool = [p.code, p.name, p.store, p.location, p.year, p.detail, p.party];
      const tPools = pTasks.map(t => [
        t.name,
        t.taskCodePhu,
        t.party,
        t.runningStatus,
        t.predecessor,
        ...(t.notes ? t.notes.map(n => n.content) : [])
      ]);
      map.set(p.id || p.code, { pPool, tPools });
    });
    return map;
  }, [projects, getGroupTasksForProject]);

  const filteredProjects = useMemo(() => {
    return projects.filter(p => {
      // Strict single-project isolation when navigated via Quick Navigation
      if (ganttTabFilterProjectId) {
        const isTarget = (p.id && String(p.id) === String(ganttTabFilterProjectId)) ||
                         (p.code && String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase() === String(ganttTabFilterProjectId).trim().toUpperCase());
        return isTarget;
      }

      const { computedTakStart, status } = getProjectDatesAndStatus(p, tasks);
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : null;
      const matchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || (pYear !== null && pYear === String(globalYearFilter).trim());
      const matchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || (Boolean(p.store) && String(p.store).toUpperCase().trim() === effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim());
      const matchesLocation = globalLocationFilter.length === 0 || (p.location && globalLocationFilter.includes(p.location));

      const matchesStatus = matchProjectStatusFilter(status, filterProjectStatus, computedTakStart);

      if (!matchesYear || !matchesStore || !matchesLocation || !matchesStatus) return false;

      if (!deferredSearchTerm.trim()) return true;

      const indexed = projectSearchIndex.get(p.id || p.code);
      if (indexed) {
        if (matchesAdvancedSearch(deferredSearchTerm, [...indexed.pPool, status])) return true;
        return indexed.tPools.some(tPool => matchesAdvancedSearch(deferredSearchTerm, tPool));
      }

      return matchesAdvancedSearch(deferredSearchTerm, [p.code, p.name, p.store, p.location, status]);
    });
  }, [
    projects,
    ganttTabFilterProjectId,
    globalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    globalLocationFilter,
    filterProjectStatus,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    projectSearchIndex
  ]);

  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(20);

  const sortedProjects = useMemo(() => {
    return [...filteredProjects].sort((a, b) => {
      const startA = a.computedTakStart || '9999-12-31';
      const startB = b.computedTakStart || '9999-12-31';
      if (startA !== startB) return startA.localeCompare(startB);
      return a.code.localeCompare(b.code);
    });
  }, [filteredProjects]);

  const paginatedProjects = useMemo(() => {
    return sortedProjects.slice(0, visibleCount);
  }, [sortedProjects, visibleCount]);

  const handleLoadMore = useCallback(() => {
    setVisibleCount(prev => Math.min(prev + 20, sortedProjects.length));
  }, [sortedProjects.length]);

  // Search filter operates on Project Summaries + loaded tasks first (Tri-Gate Lazy Loading).
  // Projects remain in summarized/collapsed state until user taps Expand (+), which triggers Delta Sync task fetching.
  const lastAutoExpandedSearchRef = useRef<string>('');
  useEffect(() => {
    const term = deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!term) {
      lastAutoExpandedSearchRef.current = '';
    }
  }, [deferredSearchTerm]);

  return {
    globalYearFilter,
    setGlobalYearFilter,
    globalStoreFilter,
    setGlobalStoreFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    filterProjectStatus,
    onStatusFilterChange,
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    uniqueYears,
    uniqueStores,
    locationOptions,
    tasksByProject,
    tasksMapByCode,
    getGroupTasksForProject,
    filteredProjects,
    sortedProjects,
    paginatedProjects,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    handleLoadMore
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/LayoutTabParts/useLayoutTabData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { useEnrichedData } from '../../hooks/useEnrichedData';
import { AppRole } from '../../types';
import { getUserAllowedStores, getEffectiveStoreFilter } from '../../lib/utils';
import { useDataStore } from '../PictureMapping/DataContext';
import { useLayoutTabSettings } from './useLayoutTabSettings';
import { 
  buildUnitSuggestions, 
  scoreAndFilterUnitSuggestions, 
  resolveTargetFloorForUnit, 
  resolveInitialTargetLocation,
  sortLocationsByFloorRank
} from './layoutTabSearchUtils';

export interface UseLayoutTabDataProps {
  propInitialStore?: string;
  propInitialLocation?: string;
  propInitialProjectCode?: string;
  onClearFocus?: () => void;
}

export function useLayoutTabData({
  propInitialStore,
  propInitialLocation,
  propInitialProjectCode,
  onClearFocus,
}: UseLayoutTabDataProps) {
  const layoutTabFilters = useAppStore(state => state.layoutTabFilters);
  const initialStore = propInitialStore !== undefined ? propInitialStore : (layoutTabFilters?.store || 'all');
  const initialLocation = propInitialLocation !== undefined ? propInitialLocation : (layoutTabFilters?.location || 'all');
  const initialProjectCode = propInitialProjectCode !== undefined ? propInitialProjectCode : (layoutTabFilters?.projectCode || '');

  const { enrichedProjects: projects, filteredTasks: tasks } = useEnrichedData();
  const currentUser = useAppStore(state => state.currentUser)!;
  const permissionMatrix = useAppStore(state => state.permissionMatrix);
  const locations = useAppStore(state => state.locations);
  const locationVisibility = useAppStore(state => state.locationVisibility);

  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setGlobalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalStoreFilter);
  const globalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalLocationFilter);
  const setGlobalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalLocationFilter);

  const [projectCodeFilter, setProjectCodeFilter] = useState(initialProjectCode);
  
  const isAdmin = currentUser.role === 'admin';
  const role = currentUser.role as AppRole;
  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setGlobalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalYearFilter);
  const isArchiveYear = globalYearFilter !== 'all' && Number(globalYearFilter) < new Date().getFullYear();
  const canWrite = (isAdmin || !!permissionMatrix[role]?.Write?.layout) && !isArchiveYear;

  const allowedStores = useMemo(() => {
    return getUserAllowedStores(currentUser);
  }, [currentUser]);
  const effectiveStore = getEffectiveStoreFilter(globalStoreFilter, currentUser);
  const activeStore = effectiveStore === 'all' ? (allowedStores[0] || '') : (allowedStores.includes(effectiveStore) ? effectiveStore : (allowedStores[0] || ''));

  const getAllStoreVersions = useDataStore((state) => state.getAllStoreVersions);
  const storeLocationData = useDataStore((state) => state.storeLocationData);
  const mapVersions = useDataStore((state) => state.mapVersions);

  // Stable memoized versions for activeStore to prevent reference churn across partition updates
  const prevStoreVersionsRef = useRef<any[]>([]);
  const prevStoreVersionsKeyRef = useRef<string>('');

  const allStoreVersions = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      const vers = getAllStoreVersions((activeStore === 'HN' ? 'HN' : 'HCM'));
      if (!Array.isArray(vers)) return [];
      const sig = `${activeStore}_${vers.map(v => `${v?.id || ''}_${v?.backgroundUrl || ''}`).join('|')}`;
      if (sig === prevStoreVersionsKeyRef.current) {
        return prevStoreVersionsRef.current;
      }
      prevStoreVersionsKeyRef.current = sig;
      prevStoreVersionsRef.current = vers;
      return vers;
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }, [getAllStoreVersions, activeStore, storeLocationData, mapVersions]);

  // Initial params sync effect - runs on mount or when props / layoutTabFilters change!
  const hasSyncedInitialRef = useRef(false);
  const lastInitialPropKeyRef = useRef('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const activeStoreParam = propInitialStore !== undefined ? propInitialStore : (layoutTabFilters?.store || '');
    const activeLocParam = propInitialLocation !== undefined ? propInitialLocation : (layoutTabFilters?.location || '');
    const activeCodeParam = propInitialProjectCode !== undefined ? propInitialProjectCode : (layoutTabFilters?.projectCode || '');
    const filterTs = (layoutTabFilters as any)?._ts || '';

    const propKey = `${activeStoreParam}_${activeLocParam}_${activeCodeParam}_${filterTs}`;
    if (hasSyncedInitialRef.current && propKey === lastInitialPropKeyRef.current) {
      return;
    }
    hasSyncedInitialRef.current = true;
    lastInitialPropKeyRef.current = propKey;

    if (activeStoreParam && activeStoreParam !== 'all') {
      setGlobalStoreFilter(activeStoreParam);
    }
    
    if (activeLocParam && activeLocParam !== 'all' && activeLocParam !== 'ALL') {
      setIsLiveMapMode(true);
      setGlobalLocationFilter([activeLocParam.toUpperCase().trim()]);
    }
    
    if (activeCodeParam) {
      setProjectCodeFilter(activeCodeParam);
      setUnitSearchInput(activeCodeParam);
      setTargetUnitSearch(activeCodeParam);
      setIsLiveMapMode(true);
    }

    const unitInfo = useDataStore.getState().unitInfo || [];
    const targetLoc = resolveInitialTargetLocation(
      activeLocParam, 
      activeCodeParam, 
      projects, 
      tasks, 
      allStoreVersions, 
      unitInfo
    );

    if (targetLoc && targetLoc.toLowerCase() !== 'all') {
      setGlobalLocationFilter([targetLoc]);
    }
  }, [propInitialStore, propInitialLocation, propInitialProjectCode, layoutTabFilters, projects, tasks, allStoreVersions, setGlobalStoreFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter]);

  // Available years: based on user permissions, projects, tasks, and store versions
  const prevAvailableYearsRef = useRef<string[]>([]);
  const prevAvailableYearsKeyRef = useRef<string>('');

  const availableYears = useMemo(() => {
    const storeProjects = projects.filter(p => {
      if (!activeStore || activeStore === 'all') return true;
      const pStore = String(p.store || 'HCM').toUpperCase().trim();
      return pStore === activeStore;
    });

    const projectYearsSet = new Set<string>();
    storeProjects.forEach(p => {
      const y = String(p.year || '').trim();
      if (y && y.toLowerCase() !== 'all' && /^\d{4}$/.test(y)) {
        projectYearsSet.add(y);
      }
    });

    const storeTasks = tasks.filter(t => {
      if (!activeStore || activeStore === 'all') return true;
      const tStore = String(t.projectStore || 'HCM').toUpperCase().trim();
      return tStore === activeStore;
    });
    const taskYearsSet = new Set<string>();
    storeTasks.forEach(t => {
      const y = String(t.projectYear || '').trim();
      if (y && y.toLowerCase() !== 'all' && /^\d{4}$/.test(y)) {
        taskYearsSet.add(y);
      }
    });

    const userPermittedYears = new Set([...projectYearsSet, ...taskYearsSet]);

    const versionYears = Array.from(
      new Set(
        allStoreVersions
          .filter(v => {
            if (!activeStore || activeStore === 'all') return true;
            const vStore = String(v.store || (activeStore === 'HN' ? 'HN' : 'HCM')).toUpperCase().trim();
            return vStore === activeStore;
          })
          .map(v => (v.year ? String(v.year).trim() : ''))
          .filter(y => y && y.toLowerCase() !== 'all' && /^\d{4}$/.test(y))
      )
    );

    const defaultStoreYears = activeStore === 'HN' ? ['2027'] : ['2026', '2027'];

    const finalYears = Array.from(new Set([...userPermittedYears, ...versionYears, ...defaultStoreYears]))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .sort((a, b) => Number(b) - Number(a));

    const key = finalYears.join('|');
    if (key === prevAvailableYearsKeyRef.current) {
      return prevAvailableYearsRef.current;
    }
    prevAvailableYearsKeyRef.current = key;
    prevAvailableYearsRef.current = finalYears;
    return finalYears;
  }, [allStoreVersions, projects, tasks, activeStore]);

  const effectiveYear = useMemo(() => {
    if (globalYearFilter && globalYearFilter !== 'all' && availableYears.includes(globalYearFilter)) {
      return globalYearFilter;
    }
    return availableYears[0] || '';
  }, [globalYearFilter, availableYears]);

  // Layout tab requires specific year selection (no 'all' option allowed)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (availableYears.length > 0 && (!globalYearFilter || globalYearFilter === 'all' || !availableYears.includes(globalYearFilter))) {
      setGlobalYearFilter(effectiveYear);
    }
  }, [globalYearFilter, availableYears, effectiveYear, setGlobalYearFilter]);

  // Layout tab only supports store-specific floor plans (HCM, HN), hide all-stores
  useEffect(() => {
    if (allowedStores.length > 0 && (!globalStoreFilter || globalStoreFilter === 'all' || !allowedStores.includes(globalStoreFilter))) {
      setGlobalStoreFilter(allowedStores[0]);
    }
  }, [globalStoreFilter, allowedStores, setGlobalStoreFilter]);

  // Danh sách location của Tab Layout (Cached to prevent array reference churn)
  const prevAvailableLocationsRef = useRef<string[]>([]);
  const prevAvailableLocationsKeyRef = useRef<string>('');

  const availableLocations = useMemo(() => {
    const storeParam = (activeStore === 'HN' ? 'HN' : 'HCM');
    const isUserAdmin = currentUser?.role === 'admin';
    const locPerms = currentUser?.locationPermissions || {};
    const hasAll = isUserAdmin || locPerms['all'] === true;

    const isFloorName = (name: string) => {
      if (!name) return false;
      const n = name.toUpperCase().trim();
      const nonFloors = ['BUDGET', 'COST', 'ALL', 'SUMMARY', 'GENERAL', 'SETTING'];
      if (nonFloors.includes(n)) return false;
      return true;
    };

    const isPermitted = (loc: string) => {
      if (!loc || !isFloorName(loc)) return false;
      if (locationVisibility[loc] === false) return false;
      if (hasAll) return true;
      const lLower = loc.toLowerCase().trim();
      const cleanLoc = lLower.replace(/^md-/, '');
      return !!(locPerms[cleanLoc] || locPerms[`md-${cleanLoc}`] || locPerms[lLower]);
    };

    const pmLocationsSet = new Set<string>();

    allStoreVersions.forEach(v => {
      const loc = String(v.location || '').toUpperCase().trim();
      if (loc && isPermitted(loc)) {
        pmLocationsSet.add(loc);
      }
    });

    if (storeLocationData) {
      Object.entries(storeLocationData).forEach(([key, partition]) => {
        if (key.startsWith(`${storeParam}_`)) {
          const loc = key.replace(`${storeParam}_`, '').toUpperCase().trim();
          if (loc && isPermitted(loc)) {
            if (
              (partition?.mapVersions && partition.mapVersions.length > 0) ||
              (partition?.mapUnits && partition.mapUnits.length > 0) ||
              partition?.activeMapVersionId
            ) {
              pmLocationsSet.add(loc);
            }
          }
        }
      });
    }

    let finalLocations: string[] = [];
    if (pmLocationsSet.size > 0) {
      finalLocations = Array.from(pmLocationsSet);
    } else {
      finalLocations = (locations || []).map(l => String(l).toUpperCase().trim()).filter(isPermitted);
    }

    const sorted = sortLocationsByFloorRank(finalLocations);
    const key = sorted.join('|');
    if (key === prevAvailableLocationsKeyRef.current) {
      return prevAvailableLocationsRef.current;
    }
    prevAvailableLocationsKeyRef.current = key;
    prevAvailableLocationsRef.current = sorted;
    return sorted;
  }, [allStoreVersions, storeLocationData, activeStore, currentUser, locationVisibility, locations]);

  // Ensure globalLocationFilter is never 'all' or empty in Layout tab
  useEffect(() => {
    if (availableLocations.length > 0) {
      const currentLoc = (globalLocationFilter[0] || '').toUpperCase().trim();
      if (!currentLoc || currentLoc === 'ALL') {
        const initialTargetLoc = resolveInitialTargetLocation(
          propInitialLocation || layoutTabFilters?.location || '', 
          propInitialProjectCode || layoutTabFilters?.projectCode || '', 
          projects, 
          tasks, 
          allStoreVersions, 
          useDataStore.getState().unitInfo || []
        );
        if (initialTargetLoc && availableLocations.includes(initialTargetLoc)) {
          setGlobalLocationFilter([initialTargetLoc]);
          return;
        }
      }
      const matched = availableLocations.find(l => l.toUpperCase().trim() === currentLoc);
      if (matched) {
        if (globalLocationFilter[0] !== matched) {
          setGlobalLocationFilter([matched]);
        }
      } else {
        const initialTargetLoc = resolveInitialTargetLocation(
          propInitialLocation || layoutTabFilters?.location || '', 
          propInitialProjectCode || layoutTabFilters?.projectCode || '', 
          projects, 
          tasks, 
          allStoreVersions, 
          useDataStore.getState().unitInfo || []
        );
        if (initialTargetLoc && availableLocations.includes(initialTargetLoc)) {
          setGlobalLocationFilter([initialTargetLoc]);
        } else {
          setGlobalLocationFilter([availableLocations[0]]);
        }
      }
    }
  }, [availableLocations, globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter, propInitialLocation, propInitialProjectCode, layoutTabFilters, projects, tasks, allStoreVersions]);

  const locFilterStr = useMemo(() => {
    const currentLoc = (globalLocationFilter[0] || '').toUpperCase().trim();
    if (currentLoc && currentLoc !== 'ALL') {
      const matched = availableLocations.find(l => l.toUpperCase().trim() === currentLoc);
      if (matched) return matched;
      return currentLoc;
    }
    return availableLocations[0] || '';
  }, [globalLocationFilter, availableLocations]);
  const [isLiveMapMode, setIsLiveMapMode] = useState(true);
  const [isFullscreenView, setIsFullscreenView] = useState(false);

  // Settings, URL persistence, and export triggers
  const {
    url,
    isEditing,
    setIsEditing,
    editUrl,
    setEditUrl,
    handleSave,
    isExporting,
    exportStatusMessage,
    handleTriggerReExport,
    getCleanUrl,
    fallbackSvg,
    cacheBuster,
    setCacheBuster,
  } = useLayoutTabSettings({ activeStore, locFilterStr });

  // Search Unit functionality next to Trigger Export
  const [unitSearchInput, setUnitSearchInput] = useState('');
  const [targetUnitSearch, setTargetUnitSearch] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showSuggestions, setShowSuggestions] = useState(false);
  const searchContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const getAllStoreUnits = useDataStore((state) => state.getAllStoreUnits);

  // Lazy autocomplete suggestions: ONLY compute when user opens or types into search box
  const unitSuggestions = useMemo(() => {
    if (!showSuggestions && !unitSearchInput.trim()) {
      return [];
    }
    const storeUnits: any[] = typeof getAllStoreUnits === 'function' ? (getAllStoreUnits as any)(activeStore) || [] : [];
    return buildUnitSuggestions(storeUnits, projects, activeStore, availableLocations, isAdmin);
  }, [getAllStoreUnits, activeStore, projects, availableLocations, isAdmin, showSuggestions, unitSearchInput]);

  const filteredUnitSuggestions = useMemo(() => {
    if (!unitSearchInput.trim()) return [];
    const currentLoc = globalLocationFilter[0] || '';
    return scoreAndFilterUnitSuggestions(unitSuggestions, unitSearchInput, currentLoc);
  }, [unitSuggestions, unitSearchInput, globalLocationFilter]);

  const handleSelectSearchUnit = useCallback((unitItem: { unit: string; location?: string; brand?: string; project?: string; code?: string }) => {
    setUnitSearchInput(unitItem.unit);
    setShowSuggestions(false);

    if (!isLiveMapMode) {
      setIsLiveMapMode(true);
    }

    const targetFloor = resolveTargetFloorForUnit(unitItem, projects);
    if (targetFloor && targetFloor.toLowerCase() !== 'all') {
      setGlobalLocationFilter([targetFloor]);
    }

    setTargetUnitSearch(unitItem.unit);
  }, [isLiveMapMode, projects, setGlobalLocationFilter]);

  const handleClearSearchAndFocus = useCallback(() => {
    setUnitSearchInput('');
    setTargetUnitSearch(null);
    setShowSuggestions(false);
    onClearFocus?.();
  }, [onClearFocus]);

  // Close suggestions on outside click
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOutsideClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (searchContainerRef.current && !searchContainerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setShowSuggestions(false);
      }
    };
