    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleOutsideClick);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleOutsideClick);
  }, []);

  // When activeStore, locFilterStr, or effectiveYear changes, reset search & focus states to default
  const prevFilterStateRef = useRef({ store: activeStore, loc: locFilterStr, yr: effectiveYear });
  useEffect(() => {
    const prev = prevFilterStateRef.current;
    if (prev.store !== activeStore || prev.loc !== locFilterStr || prev.yr !== effectiveYear) {
      prevFilterStateRef.current = { store: activeStore, loc: locFilterStr, yr: effectiveYear };
      setUnitSearchInput('');
      setTargetUnitSearch(null);
      setShowSuggestions(false);
      onClearFocus?.();
    }
  }, [activeStore, locFilterStr, effectiveYear, onClearFocus]);

  return {
    allowedStores, activeStore, setGlobalStoreFilter,
    globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter,
    availableLocations, locFilterStr,
    availableYears, effectiveYear, setGlobalYearFilter, globalYearFilter,
    canWrite, isAdmin, isEditing, setIsEditing, editUrl, setEditUrl, handleSave,
    isLiveMapMode, setIsLiveMapMode, url, getCleanUrl, fallbackSvg,
    isExporting, exportStatusMessage, handleTriggerReExport,
    unitSearchInput, setUnitSearchInput, targetUnitSearch, setTargetUnitSearch,
    showSuggestions, setShowSuggestions, searchContainerRef,
    filteredUnitSuggestions, handleSelectSearchUnit, handleClearSearchAndFocus,
    isFullscreenView, setIsFullscreenView,
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/CostParts/useCostData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Project, Cost, AppUser, AppRole } from '../../types';
import {
  getUserAllowedStores,
  getEffectiveStoreFilter,
  matchesAdvancedSearch,
  formatCurrencyValue,
} from '../../lib/utils';
import { useLocationOptions } from '../../hooks/useLocationOptions';
import { safeSetItemLocal, safeSetItemSession } from '../../lib/storage';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { useCostDateFilter } from './useCostDateFilter';
import { useCostColumns } from './useCostColumns';
import { useCostTotals } from './useCostTotals';
import { useCostSearchFilter } from './useCostSearchFilter';

interface UseCostDataParams {
  projects: Project[];
  costs: Cost[];
  currentUser: AppUser;
  initialSearchTerm?: string;
}

export function useCostData({
  projects,
  costs,
  currentUser,
  initialSearchTerm = '',
}: UseCostDataParams) {
  const permissionMatrix = useAppStore((state) => state.permissionMatrix);
  const currency = useAppStore((state) => state.currency);
  const jpyRate = useAppStore((state) => state.jpyRate);
  const usdRate = useAppStore((state) => state.usdRate);

  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setGlobalYearFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setGlobalYearFilter);
  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setGlobalStoreFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setGlobalStoreFilter);
  const effectiveStore = getEffectiveStoreFilter(globalStoreFilter, currentUser);
  const globalLocationFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalLocationFilter);
  const setGlobalLocationFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setGlobalLocationFilter);
  const setDetailTabFilterCode = useAppStore((state) => state.setDetailTabFilterCode);
  const setDetailTabStoreFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setDetailTabStoreFilter);
  const setActiveTab = useAppStore((state) => state.setActiveTab);
  const costTabSearchTerm = useAppStore((state) => state.costTabSearchTerm);

  const expandedCodes = useAppStore((state) => state.expandedProjectIds);
  const toggleProjectExpansion = useAppStore((state) => state.toggleProjectExpansion);
  const setExpandedCodes = useAppStore((state) => state.setExpandedProjectIds);

  const handleProjectLinkClick = (p: Project) => {
    if (!p) return;
    setDetailTabFilterCode(p.code);
    setDetailTabStoreFilter(p.store || 'all');
    if (p.year) {
      setGlobalYearFilter(String(p.year));
      safeSetItemSession('taka_detail_year', String(p.year));
    } else {
      setGlobalYearFilter('all');
      safeSetItemSession('taka_detail_year', 'all');
    }
    if (p.store) {
      setGlobalStoreFilter(p.store);
      safeSetItemLocal(`taka_store_filter_${currentUser.id}`, String(p.store));
    } else {
      setGlobalStoreFilter('all');
      safeSetItemLocal(`taka_store_filter_${currentUser.id}`, 'all');
    }
    setActiveTab('detail');
  };

  // O(1) Pre-indexed Map for Costs by Project Code
  const costsMapByCode = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Cost[]>();
    for (let i = 0; i < costs.length; i++) {
      const c = costs[i];
      if (c.isDeleted) continue;
      const cCode = String(c.projectCode || (c as any).code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!cCode) continue;
      let list = map.get(cCode);
      if (!list) {
        list = [];
        map.set(cCode, list);
      }
      list.push(c);
    }
    return map;
  }, [costs]);

  // Helper to get matching costs for a project in O(1) + O(k) candidate filter
  const getGroupCostsForProject = useCallback(
    (p: Project) => {
      const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const candidateCosts = costsMapByCode.get(pCode) || [];
      const pYr = String(p.year || '').trim();
      const pStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toLowerCase();

      return candidateCosts.filter((c) => {
        const costYear = String(c.projectYear || (c as any).year || '').trim();
        const matchesYear = !costYear || !pYr || costYear === pYr;
        if (!matchesYear) return false;

        const costStore = String(c.projectStore || (c as any).store || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const matchesStore = !costStore || !pStore || costStore === pStore;
        if (!matchesStore) return false;

        return true;
      });
    },
    [costsMapByCode]
  );

  // Sub-hook: Search term & filter synchronization
  const {
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    hasActForcFilter,
    setHasActForcFilter,
  } = useCostSearchFilter({
    projects,
    costs,
    currentUser,
    initialSearchTerm,
    costTabSearchTerm,
    globalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    getGroupCostsForProject,
  });

  // Sub-hook: Columns visibility & resizable widths
  const {
    visibleColumns,
    colWidths,
    handleResize,
    handleToggleColumn,
  } = useCostColumns({ userId: currentUser.id });

  // Sub-hook: Paid Date filter
  const {
    isPaidDateFilterOpen,
    setIsPaidDateFilterOpen,
    paidDateStart,
    paidDateEnd,
    paidStageFilter,
    setPaidStageFilter,
    tempDateStart,
    tempDateEnd,
    handleTempStartChange,
    handleTempEndChange,
    commitDateFilter,
    handleClearPaidDateFilter,
    getCostPaidMatches,
  } = useCostDateFilter();

  const isAdmin = currentUser.role === 'admin';
  const role = currentUser.role as AppRole;
  const isArchiveYear =
    globalYearFilter !== 'all' && Number(globalYearFilter) < new Date().getFullYear();
  const canWrite =
    (isAdmin || !!permissionMatrix[role]?.Write?.costDetail) && !isArchiveYear;

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const projYears = projects
      .map((p) => {
        if (p.year) return String(p.year).trim();
        if (p.start) return String(p.start).substring(0, 4);
        return null;
      })
      .filter(Boolean) as string[];

    const baseYears = ['2026', '2027'];
    const unique = Array.from(new Set([...projYears, ...baseYears]))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .sort();
    return unique.length > 0 ? unique : ['2026', '2027'];
  }, [projects]);

  const allowedStores = useMemo(() => getUserAllowedStores(currentUser), [currentUser]);
  const stores = allowedStores;
  const locationOptions = useLocationOptions();

  const toggleGroup = (id: string | number) => {
    toggleProjectExpansion(String(id));
  };

  const formatCurrency = (val: number | undefined) => {
    return formatCurrencyValue(val, currency, jpyRate, usdRate);
  };

  const filteredProjectsForDisplay = useMemo(() => {
    return projects.filter((p) => {
      const pYear = p.year
        ? String(p.year).trim()
        : p.start
        ? String(p.start).substring(0, 4)
        : null;
      if (
        globalYearFilter !== 'all' &&
        (pYear === null || pYear !== String(globalYearFilter).trim())
      )
        return false;
      if (
        effectiveStore !== 'all' &&
        (!p.store ||
          String(p.store).toUpperCase().trim() !== effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim())
      )
        return false;
      if (
        globalLocationFilter.length > 0 &&
        (!p.location || !globalLocationFilter.includes(p.location))
      )
        return false;

      const groupCosts = getGroupCostsForProject(p);

      if (paidDateStart || paidDateEnd) {
        const hasMatchingPaidCost = groupCosts.some(
          (c) => getCostPaidMatches(c, paidDateStart, paidDateEnd, paidStageFilter).matches
        );
        if (!hasMatchingPaidCost) return false;
      }

      const matchingGroupCosts =
        paidDateStart || paidDateEnd
          ? groupCosts.filter(
              (c) =>
                getCostPaidMatches(c, paidDateStart, paidDateEnd, paidStageFilter).matches
            )
          : groupCosts;

      if (deferredSearchTerm) {
        const isMatch = matchesAdvancedSearch(
          deferredSearchTerm,
          [
            p.store,
            p.location,
            p.year,
            p.code,
            p.linkCodes,
            p.name,
            p.detail,
            (p as any).description,
            p.note,
          ],
          [],
          (term) => {
            return matchingGroupCosts.some((c) => {
              const cBalance =
                (c.actual || 0) -
                (c.firstPaid || 0) -
                (c.secondPaid || 0) -
                (c.thirdPaid || 0);
              const costFields = [
                c.description,
                c.vendor,
                c.actual,
                c.firstPaid,
                c.secondPaid,
                c.thirdPaid,
                c.supportingFee,
                cBalance,
              ];
              return costFields.some((val) =>
                String(val || '').toLowerCase().includes(term)
              );
            });
          }
        );
        if (!isMatch) return false;
      }

      const totalAct =
        matchingGroupCosts.length > 0
          ? matchingGroupCosts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (c.actual || 0), 0)
          : p.financial?.totalActualPaid || p.actual || 0;
      if (
        hasActForcFilter === 'has_value' &&
        totalAct === 0 &&
        !paidDateStart &&
        !paidDateEnd
      )
        return false;

      return true;
    });
  }, [
    projects,
    costs,
    globalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    globalLocationFilter,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    hasActForcFilter,
    paidDateStart,
    paidDateEnd,
    paidStageFilter,
    getGroupCostsForProject,
    getCostPaidMatches,
  ]);

  // Sub-hook: Aggregated Financial Totals
  const {
    filteredCostsForTotals,
    totalActAll,
    totalFirstPaidAll,
    totalSecondPaidAll,
    totalThirdPaidAll,
    totalBalanceAll,
    totalSupAll,
    totalRemainingAll,
  } = useCostTotals({
    filteredProjectsForDisplay,
    projects,
    costs,
    paidDateStart,
    paidDateEnd,
    paidStageFilter,
    getGroupCostsForProject,
    getCostPaidMatches,
  });

  const sortedProjects = projects;
  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(30);

  const handleCostScroll = useCallback(
    (e: React.UIEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
      const target = e.currentTarget;
      const st = target.scrollTop;
      const sh = target.scrollHeight;
      const ch = target.clientHeight;

      if (sh - (st + ch) < 350) {
        if (visibleCount < filteredProjectsForDisplay.length) {
          setVisibleCount((prev) => Math.min(prev + 30, filteredProjectsForDisplay.length));
        }
      }
    },
    [visibleCount, filteredProjectsForDisplay.length]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (paidDateStart || paidDateEnd) {
      setExpandedCodes(
        filteredProjectsForDisplay.map((p) => `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`)
      );
    }
  }, [paidDateStart, paidDateEnd, paidStageFilter, filteredProjectsForDisplay, setExpandedCodes]);

  const toggleAll = () => {
    if (expandedCodes.length === 0) {
      setExpandedCodes(
        filteredProjectsForDisplay.map((p) => `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`)
      );
    } else {
      setExpandedCodes([]);
    }
  };

  return {
    currency,
    jpyRate,
    usdRate,
    canWrite,
    isAdmin,
    globalYearFilter,
    setGlobalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    setGlobalStoreFilter,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    hasActForcFilter,
    setHasActForcFilter,
    years,
    stores,
    locationOptions,
    visibleColumns,
    colWidths,
    handleResize,
    handleToggleColumn,
    expandedCodes,
    setExpandedCodes,
    toggleGroup,
    toggleAll,
    toggleProjectExpansion,
    handleProjectLinkClick,
    formatCurrency,
    filteredProjectsForDisplay,
    filteredCostsForTotals,
    totalActAll,
    totalFirstPaidAll,
    totalSecondPaidAll,
    totalThirdPaidAll,
    totalBalanceAll,
    totalSupAll,
    totalRemainingAll,
    sortedProjects,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    handleCostScroll,
    getGroupCostsForProject,
    getCostPaidMatches,
    isPaidDateFilterOpen,
    setIsPaidDateFilterOpen,
    paidDateStart,
    paidDateEnd,
    paidStageFilter,
    setPaidStageFilter,
    tempDateStart,
    tempDateEnd,
    handleTempStartChange,
    handleTempEndChange,
    commitDateFilter,
    handleClearPaidDateFilter,
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/DetailTabParts/useDetailData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useCallback, useDeferredValue, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Project, Task, DelegationGroup, AppUser } from '../../types';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { 
  getTaskStatus, 
  parseDateTime, 
  formatDisplayDateMain, 
  formatDisplayDateTime, 
  resolveActualStatus, 
  matchesAdvancedSearch 
} from '../../lib/utils';
import { computeTaskCode, computeAllTaskCodes } from '../../lib/ganttUtils';
import { safeGetItemSession, safeSetItemSession } from '../../lib/storage';
import { SortConfig } from './detailTypes';
import { useDetailProjectInfo } from './useDetailProjectInfo';
import { useDetailFlatRows } from './useDetailFlatRows';
import { useDetailFocusTracking } from './useDetailFocusTracking';

interface UseDetailDataParams {
  projects: Project[];
  tasks: Task[];
  delegationGroups: DelegationGroup[];
  currentUser: AppUser;
  effectiveStore: string;
  globalYearFilter: string;
  setGlobalYearFilter: (year: string) => void;
  globalStoreFilter: string;
  setGlobalStoreFilter: (store: string) => void;
  globalLocationFilter: string[];
  setGlobalLocationFilter: (locs: string[]) => void;
  initialFilterCode?: string;
  initialFocusTaskId?: string;
  returnScrollId?: string;
  onClearFocus?: () => void;
  onBackToGantt?: () => void;
  sortConfig: SortConfig | null;
  expandedCodes: string[];
  setExpandedCodes: (codes: string[]) => void;
  setVisibleActiveProjectIds: (ids: string[]) => void;
  canWrite: boolean;
}

export function useDetailData({
  projects,
  tasks,
  delegationGroups,
  currentUser: _currentUser,
  effectiveStore,
  globalYearFilter,
  setGlobalYearFilter,
  globalStoreFilter,
  setGlobalStoreFilter,
  globalLocationFilter,
  setGlobalLocationFilter,
  initialFilterCode,
  initialFocusTaskId,
  returnScrollId: _returnScrollId,
  onClearFocus,
  onBackToGantt,
  sortConfig,
  expandedCodes,
  setExpandedCodes,
  setVisibleActiveProjectIds,
}: UseDetailDataParams) {
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const deferredSearchTerm = useDeferredValue(searchTerm);
  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(30);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState(() => safeGetItemSession("taka_detail_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days');
  const lastSelectedStatusFilterRef = useRef<string>(safeGetItemSession("taka_detail_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days');
  const lastSelectedLocationFilterRef = useRef<string[]>(globalLocationFilter);
  const wasSearchingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const isSearching = !!deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!wasSearchingRef.current && isSearching) {
      if (statusFilter !== 'all') {
        lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current = statusFilter;
        safeSetItemSession("taka_detail_status_filter", statusFilter);
      }
      lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current = globalLocationFilter;
      setStatusFilter('all');
      wasSearchingRef.current = true;
    } else if (wasSearchingRef.current && !isSearching) {
      const restored = lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current || safeGetItemSession("taka_detail_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days';
      setStatusFilter(restored);
      setGlobalLocationFilter(lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current || []);
      wasSearchingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [deferredSearchTerm]);

  const [selectedTaskIds, setSelectedTaskIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const lastCheckedTaskIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  // Focus & Viewport Tracking Hook
  const {
    activeProjectInView,
    handleClearFocus,
    handleBackToGantt,
  } = useDetailFocusTracking({
    projects,
    tasks,
    initialFilterCode,
    initialFocusTaskId,
    onClearFocus,
    onBackToGantt,
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    globalYearFilter,
    setGlobalYearFilter,
    globalStoreFilter,
    setGlobalStoreFilter,
    statusFilter,
    setStatusFilter,
    expandedCodes,
    setExpandedCodes,
  });

  // Project Info Map & Helpers Hook
  const {
    tasksByProjectKey,
    projectInfoMap,
    getProjectInfo,
  } = useDetailProjectInfo({ projects, tasks });

  // Auto-jump to matched project location(s) when searching (Debounced)
  useEffect(() => {
    const term = deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!term) return;

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const matchedProjects = projects.filter(p => {
        const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year) : null;
        if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && pYear !== globalYearFilter) return false;
        if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && p.store !== effectiveStore) return false;

        const groupTasks = tasksByProjectKey.get(`${p.code || ''}-${p.year || ''}-${p.store || ''}`) || [];
        const isMatch = matchesAdvancedSearch(
          term,
          [p.store, p.location, p.year, p.code, p.linkCodes, p.name, p.detail, (p as any).description, p.note, p.party],
          [],
          (tLower) => {
            const summariesMatch = p.taskSummaries?.some(ts =>
              String(ts.name || "").toLowerCase().includes(tLower)
            ) || false;
            const taskMatches = groupTasks.some((t, idx) => {
              const taskFields = [
                t.taskCodePhu,
                `${p.code}#${(t.orderIndex !== undefined ? t.orderIndex : idx) + 1}`,
                t.name,
                t.duration,
                t.start,
                t.finish,
                t.party,
                formatDisplayDateMain(t.start),
                formatDisplayDateTime(t.start),
                formatDisplayDateMain(t.finish),
                formatDisplayDateTime(t.finish)
              ];
              const fieldMatch = taskFields.some(val => String(val || "").toLowerCase().includes(tLower));
              const notesMatch = t.notes?.some(n => String(n.content || '').toLowerCase().includes(tLower)) || false;
              return fieldMatch || notesMatch;
            });
            return summariesMatch || taskMatches;
          }
        );
        return isMatch;
      });

      const matchedLocations = Array.from(
        new Set(matchedProjects.map(p => p.location).filter((loc): loc is string => !!loc && loc.trim() !== ''))
      );
      if (matchedLocations.length > 0) {
        const currentLocs = globalLocationFilter || [];
        const isSame = currentLocs.length === matchedLocations.length && currentLocs.every(l => matchedLocations.includes(l));
        if (!isSame) {
          setGlobalLocationFilter(matchedLocations);
        }
      }
    }, 350);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [deferredSearchTerm, projects, tasksByProjectKey, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter]);

  const delegationGroupByParentKey = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, DelegationGroup>();
    delegationGroups.forEach(g => {
      if (g.parentTaskId) {
        const keyWithStore = `${g.parentTaskId}-${g.projectStore || ''}`;
        map.set(keyWithStore, g);
        if (!map.has(g.parentTaskId)) {
          map.set(g.parentTaskId, g);
        }
      }
    });
    return map;
  }, [delegationGroups]);

  useEffect(() => { setVisibleCount(20); }, [searchTerm, globalStoreFilter, globalYearFilter, globalLocationFilter, statusFilter]);

  // === Auto-clear stale filters when user changes Year / Store in Task Detail ===
  const prevYearStoreRef = useRef(`${globalYearFilter}_${effectiveStore}`);

  useEffect(() => {
    const currentKey = `${globalYearFilter}_${effectiveStore}`;
    if (prevYearStoreRef.current !== currentKey) {
      prevYearStoreRef.current = currentKey;

      // 1. Khi đổi Year hoặc Store: nếu đang có detailTabFilterCode / detailTabFocusTaskId của dự án thuộc Store/Year cũ thì xóa đi
      const storeState = useAppStore.getState();
      if (storeState.detailTabFilterCode || storeState.detailTabFocusTaskId) {
        storeState.setDetailTabFilterCode('');
        storeState.setDetailTabFocusTaskId('');
        if (searchTerm && searchTerm === storeState.detailTabFilterCode) {
          setSearchTerm('');
        }
      }

      // 2. Kiểm tra xem globalLocationFilter hiện tại có hợp lệ với Store/Year mới không
      const projectsInNewScope = projects.filter((p) => {
        if (p.isDeleted) return false;
        const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year) : null;
        if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && pYear !== String(globalYearFilter)) return false;
        if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && String(p.store || '').toUpperCase() !== String(effectiveStore).toUpperCase()) return false;
        return true;
      });

      if (projectsInNewScope.length > 0) {
        // Nếu location đang chọn không có dự án nào ở Store/Year mới -> reset location filter
        if (globalLocationFilter.length > 0) {
          const hasMatchingLoc = projectsInNewScope.some(
            (p) => p.location && globalLocationFilter.includes(p.location)
          );
          if (!hasMatchingLoc) {
            setGlobalLocationFilter([]);
          }
        }

        // Nếu statusFilter đang khác 'all' mà làm ẩn toàn bộ dự án của Store/Year mới -> tự động mở về 'all'
        if (statusFilter !== 'all') {
          const today = new Date();
          today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
          const todayTime = today.getTime();
          const hasMatchingStatus = projectsInNewScope.some((p) => {
            const info = getProjectInfo(p);
            if (statusFilter === 'On process + Upcoming 3 days') {
              if (info.status === 'On process') return true;
              if (info.status === 'Upcoming') {
                const dStart = parseDateTime(info.start);
                if (dStart) {
                  dStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
                  const diffDays = Math.ceil((dStart.getTime() - todayTime) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                  return diffDays <= 3 && diffDays >= 0;
                }
              }
              return false;
            }
            return info.status === statusFilter;
          });
          if (!hasMatchingStatus) {
            setStatusFilter('all');
            safeSetItemSession('taka_detail_status_filter', 'all');
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }, [
    globalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    projects,
    globalLocationFilter,
    statusFilter,
    searchTerm,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    setStatusFilter,
    setSearchTerm,
    getProjectInfo,
  ]);

  // Vị trí đang chọn nhưng không có dự án nào trong store/năm hiện tại thì bỏ qua,
  // tránh bảng trống dù nhãn hiển thị "All Locations" (ví dụ vị trí STORE của HN còn sót khi chuyển sang HCM)
  const effectiveLocationFilter = useMemo(() => {
    if (globalLocationFilter.length === 0) return globalLocationFilter;
    const available = new Set<string>();
    projects.forEach((p) => {
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';
      if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && pYear !== String(globalYearFilter).trim()) return;
      if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && (!p.store || String(p.store).toUpperCase().trim() !== effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim())) return;
      if (p.location) available.add(p.location);
    });
    return globalLocationFilter.filter((loc) => available.has(loc));
  }, [projects, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter]);

  const filteredProjects = useMemo(() => {
    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    const todayTime = today.getTime();

    return projects.filter(p => {
      const pKey = `${p.code || ''}-${p.year || ''}-${p.store || ''}-${p.id || p.no}`;
      const info = projectInfoMap.get(pKey) || getProjectInfo(p);
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : null;
      if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && (pYear === null || pYear !== String(globalYearFilter).trim())) return false;
      if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && (!p.store || String(p.store).toUpperCase().trim() !== effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim())) return false;
      if (effectiveLocationFilter.length > 0 && (!p.location || !effectiveLocationFilter.includes(p.location))) return false;

      let matchesStatus = true;
      if (statusFilter === 'On process + Upcoming 3 days') {
        if (info.status === 'On process') {
          matchesStatus = true;
        } else if (info.status === 'Upcoming') {
          const dStart = parseDateTime(info.start);
          if (dStart) {
            dStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
            const diffTime = dStart.getTime() - todayTime;
            const diffDays = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
            matchesStatus = diffDays <= 3 && diffDays >= 0;
          } else {
            matchesStatus = false;
          }
        } else {
          matchesStatus = false;
        }
      } else {
        matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'all' || info.status === statusFilter;
      }
      if (!matchesStatus) return false;

      const groupTasks = info.sortedTasks || tasksByProjectKey.get(`${p.code || ''}-${p.year || ''}-${p.store || ''}`) || [];
      if (deferredSearchTerm) {
        const isMatch = matchesAdvancedSearch(
          deferredSearchTerm,
          [p.store, p.location, p.year, p.code, p.linkCodes, p.name, p.detail, (p as any).description, p.note, p.party],
          [],
          (term) => {
            const summariesMatch = p.taskSummaries?.some(ts =>
              String(ts.name || "").toLowerCase().includes(term)
            ) || false;
            const taskMatches = groupTasks.some((t, idx) => {
              const taskFields = [
                t.taskCodePhu,
                `${p.code}#${(t.orderIndex !== undefined ? t.orderIndex : idx) + 1}`,
                t.name,
                t.duration,
                t.start,
                t.finish,
                t.party,
                formatDisplayDateMain(t.start),
                formatDisplayDateTime(t.start),
                formatDisplayDateMain(t.finish),
                formatDisplayDateTime(t.finish)
              ];
              const fieldMatch = taskFields.some(val => String(val || "").toLowerCase().includes(term));
              const notesMatch = t.notes?.some(n => String(n.content || '').toLowerCase().includes(term)) || false;
              return fieldMatch || notesMatch;
            });
            return summariesMatch || taskMatches;
          }
        );
        if (!isMatch) return false;
      }

      return true;
    });
  }, [projects, projectInfoMap, getProjectInfo, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, effectiveLocationFilter, statusFilter, deferredSearchTerm, tasksByProjectKey]);

  // Chẩn đoán: bảng trống dù store có dự án
  useEffect(() => {
    if (filteredProjects.length === 0 && projects.length > 0) {
      console.warn('[Detail] 0 dự án sau khi lọc', {
        totalProjects: projects.length,
        year: globalYearFilter,
        store: effectiveStore,
        locationSelected: globalLocationFilter,
        locationEffective: effectiveLocationFilter,
        status: statusFilter,
        search: deferredSearchTerm,
      });
    }
  }, [filteredProjects.length, projects.length, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, effectiveLocationFilter, statusFilter, deferredSearchTerm]);

  // Ensure visibleCount is large enough to render target project row
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!initialFilterCode) return;
    const filterClean = String(initialFilterCode).trim().toUpperCase();
    const projIdx = filteredProjects.findIndex(p => {
      const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const pId = String(p.id || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      return pId === filterClean || pCode === filterClean || (pCode && filterClean.startsWith(pCode));
    });
    if (projIdx >= 0 && projIdx >= visibleCount) {
      setVisibleCount(projIdx + 25);
    }
  }, [initialFilterCode, filteredProjects, visibleCount]);

  const prevVisibleIdsRef = useRef<string>("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const targetProjects = filteredProjects.length > 0
      ? filteredProjects.slice(0, visibleCount)
      : projects.filter(p => {
          const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year) : null;
          if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && pYear !== globalYearFilter) return false;
          if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && p.store !== effectiveStore) return false;
          if (globalLocationFilter.length > 0 && (!p.location || !globalLocationFilter.includes(p.location))) return false;
          return true;
        }).slice(0, visibleCount);

    const visibleIds = targetProjects.map(p => String(p.id || p.no));
    const idsKey = visibleIds.join(',');
    if (prevVisibleIdsRef.current !== idsKey) {
      prevVisibleIdsRef.current = idsKey;
      setVisibleActiveProjectIds(visibleIds);
    }
  }, [filteredProjects, projects, visibleCount, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, setVisibleActiveProjectIds]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      useAppStore.getState().setVisibleActiveProjectIds([]);
    };
  }, []);

  const allVisibleTaskIds = useMemo(() => {
    return filteredProjects.slice(0, visibleCount).flatMap(p => {
      const pKey = `${p.code || ''}-${p.year || ''}-${p.store || ''}-${p.id || p.no}`;
      const pInfo = projectInfoMap.get(pKey) || getProjectInfo(p);
      return (pInfo.sortedTasks || []).map(t => t.id);
    });
  }, [filteredProjects, visibleCount, projectInfoMap, getProjectInfo]);

  const lowerSearchTerm = searchTerm.toLowerCase().trim();

  // Task code & status maps
  const taskCodeMap = useMemo(() => {
    return computeAllTaskCodes(projects, tasks);
  }, [projects, tasks]);

  const taskActualStatusMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, { label: string; color: string }>();
    for (let idx = 0; idx < tasks.length; idx++) {
      const t = tasks[idx];
      const taskStatus = getTaskStatus(t.start, t.finish);
      const delegationGroup = delegationGroupByParentKey.get(t.id);
      const actStatus = resolveActualStatus(taskStatus, delegationGroup?.subTasks || [], t);
      map.set(t.id, actStatus);
    }
    return map;
  }, [tasks, delegationGroupByParentKey]);

  // Pre-indexed O(1) map of subtask counts per parent to avoid 250,000 array filters in DetailTaskRow
  const childTasksCountMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, number>();
    for (let idx = 0; idx < tasks.length; idx++) {
      const child = tasks[idx];
      if (child.parentTaskId) {
        map.set(child.parentTaskId, (map.get(child.parentTaskId) || 0) + 1);
      }
    }
    return map;
  }, [tasks]);

  // Virtual Windowing Flat Rows
  const flatTableRows = useDetailFlatRows({
    filteredProjects,
    visibleCount,
    projectInfoMap,
    getProjectInfo,
    sortConfig,
    expandedCodes,
    delegationGroups,
    lowerSearchTerm,
    taskCodeMap,
  });

  // Selection handlers
  const handleSelectAllTasksInProject = useCallback((groupTasks: Task[], select: boolean) => {
    setSelectedTaskIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      groupTasks.forEach(t => {
        if (select) next.add(t.id);
        else next.delete(t.id);
      });
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const handleToggleSelectTask = useCallback((taskId: string, shiftKey: boolean) => {
    const strTaskId = String(taskId);
    setSelectedTaskIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(Array.from(prev).map(String));
      const isChecked = !next.has(strTaskId);
      if (isChecked) {
        if (shiftKey && lastCheckedTaskIdRef.current && lastCheckedTaskIdRef.current !== strTaskId) {
          const visibleIds = allVisibleTaskIds.map(String);
          const lastIdx = visibleIds.indexOf(lastCheckedTaskIdRef.current);
          const currentIdx = visibleIds.indexOf(strTaskId);
          if (lastIdx !== -1 && currentIdx !== -1) {
            const minIdx = Math.min(lastIdx, currentIdx);
            const maxIdx = Math.max(lastIdx, currentIdx);
            for (let idx = minIdx; idx <= maxIdx; idx++) {
              next.add(visibleIds[idx]);
            }
          } else {
            next.add(strTaskId);
          }
        } else {
          next.add(strTaskId);
        }
        lastCheckedTaskIdRef.current = strTaskId;
      } else {
        next.delete(strTaskId);
        lastCheckedTaskIdRef.current = null;
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, [allVisibleTaskIds]);

  return {
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    statusFilter,
    setStatusFilter,
    lastSelectedStatusFilterRef,
    selectedTaskIds,
    setSelectedTaskIds,
    lastCheckedTaskIdRef,
    activeProjectInView,
    delegationGroupByParentKey,
    projectInfoMap,
    getProjectInfo,
    filteredProjects,
    allVisibleTaskIds,
    lowerSearchTerm,
    taskCodeMap,
    taskActualStatusMap,
    childTasksCountMap,
    flatTableRows,
    handleSelectAllTasksInProject,
    handleToggleSelectTask,
    handleClearFocus,
    handleBackToGantt,
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/SummaryParts/useSummaryData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { useEnrichedData } from '../../hooks/useEnrichedData';
import { getStatus, parseDateTime, getUserAllowedStores, getEffectiveStoreFilter } from '../../lib/utils';
import { useLocationOptions } from '../../hooks/useLocationOptions';
import { StatusCountItem } from './summaryTypes';

export function useSummaryData() {
  const { enrichedProjects: projects } = useEnrichedData();
  const currentUser = useAppStore((state) => state.currentUser)!;

  const [activeSubTab, setActiveSubTab] = useState<'project_summary' | 'exec_dashboard'>('project_summary');

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const yearsSet = new Set<string>();
    projects.forEach((p) => {
      const year = p.year ? String(p.year) : null;
      if (year) yearsSet.add(year);
    });
    yearsSet.add('2026');
    yearsSet.add('2027');
    const arr = Array.from(yearsSet).sort((a, b) => b.localeCompare(a));
    return arr.length > 0 ? arr : ['2027', '2026'];
  }, [projects, currentUser?.role]);

  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setGlobalYearFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setGlobalYearFilter);
  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setGlobalStoreFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setGlobalStoreFilter);
  const globalLocationFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.globalLocationFilter);
  const setGlobalLocationFilter = useAppStore((state) => state.setGlobalLocationFilter);

  const [startMonth, setStartMonth] = useState<number>(1);
  const [endMonth, setEndMonth] = useState<number>(12);

  const allowedStores = useMemo(() => getUserAllowedStores(currentUser), [currentUser]);
  const effectiveStore = getEffectiveStoreFilter(globalStoreFilter, currentUser);
  const stores = allowedStores;

  const locationOptions = useLocationOptions();

  const filteredProjects = useMemo(() => {
    return projects.filter((p) => {
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year) : null;
      const matchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || pYear === globalYearFilter;
      const matchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || p.store === effectiveStore;
      const matchesLocation = globalLocationFilter.length === 0 || (p.location && globalLocationFilter.includes(p.location));
      return matchesYear && matchesStore && matchesLocation;
    });
  }, [projects, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter]);

  const projectsInPeriod = useMemo(() => {
    return filteredProjects.filter((p) => {
      const dateStr = p.computedTakStart || p.start;
      if (!dateStr) return false;
      const date = parseDateTime(dateStr);
      if (!date) return false;
      const month = date.getMonth() + 1; // 1-indexed

      const minM = Math.min(startMonth, endMonth);
      const maxM = Math.max(startMonth, endMonth);

      return month >= minM && month <= maxM;
    });
  }, [filteredProjects, startMonth, endMonth]);

  const yearChartData: StatusCountItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    filteredProjects.forEach((p) => {
      const status = getStatus(p.computedTakStart, p.computedTakCompl, p.computedOpening || p.opening, p.code);
      counts[status] = (counts[status] || 0) + 1;
    });
    return Object.entries(counts).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }));
  }, [filteredProjects]);

  const monthChartData: StatusCountItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    projectsInPeriod.forEach((p) => {
      const status = getStatus(p.computedTakStart, p.computedTakCompl, p.computedOpening || p.opening, p.code);
      counts[status] = (counts[status] || 0) + 1;
    });
    return Object.entries(counts).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }));
  }, [projectsInPeriod]);

  const totalBudget = useMemo(() => filteredProjects.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.budget || 0), 0), [filteredProjects]);
  const totalActual = useMemo(() => filteredProjects.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.actual || 0), 0), [filteredProjects]);
  const periodBudget = useMemo(() => projectsInPeriod.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.budget || 0), 0), [projectsInPeriod]);
  const periodActual = useMemo(() => projectsInPeriod.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.actual || 0), 0), [projectsInPeriod]);

  return {
    activeSubTab,
    setActiveSubTab,
    years,
    stores,
    locationOptions,
    globalYearFilter,
    setGlobalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    setGlobalStoreFilter,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    startMonth,
    setStartMonth,
    endMonth,
    setEndMonth,
    filteredProjects,
    projectsInPeriod,
    yearChartData,
    monthChartData,
    totalBudget,
    totalActual,
    periodBudget,
    periodActual,
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/BudgetParts/useBudgetFilterSort.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { matchesAdvancedSearch } from '../../lib/utils';
import { BudgetTreeNode, BudgetSortConfig } from './budgetTypes';

interface UseBudgetFilterSortParams {
  treeData: BudgetTreeNode[];
  globalYearFilter: string;
  effectiveStore: string;
  globalLocationFilter: string[];
  setGlobalLocationFilter: (locs: string[]) => void;
  moneyFilter: string;
  searchTerm: string;
  deferredSearchTerm: string;
}

export function useBudgetFilterSort({
  treeData,
  globalYearFilter,
  effectiveStore,
  globalLocationFilter,
  setGlobalLocationFilter,
  moneyFilter,
  searchTerm,
  deferredSearchTerm
}: UseBudgetFilterSortParams) {
  const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState<BudgetSortConfig | null>(null);
  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(30);

  const isChildMatchSearch = (child: any, term: string): boolean => {
    if (!term) return false;
    const cSearchFields = [
      child.code,
      child.name,
      child.store,
      child.location,
      child.type,
      child.note,
      child.year,
      child.budget,
      child.totalActual,
      child.remaining
    ];
    return matchesAdvancedSearch(term, cSearchFields);
  };

  const isParentMatchSearch = (node: any, term: string): boolean => {
    if (!term) return false;
    const searchFields = [
      node.code,
      node.name,
      node.store,
      node.location,
      node.type,
      node.note,
      node.year,
      node.totalBudget,
      node.totalActual,
      node.remaining
    ];
    return matchesAdvancedSearch(term, searchFields);
  };

  // Auto-jump to matched project location(s) when searching (Debounced)
  useEffect(() => {
    const term = searchTerm.trim();
    if (!term) return;
    if (!treeData || treeData.length === 0) return;

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const matchedLocationsList: string[] = [];

      treeData.forEach((node: any) => {
        const pYear = node.year ? String(node.year) : null;
        if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && pYear !== globalYearFilter) return;
        if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && node.store !== effectiveStore) return;

        const parentMatch = isParentMatchSearch(node, term);
        if (parentMatch && node.location) {
          matchedLocationsList.push(node.location);
        }

        if (node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
          node.children.forEach((child: any) => {
            const cYear = child.year ? String(child.year) : null;
            if (globalYearFilter !== 'all' && cYear && cYear !== globalYearFilter) return;
            if (effectiveStore !== 'all' && child.store && child.store !== effectiveStore) return;

            if (isChildMatchSearch(child, term) || parentMatch) {
              if (child.location) matchedLocationsList.push(child.location);
              else if (node.location) matchedLocationsList.push(node.location);
            }
          });
        }
      });

      const matchedLocations = Array.from(
        new Set(matchedLocationsList.filter((loc): loc is string => !!loc && loc.trim() !== ''))
      );

      if (matchedLocations.length > 0) {
        const currentLocs = globalLocationFilter || [];
        const isSame = currentLocs.length === matchedLocations.length && currentLocs.every(l => matchedLocations.includes(l));
        if (!isSame) {
          setGlobalLocationFilter(matchedLocations);
        }
      }
    }, 350);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchTerm, treeData, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter]);

  const filterNode = (node: any): boolean => {
    const pYear = node.year ? String(node.year).trim() : null;
    const matchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || (pYear !== null && pYear === String(globalYearFilter).trim());
    const matchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || (Boolean(node.store) && String(node.store).toUpperCase().trim() === effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim());
    
    // Strict Scope Guard: A parent project that does not belong to the selected Year or Store scope MUST NOT be displayed
    if (!matchesYear || !matchesStore) {
      return false;
    }

    const matchesLocation = globalLocationFilter.length === 0 || (node.location && globalLocationFilter.includes(node.location));
    
    const hasBudget = (node.totalBudget || 0) !== 0;
    const hasActForc = (node.totalActual || 0) !== 0;
    let matchesMoney = true;
    if (moneyFilter === 'has_money') matchesMoney = hasBudget || hasActForc;
    else if (moneyFilter === 'has_budget') matchesMoney = hasBudget;
    else if (moneyFilter === 'has_act_forc') matchesMoney = hasActForc;
    
    const baseParentMatch = matchesLocation && matchesMoney;
    const parentMatchesSearch = !deferredSearchTerm || isParentMatchSearch(node, deferredSearchTerm);

    if (node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
      const validChildren = node.children.filter((child: any) => {
        const cYear = child.year ? String(child.year).trim() : pYear;
        const cMatchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || !cYear || cYear === String(globalYearFilter).trim();
        const cStore = child.store ? String(child.store).toUpperCase().trim() : (node.store ? String(node.store).toUpperCase().trim() : '');
        const cMatchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || !cStore || cStore === effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim();
        const cMatchesLocation = globalLocationFilter.length === 0 || (!child.location) || globalLocationFilter.includes(child.location);
        
        const cHasBudget = (child.budget || 0) !== 0;
        const cHasActForc = (child.totalActual || 0) !== 0;
        let cMatchesMoney = true;
        if (moneyFilter === 'has_money') cMatchesMoney = cHasBudget || cHasActForc || matchesMoney;
        else if (moneyFilter === 'has_budget') cMatchesMoney = cHasBudget || hasBudget;
        else if (moneyFilter === 'has_act_forc') cMatchesMoney = cHasActForc || hasActForc;

        return cMatchesYear && cMatchesStore && cMatchesLocation && cMatchesMoney;
      });

      const hasMultiChildren = node.children.length >= 2;

      if (!deferredSearchTerm.trim()) {
        node.filteredChildren = validChildren.map((c: any) => ({ ...c, isSearchMatch: false }));
        return baseParentMatch || validChildren.length > 0;
      }

      const matchingChildIds = new Set<string>();
      validChildren.forEach((child: any) => {
        if (isChildMatchSearch(child, deferredSearchTerm)) {
          matchingChildIds.add(child.id);
        }
      });

      const anyChildMatchesSearch = matchingChildIds.size > 0;

      if (hasMultiChildren) {
        if (anyChildMatchesSearch || (baseParentMatch && parentMatchesSearch)) {
          const shouldFilterChildren = !parentMatchesSearch && anyChildMatchesSearch;
          node.filteredChildren = validChildren
            .filter((child: any) => shouldFilterChildren ? matchingChildIds.has(child.id) : true)
            .map((child: any) => ({
              ...child,
              isSearchMatch: matchingChildIds.has(child.id)
            }));
          return true;
        }
        node.filteredChildren = [];
        return false;
      } else {
        if (anyChildMatchesSearch) {
          node.filteredChildren = validChildren
            .filter((c: any) => matchingChildIds.has(c.id))
            .map((c: any) => ({ ...c, isSearchMatch: true }));
          return true;
        } else if (baseParentMatch && parentMatchesSearch) {
          node.filteredChildren = validChildren.map((c: any) => ({ ...c, isSearchMatch: false }));
          return true;
        }
        node.filteredChildren = [];
        return false;
      }
    } else {
      node.filteredChildren = [];
      return baseParentMatch && parentMatchesSearch;
    }
  };

  const filteredRoots = useMemo(() => {
    let roots = treeData.filter(filterNode);
    if (sortConfig) {
      const sortFn = (a: any, b: any) => {
        let valA = 0, valB = 0;
        if (sortConfig.key === 'totalBudget') { valA = a.totalBudget || 0; valB = b.totalBudget || 0; }
        else if (sortConfig.key === 'actual') { valA = a.totalActual || 0; valB = b.totalActual || 0; }
        else if (sortConfig.key === 'variance') { valA = a.remaining || 0; valB = b.remaining || 0; }

        if (valA < valB) return sortConfig.direction === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
        if (valA > valB) return sortConfig.direction === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
        return 0;
      };
      roots = [...roots].sort(sortFn);
    }
    return roots;
  }, [treeData, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, moneyFilter, deferredSearchTerm, sortConfig]);

  const handleBudgetScroll = useCallback((e: React.UIEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const target = e.currentTarget;
    const st = target.scrollTop;
    const sh = target.scrollHeight;
    const ch = target.clientHeight;
    if (sh - (st + ch) < 350) {
      if (visibleCount < filteredRoots.length) {
        setVisibleCount(prev => Math.min(prev + 30, filteredRoots.length));
      }
    }
  }, [visibleCount, filteredRoots.length]);

  const handleSort = (key: string) => {
    setSortConfig(prev => {
      if (prev && prev.key === key) {
        if (prev.direction === 'asc') return { key, direction: 'desc' };
        return null;
      }
      return { key, direction: 'asc' };
    });
  };

  const totalGlobalBudget = filteredRoots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalBudget || 0), 0);
  const totalGlobalActual = filteredRoots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalActual || 0), 0);
  const totalGlobalSupportingFee = filteredRoots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalSupportingFee || 0), 0);
  const totalGlobalRemaining = totalGlobalBudget - totalGlobalActual + totalGlobalSupportingFee;
  const totalVarianceColor = totalGlobalRemaining < 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400';

  return {
    sortConfig,
    setSortConfig,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    filteredRoots,
    handleBudgetScroll,
    handleSort,
    totalGlobalBudget,
    totalGlobalActual,
    totalGlobalSupportingFee,
    totalGlobalRemaining,
    totalVarianceColor
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/BudgetParts/BudgetLinkModal.tsx
// ================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { motion, useDragControls } from 'motion/react';
import { Layers, X, Filter, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { Project } from '../../types';
import { ProjectFinancials } from './budgetTypes';
import { useLanguage } from '../../lib/LanguageContext';
import { getZIndexClass } from '../../lib/zIndexManager';
import { BudgetLinkParentColumn } from './BudgetLinkParentColumn';
import { BudgetLinkChildColumn } from './BudgetLinkChildColumn';

interface BudgetLinkModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  selectedParentProject: Project | null;
  setSelectedParentProject: (p: Project | null) => void;
  parentSearchTerm: string;
  setParentSearchTerm: (term: string) => void;
  childSearchTerm: string;
  setChildSearchTerm: (term: string) => void;
  candidateParentProjects: Project[];
  candidateChildProjects: Project[];
  linkedChildrenCount: number;
  getProjectFinancials: (p: Project) => ProjectFinancials;
  handleLinkParent: (childProject: Project, parentCode: string | null) => Promise<void>;
  formatCurrency: (val: number | undefined) => string;
  projects?: Project[];
  initialYear?: string;
  initialStore?: string;
  initialLocation?: string;
}

export function BudgetLinkModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  selectedParentProject,
  setSelectedParentProject,
  parentSearchTerm,
  setParentSearchTerm,
  childSearchTerm,
  setChildSearchTerm,
  candidateParentProjects,
  candidateChildProjects,
  linkedChildrenCount,
  getProjectFinancials,
  handleLinkParent,
  formatCurrency,
  projects,
  initialYear,
  initialStore,
  initialLocation
}: BudgetLinkModalProps) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const dragControls = useDragControls();

  const [modalYearFilter, setModalYearFilter] = useState<string>(initialYear || 'all');
  const [modalStoreFilter, setModalStoreFilter] = useState<string>(initialStore || 'all');
  const [modalLocationFilter, setModalLocationFilter] = useState<string>(initialLocation || 'all');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      if (selectedParentProject) {
        if (selectedParentProject.year) setModalYearFilter(String(selectedParentProject.year));
        if (selectedParentProject.store) setModalStoreFilter(String(selectedParentProject.store).toUpperCase());
      } else {
        setModalYearFilter(initialYear || 'all');
        setModalStoreFilter(initialStore || 'all');
        setModalLocationFilter(initialLocation || 'all');
      }
    }
  }, [isOpen, selectedParentProject, initialYear, initialStore, initialLocation]);

  const allProjects = projects && projects.length > 0 ? projects : candidateParentProjects;

  const availableYears = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    allProjects.forEach(p => {
      if (p.year) set.add(String(p.year).trim());
      else if (p.start) set.add(String(p.start).substring(0, 4));
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => Number(b) - Number(a));
  }, [allProjects]);

  const availableLocations = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    allProjects.forEach(p => {
      if (p.location && p.location.trim()) {
        set.add(p.location.trim());
      }
    });
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [allProjects]);

  const filteredParentProjects = useMemo(() => {
    const term = parentSearchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
    return allProjects.filter(p => {
      if (modalYearFilter !== 'all') {
        const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';
        if (pYear && pYear !== modalYearFilter) return false;
      }
      if (modalStoreFilter !== 'all') {
        const pStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        if (pStore && pStore !== modalStoreFilter.toUpperCase()) return false;
      }
      if (modalLocationFilter !== 'all') {
        if (p.location !== modalLocationFilter) return false;
      }
      if (!term) return true;
      const searchPool = [
        p.code,
        p.name,
        p.year,
        p.store,
        p.location,
        p.type,
        p.note
      ].map(v => String(v || '').toLowerCase());
      return searchPool.some(val => val.includes(term));
    }).slice(0, 100);
  }, [allProjects, parentSearchTerm, modalYearFilter, modalStoreFilter, modalLocationFilter]);

  const filteredChildProjects = useMemo(() => {
    const parentCode = selectedParentProject ? String(selectedParentProject.code || '').trim().toUpperCase() : '';
    const parentId = selectedParentProject?.id || (selectedParentProject ? `${selectedParentProject.code}-${selectedParentProject.year}-${selectedParentProject.store}-${selectedParentProject.no}` : '');
    const term = childSearchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    const filtered = allProjects.filter(p => {
      const pId = p.id || `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`;
      const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();

      if (selectedParentProject && (pId === parentId || pCode === parentCode)) return false;
      if (selectedParentProject?.parentId && String(selectedParentProject.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() === pCode) return false;

      const targetYear = selectedParentProject?.year 
        ? String(selectedParentProject.year).trim() 
        : (modalYearFilter !== 'all' ? modalYearFilter : '');
      if (targetYear) {
        const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';
        if (pYear && pYear !== targetYear) return false;
      }

      const targetStore = selectedParentProject?.store 
        ? String(selectedParentProject.store).trim().toUpperCase() 
        : (modalStoreFilter !== 'all' ? modalStoreFilter.toUpperCase() : '');
      if (targetStore) {
        const pStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        if (pStore && pStore !== targetStore) return false;
      }

      if (modalLocationFilter !== 'all') {
        if (p.location !== modalLocationFilter) return false;
      }

      if (!term) return true;
      const searchPool = [
        p.code,
        p.name,
        p.year,
        p.store,
        p.location,
        p.type,
        p.note
      ].map(v => String(v || '').toLowerCase());
      return searchPool.some(val => val.includes(term));
    });

    if (parentCode) {
      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const aIsChild = a.parentId && String(a.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() === parentCode ? 1 : 0;
        const bIsChild = b.parentId && String(b.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() === parentCode ? 1 : 0;
        return bIsChild - aIsChild;
      });
    }

    return filtered.slice(0, 100);
  }, [allProjects, selectedParentProject, childSearchTerm, modalYearFilter, modalStoreFilter, modalLocationFilter]);

  if (!isOpen && !selectedParentProject) return null;

  const parentStats = selectedParentProject ? getProjectFinancials(selectedParentProject) : null;

  return (
    <div className={`fixed inset-0 ${getZIndexClass('MODAL_MODULAR')} flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs p-2 sm:p-4 overflow-hidden pointer-events-auto`}>
      <motion.div
        drag
        dragControls={dragControls}
        dragListener={false}
        dragMomentum={false}
        className="bg-white dark:bg-[#1e1e20] rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-[96vw] max-w-6xl xl:max-w-7xl h-[90vh] max-h-[860px] flex flex-col border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#333336] overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150"
      >
        {/* Modal Drag Header */}
        <div 
          onPointerDown={(e) => dragControls.start(e)}
          className="p-3.5 sm:p-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-[#333336] flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50/90 dark:bg-[#18181a] cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing select-none shrink-0"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/40 text-blue-600 dark:text-[#8ab4f8]">
              <Layers className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base md:text-lg text-slate-900 dark:text-[#e3e3e3]">
              {t('Project Modular (Link Budget)')}
            </h3>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button 
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200/50 dark:hover:bg-[#28282a] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Scope Filter Toolbar: Year, Store, Location */}
        <div className="px-3.5 sm:px-4 py-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#18181a] border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-[#333336] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2.5 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 mr-1">
              <Filter className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600 dark:text-[#8ab4f8]" />
              <span>{t('Filter Scope')}:</span>
            </div>

            {/* Year Selector */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{t('Year')}:</span>
              <select
                value={modalYearFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setModalYearFilter(e.target.value)}
                className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-[#222225] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#444446] rounded-lg px-2 py-1 font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"
              >
                <option value="all">{t('All Years')}</option>
                {availableYears.map(y => (
                  <option key={y} value={y}>{y}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Store Selector */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{t('Store')}:</span>
              <select
                value={modalStoreFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setModalStoreFilter(e.target.value)}
                className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-[#222225] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#444446] rounded-lg px-2 py-1 font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"
              >
                <option value="all">{t('All Stores')}</option>
                <option value="HCM">HCM</option>
                <option value="HN">HN</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Location Selector */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{t('Location')}:</span>
              <select
                value={modalLocationFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setModalLocationFilter(e.target.value)}
                className="text-xs bg-white dark:bg-[#222225] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#444446] rounded-lg px-2 py-1 font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer shadow-2xs max-w-[140px] truncate"
              >
                <option value="all">{t('All Locations')}</option>
                {availableLocations.map(loc => (
                  <option key={loc} value={loc}>{loc}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Reset Filter */}
          {(modalYearFilter !== 'all' || modalStoreFilter !== 'all' || modalLocationFilter !== 'all') && (
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setModalYearFilter('all');
                setModalStoreFilter('all');
                setModalLocationFilter('all');
              }}
              className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 hover:text-blue-600 dark:hover:text-[#8ab4f8] flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>{t('Reset Filter')}</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Modal Main Content: 2 Columns Side-by-Side */}
        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 p-3 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto lg:overflow-hidden">
          {/* Column 1: Parent Projects */}
          <BudgetLinkParentColumn
            filteredParentProjects={filteredParentProjects}
            selectedParentProject={selectedParentProject}
            setSelectedParentProject={setSelectedParentProject}
            parentSearchTerm={parentSearchTerm}
            setParentSearchTerm={setParentSearchTerm}
            parentStats={parentStats}
            linkedChildrenCount={linkedChildrenCount}
            formatCurrency={formatCurrency}
          />

          {/* Column 2: Child Projects */}
          <BudgetLinkChildColumn
            selectedParentProject={selectedParentProject}
            childSearchTerm={childSearchTerm}
            setChildSearchTerm={setChildSearchTerm}
            filteredChildProjects={filteredChildProjects}
            handleLinkParent={handleLinkParent}
            getProjectFinancials={getProjectFinancials}
            formatCurrency={formatCurrency}
          />
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/BudgetParts/useBudgetData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useDeferredValue, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Project, Cost, AppUser, AppRole } from '../../types';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { 
  getUserAllowedStores, 
  getEffectiveStoreFilter, 
  formatCurrencyValue 
} from '../../lib/utils';
import { useLocationOptions } from '../../hooks/useLocationOptions';
import { safeSetItemLocal, safeSetItemSession, safeGetItemSession, safeGetDeviceItemLocal, safeSetDeviceItemLocal, getDeviceScreenType } from '../../lib/storage';
import { AVAILABLE_COLUMNS, ProjectFinancials } from './budgetTypes';
import { useBudgetTreeData } from './useBudgetTreeData';
import { useBudgetFilterSort } from './useBudgetFilterSort';
import { useBudgetCsvOps } from './useBudgetCsvOps';

interface UseBudgetDataParams {
  projects: Project[];
  costs: Cost[];
  currentUser: AppUser;
  initialSearchTerm?: string;
}

export function useBudgetData({
  projects,
  costs,
  currentUser,
  initialSearchTerm = ''
}: UseBudgetDataParams) {
  const permissionMatrix = useAppStore(state => state.permissionMatrix);
  const currency = useAppStore(state => state.currency);
  const jpyRate = useAppStore(state => state.jpyRate);
  const usdRate = useAppStore(state => state.usdRate);

  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setGlobalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalYearFilter);
  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setGlobalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalStoreFilter);
  const effectiveStore = getEffectiveStoreFilter(globalStoreFilter, currentUser);
  const globalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalLocationFilter);
  const setGlobalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalLocationFilter);
  const setDetailTabFilterCode = useAppStore(state => state.setDetailTabFilterCode);
  const setDetailTabStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setDetailTabStoreFilter);
  const setActiveTab = useAppStore(state => state.setActiveTab);
  const budgetTabSearchTerm = useAppStore(state => state.budgetTabSearchTerm);

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState(initialSearchTerm || budgetTabSearchTerm || '');
  const deferredSearchTerm = useDeferredValue(searchTerm);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialSearchTerm) {
      setSearchTerm(initialSearchTerm);
    } else if (budgetTabSearchTerm) {
      setSearchTerm(budgetTabSearchTerm);
    }
  }, [initialSearchTerm, budgetTabSearchTerm]);

  const [collapsedInSearch, setCollapsedInSearch] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [moneyFilter, setMoneyFilter] = useState(() => {
    if ((initialSearchTerm || '').trim()) return 'all';
    return safeGetItemSession("taka_budget_money_filter") || 'has_money';
  });
  const lastSelectedMoneyFilterRef = useRef<string>(
    safeGetItemSession("taka_budget_money_filter") || 'has_money'
  );
  const lastSelectedLocationFilterRef = useRef<string[]>(globalLocationFilter);
  const wasSearchingRef = useRef<boolean>(!!(initialSearchTerm || '').trim());

  useEffect(() => {
    const isSearching = !!deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!wasSearchingRef.current && isSearching) {
      if (moneyFilter !== 'all') {
        lastSelectedMoneyFilterRef.current = moneyFilter;
        safeSetItemSession("taka_budget_money_filter", moneyFilter);
      }
      lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current = globalLocationFilter;
      setMoneyFilter('all');
      wasSearchingRef.current = true;
    } else if (wasSearchingRef.current && !isSearching) {
      const restored = lastSelectedMoneyFilterRef.current || safeGetItemSession("taka_budget_money_filter") || 'has_money';
      setMoneyFilter(restored);
      setGlobalLocationFilter(lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current || []);
      wasSearchingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [deferredSearchTerm]);

  const [visibleColumns, setVisibleColumns] = useState<string[]>(AVAILABLE_COLUMNS.map(c => c.id));
  const [colWidths, setColWidths] = useState<Record<string, number>>({
    no: 80,
    year: 80,
    store: 80,
    location: 130,
    code: 165,
    projectName: 220,
    totalBudget: 140,
    actual: 140,
    supportingFee: 140,
    variance: 140,
    type: 130,
    note: 200,
    actions: 90
  });

  const [expandedParents, setExpandedParents] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [selectedParentProject, setSelectedParentProject] = useState<Project | null>(null);
  const [isLinkModalOpen, setIsLinkModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [childSearchTerm, setChildSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [parentSearchTerm, setParentSearchTerm] = useState('');

  // 1. Build hierarchy tree data
  const treeData = useBudgetTreeData({ projects, costs });

  // 2. Filter, search and sort tree roots
  const {
    sortConfig,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    filteredRoots,
    handleBudgetScroll,
    handleSort,
    totalGlobalBudget,
    totalGlobalActual,
    totalGlobalSupportingFee,
    totalGlobalRemaining,
    totalVarianceColor
  } = useBudgetFilterSort({
    treeData,
    globalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    moneyFilter,
    searchTerm,
    deferredSearchTerm
  });

  // 3. Export / Import CSV
  const { handleExportBudget, handleImportBudget } = useBudgetCsvOps({ projects, filteredRoots });

  const getProjectFinancials = (p: Project): ProjectFinancials => {
    const fin = p.financial || { totalApproved: p.budget || 0, totalActualPaid: p.actual || 0, totalForecastPaid: 0 };
    const budget = fin.totalApproved || p.budget || 0;
    const actual = fin.totalActualPaid;
    const hasForc = fin.totalForecastPaid > 0;
    const supportingFee = p.supportingFee || 0;
    const remaining = budget - (actual + fin.totalForecastPaid) + supportingFee;
    return { budget, actual, supportingFee, hasForc, remaining };
  };

  // Candidates for Parent Project Selection
  const candidateParentProjects = useMemo(() => {
    if (!projects || projects.length === 0) return [];
    const term = parentSearchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
    return projects.filter(p => {
      if (!term) return true;
      const searchPool = [
        p.code,
        p.name,
        p.year,
        p.store,
        p.location,
        p.type,
        p.note
      ].map(v => String(v || '').toLowerCase());
      
      return searchPool.some(val => val.includes(term));
    }).slice(0, 100);
  }, [projects, parentSearchTerm]);

  // Candidates for Child Project Selection
  const candidateChildProjects = useMemo(() => {
    if (!projects || projects.length === 0) return [];
    const parentCode = selectedParentProject ? String(selectedParentProject.code || '').trim().toUpperCase() : '';
    const parentId = selectedParentProject?.id || (selectedParentProject ? `${selectedParentProject.code}-${selectedParentProject.year}-${selectedParentProject.store}-${selectedParentProject.no}` : '');
    const term = childSearchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();

    const filtered = projects.filter(p => {
      const pId = p.id || `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`;
      const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();

      if (selectedParentProject && (pId === parentId || pCode === parentCode)) return false;
      if (selectedParentProject?.parentId && String(selectedParentProject.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() === pCode) return false;

      // Restrict candidate children to same fiscal year and store as selected parent
      if (selectedParentProject) {
        const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';
        const parentYear = selectedParentProject.year ? String(selectedParentProject.year).trim() : '';
        if (pYear && parentYear && pYear !== parentYear) return false;

        const pStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        const parentStore = String(selectedParentProject.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        if (pStore && parentStore && pStore !== parentStore) return false;
      }

      if (!term) return true;
      const searchPool = [
        p.code,
        p.name,
        p.year,
        p.store,
        p.location,
        p.type,
        p.note
      ].map(v => String(v || '').toLowerCase());
      
      return searchPool.some(val => val.includes(term));
    });

    if (parentCode) {
      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const aIsChild = a.parentId && String(a.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() === parentCode ? 1 : 0;
        const bIsChild = b.parentId && String(b.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() === parentCode ? 1 : 0;
        return bIsChild - aIsChild;
      });
    }

    return filtered.slice(0, 100);
  }, [projects, selectedParentProject, childSearchTerm]);

  const linkedChildrenCount = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedParentProject) return 0;
    const parentCode = String(selectedParentProject.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const parentYear = selectedParentProject.year ? String(selectedParentProject.year).trim() : '';
    const parentStore = String(selectedParentProject.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();

    return projects.filter(p => {
      if (!p.parentId || String(p.parentId).trim().toUpperCase() !== parentCode) return false;
      const cYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';
      const cStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (parentYear && cYear && cYear !== parentYear) return false;
      if (parentStore && cStore && cStore !== parentStore) return false;
      return true;
    }).length;
  }, [projects, selectedParentProject]);

  // Handle Escape key
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (isLinkModalOpen || selectedParentProject) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          setIsLinkModalOpen(false);
          setSelectedParentProject(null);
          return;
        }
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        const hasOpenModal =
          state.isProjectModalOpen ||
          state.isTaskModalOpen ||
          state.isUserModalOpen ||
          state.isLoginModalOpen ||
          state.isCalendarSyncModalOpen;
        if (hasOpenModal) return;
        if (searchTerm) {
          e.preventDefault();
          const activeElement = document.activeElement;
          if (activeElement && typeof (activeElement as HTMLElement).blur === "function") {
            (activeElement as HTMLElement).blur();
          }
          setSearchTerm("");
        }
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isLinkModalOpen, selectedParentProject, searchTerm]);

  const prevScreenTypeRef = useRef(getDeviceScreenType());

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadDeviceConfig = () => {
      const saved = safeGetDeviceItemLocal('taka_budget_col_widths', currentUser.id);
      if (saved) {
        try { 
          const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
          if (parsed.code && parsed.code < 165) {
            parsed.code = 165;
          }
          if (parsed.no && parsed.no < 60) {
            parsed.no = 80;
          }
          setColWidths(prev => ({ ...prev, ...parsed })); 
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      const savedCols = safeGetDeviceItemLocal('taka_budget_visible_cols', currentUser.id);
      if (savedCols) {
        try { 
          const parsed = JSON.parse(savedCols);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
            let cleaned = parsed.filter((id: string) => id !== 'budget' && id !== 'linkCost');
            if (!cleaned.includes('no')) {
              cleaned = ['no', ...cleaned];
            }
            setVisibleColumns(cleaned);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      } else {
        const isMobile = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.innerWidth < 768;
        if (isMobile) {
          setVisibleColumns(['no', 'code', 'projectName', 'totalBudget', 'actual', 'variance']);
        }
      }
    };

    loadDeviceConfig();

    const handleResize = () => {
      const currentScreenType = getDeviceScreenType();
      if (currentScreenType !== prevScreenTypeRef.current) {
        prevScreenTypeRef.current = currentScreenType;
        loadDeviceConfig();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);
  }, [currentUser.id]);

  const handleResize = (col: string, newWidth: number) => {
    setColWidths(prev => {
      const next = { ...prev, [col]: newWidth };
      safeSetDeviceItemLocal('taka_budget_col_widths', JSON.stringify(next), currentUser.id);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleToggleColumn = (colId: string) => {
    setVisibleColumns(prev => {
      const next = prev.includes(colId) ? prev.filter(id => id !== colId) : [...prev, colId];
      safeSetDeviceItemLocal('taka_budget_visible_cols', JSON.stringify(next), currentUser.id);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const toggleExpand = (id: string) => {
    if (searchTerm.trim()) {
      setCollapsedInSearch(prev => {
        const next = new Set(prev);
        if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
        else next.add(id);
        return next;
      });
    } else {
      setExpandedParents(prev => {
        const next = new Set(prev);
        if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
        else next.add(id);
        return next;
      });
    }
  };

  const toggleAllParents = () => {
    if (expandedParents.size === 0) {
      const allParentIds = new Set<string>();
      const collectParents = (nodes: any[]) => {
        nodes.forEach(n => {
          if (n.filteredChildren && n.filteredChildren.length > 0) {
            allParentIds.add(n.id || n.code);
            collectParents(n.filteredChildren);
          }
        });
      };
      collectParents(filteredRoots);
      setExpandedParents(allParentIds);
    } else {
      setExpandedParents(new Set());
    }
  };

  const isAdmin = currentUser.role === 'admin';
  const role = currentUser.role as AppRole;
  const isArchiveYear = globalYearFilter !== 'all' && Number(globalYearFilter) < new Date().getFullYear();
  const canWrite = (isAdmin || !!currentUser?.canWrite || !!permissionMatrix[role]?.Write?.budget) && !isArchiveYear;

  const formatCurrency = (val: number | undefined) => {
    return formatCurrencyValue(val, currency, jpyRate, usdRate);
  };

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const projYears = projects.map(p => {
      if (p.year) return String(p.year);
      if (p.start) return String(p.start).substring(0, 4);
      return null;
    }).filter(Boolean) as string[];
    const baseYears = ['2026', '2027'];
    const unique = Array.from(new Set([...projYears, ...baseYears])).filter(Boolean).sort();
    return unique.length > 0 ? unique : ['2026', '2027'];
  }, [projects, currentUser?.role]);

  const allowedStores = useMemo(() => getUserAllowedStores(currentUser), [currentUser]);
  const stores = allowedStores;
  const locationOptions = useLocationOptions();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (locationOptions.length > 0 && globalLocationFilter.length > 0) {
      const valid = globalLocationFilter.filter(loc => locationOptions.includes(loc));
      if (valid.length !== globalLocationFilter.length) {
        setGlobalLocationFilter(valid);
      }
    }
  }, [locationOptions, globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter]);
