  const handleOpenLinkModal = (projectToSelect?: Project) => {
    if (projectToSelect) {
      setSelectedParentProject(projectToSelect);
      setParentSearchTerm(String(projectToSelect.code || '').trim());
    } else {
      if (!selectedParentProject && filteredRoots.length > 0) {
        setSelectedParentProject(filteredRoots[0].project);
        setParentSearchTerm(String(filteredRoots[0].project.code || '').trim());
      } else if (selectedParentProject) {
        setParentSearchTerm(String(selectedParentProject.code || '').trim());
      }
    }
    setIsLinkModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleProjectLinkClick = (p: Project) => {
    if (!p) return;
    setDetailTabFilterCode(p.code);
    setDetailTabStoreFilter(p.store || "all");
    if (p.year) {
      setGlobalYearFilter(String(p.year));
      safeSetItemSession("taka_detail_year", String(p.year));
    } else {
      setGlobalYearFilter("all");
      safeSetItemSession("taka_detail_year", "all");
    }
    if (p.store) {
      setGlobalStoreFilter(p.store);
      safeSetItemLocal(`taka_store_filter_${currentUser.id}`, String(p.store));
    } else {
      setGlobalStoreFilter("all");
      safeSetItemLocal(`taka_store_filter_${currentUser.id}`, "all");
    }
    setActiveTab("detail");
  };

  return {
    currency,
    jpyRate,
    usdRate,
    canWrite,
    globalYearFilter,
    setGlobalYearFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    setGlobalStoreFilter,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    moneyFilter,
    setMoneyFilter,
    lastSelectedMoneyFilterRef,
    collapsedInSearch,
    expandedParents,
    sortConfig,
    visibleColumns,
    colWidths,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    years,
    stores,
    locationOptions,
    filteredRoots,
    totalGlobalBudget,
    totalGlobalActual,
    totalGlobalSupportingFee,
    totalGlobalRemaining,
    totalVarianceColor,
    candidateParentProjects,
    candidateChildProjects,
    linkedChildrenCount,
    selectedParentProject,
    setSelectedParentProject,
    isLinkModalOpen,
    setIsLinkModalOpen,
    parentSearchTerm,
    setParentSearchTerm,
    childSearchTerm,
    setChildSearchTerm,
    getProjectFinancials,
    handleResize,
    handleToggleColumn,
    toggleExpand,
    toggleAllParents,
    handleSort,
    handleBudgetScroll,
    handleOpenLinkModal,
    handleExportBudget,
    handleImportBudget,
    handleProjectLinkClick,
    formatCurrency
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/BudgetParts/useBudgetTreeData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import { Project, Cost } from '../../types';
import { calculateParentBudgetRollup } from '../../lib/financialService';
import { BudgetTreeNode } from './budgetTypes';

interface UseBudgetTreeDataParams {
  projects: Project[];
  costs: Cost[];
}

export function buildBudgetTreeData(projects: Project[], costs: Cost[]): BudgetTreeNode[] {
  const projectMap = new Map<string, any>();
  const codeToProjects = new Map<string, any[]>();

    projects.forEach(p => {
      const key = `${String(p.code).trim().toLowerCase()}_${String(p.year || '')}_${String(p.store || '')}`;
      const projectCosts = costs.filter(c => {
        const costCode = String(c.projectCode || (c as any).code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (costCode !== pCode) return false;

        const costYear = String(c.projectYear || (c as any).year || '').trim();
        const pYear = String(p.year || '').trim();
        const matchesYear = !costYear || !pYear || costYear === pYear;
        if (!matchesYear) return false;

        const costStore = String(c.projectStore || (c as any).store || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const pStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const matchesStore = !costStore || !pStore || costStore === pStore;
        if (!matchesStore) return false;

        return true;
      });
      
      const computedActual = projectCosts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.actual) || 0), 0);
      const computedForc = projectCosts.filter(c => c.costType === 'forc').reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.actual) || 0), 0);
      const computedSupportingFee = projectCosts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.supportingFee) || 0), 0);

      const fin = p.financial || { totalApproved: p.budget || 0, totalActualPaid: p.actual || 0, totalForecastPaid: 0 };
      const hasCosts = projectCosts.length > 0;
      const finalActual = hasCosts ? computedActual : (fin.totalActualPaid || p.actual || 0);
      const finalForc = hasCosts ? computedForc : (fin.totalForecastPaid || 0);
      const finalSupportingFee = hasCosts ? computedSupportingFee : (p.supportingFee || 0);

      const pData = {
        ...p,
        ownActual: finalActual,
        ownForc: finalForc,
        ownSupportingFee: finalSupportingFee,
        hasForc: finalForc > 0,
        ownBudget: fin.totalApproved || p.budget || 0,
      };

      projectMap.set(key, pData);

      const codeKey = String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!codeToProjects.has(codeKey)) {
        codeToProjects.set(codeKey, []);
      }
      codeToProjects.get(codeKey)!.push(pData);
    });

    const getProjectYear = (p: any): string => 
      p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : '';

    const getProjectStore = (p: any): string => 
      String(p.store || '').trim().toUpperCase();

    // Map linked children to parent codes, scoped by parentCode + year + store
    const parentToChildrenMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
    const linkedChildIds = new Set<string>();

    projects.forEach(p => {
      if (p.parentId && String(p.parentId).trim()) {
        const parentCode = String(p.parentId).trim().toUpperCase();
        const candidateParents = codeToProjects.get(parentCode);
        if (candidateParents && candidateParents.length > 0) {
          let childYear = getProjectYear(p);
          let childStore = getProjectStore(p);

          // If child year/store missing, inherit from candidate parent if unambiguous
          if (!childYear && candidateParents.length === 1) {
            childYear = getProjectYear(candidateParents[0]);
          }
          if (!childStore && candidateParents.length === 1) {
            childStore = getProjectStore(candidateParents[0]);
          }

          const scopedKey = `${parentCode}_${childYear}_${childStore}`;
          if (!parentToChildrenMap.has(scopedKey)) {
            parentToChildrenMap.set(scopedKey, []);
          }
          const key = `${String(p.code).trim().toLowerCase()}_${String(p.year || '')}_${String(p.store || '')}`;
          const pData = projectMap.get(key) || p;
          parentToChildrenMap.get(scopedKey)!.push(pData);
          linkedChildIds.add(p.id || `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`);
        }
      }
    });

    const roots: BudgetTreeNode[] = [];

    projects.forEach(p => {
      const pId = p.id || `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`;

      // If project is linked as child of another project, it is only a child, not a root parent
      if (linkedChildIds.has(pId)) {
        return;
      }

      const key = `${String(p.code).trim().toLowerCase()}_${String(p.year || '')}_${String(p.store || '')}`;
      const pData = projectMap.get(key) || p;
      const parentCode = String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase();
      const pYear = getProjectYear(p);
      const pStore = getProjectStore(p);
      const scopedKey = `${parentCode}_${pYear}_${pStore}`;

      // Child 1: Self-Child of this project (its own budget)
      const selfBudget = pData.ownBudget ?? 0;
      const selfActual = pData.ownActual ?? 0;
      const selfSupportingFee = pData.ownSupportingFee ?? 0;
      const selfRemaining = selfBudget - selfActual + selfSupportingFee;

      const selfChild: BudgetTreeNode = {
        id: `${pId}_self`,
        isSelf: true,
        isParentNode: false,
        code: p.code,
        name: p.name,
        year: p.year,
        store: p.store,
        location: p.location,
        type: p.type,
        note: p.note,
        budget: selfBudget,
        totalBudget: selfBudget,
        ownActual: selfActual,
        totalActual: selfActual,
        ownSupportingFee: selfSupportingFee,
        totalSupportingFee: selfSupportingFee,
        hasForc: pData.hasForc,
        remaining: selfRemaining,
        project: p,
        children: [],
      };

      // Child 2+: Any other projects linked as children under this parent in the same year and store
      const matchedChildren = parentToChildrenMap.get(scopedKey) || [];
      const linkedProjects: BudgetTreeNode[] = matchedChildren.map((childP: any) => {
        const childId = childP.id || `${childP.code}-${childP.year}-${childP.store}-${childP.no}`;
        const cBudget = childP.ownBudget ?? 0;
        const cActual = childP.ownActual ?? 0;
        const cSupportingFee = childP.ownSupportingFee ?? 0;
        const cRemaining = cBudget - cActual + cSupportingFee;

        return {
          id: childId,
          isSelf: false,
          isParentNode: false,
          code: childP.code,
          name: childP.name,
          year: childP.year,
          store: childP.store,
          location: childP.location,
          type: childP.type,
          note: childP.note,
          budget: cBudget,
          totalBudget: cBudget,
          ownActual: cActual,
          totalActual: cActual,
          ownSupportingFee: cSupportingFee,
          totalSupportingFee: cSupportingFee,
          hasForc: childP.hasForc,
          remaining: cRemaining,
          parentId: childP.parentId,
          project: childP,
          children: [],
        };
      });

      const allChildren = [selfChild, ...linkedProjects];

      // Roll-up sums for Parent Node (Budget Mẹ) utilizing central financialService
      const rollup = calculateParentBudgetRollup(allChildren);

      const parentNode: BudgetTreeNode = {
        ...pData,
        id: pId,
        isParentNode: true,
        isSelf: false,
        totalBudget: rollup.totalBudget,
        totalActual: rollup.totalActual,
        totalSupportingFee: rollup.totalSupportingFee,
        ownSupportingFee: selfSupportingFee,
        hasForc: rollup.hasForc,
        remaining: rollup.remaining,
        children: allChildren,
        project: p,
      };

      roots.push(parentNode);
    });

    return roots;
}

export function useBudgetTreeData({ projects, costs }: UseBudgetTreeDataParams): BudgetTreeNode[] {
  return useMemo(() => buildBudgetTreeData(projects, costs), [projects, costs]);
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/GeneralTabParts/useGeneralGoogleSync.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Project, DelegationGroup, AppUser } from '../../types';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { getStatus, formatDisplayDateMain } from '../../lib/utils';

interface UseGeneralGoogleSyncParams {
  currentUser: AppUser;
  projects: Project[];
  delegationGroups: DelegationGroup[];
}

export function useGeneralGoogleSync({
  currentUser,
  projects,
  delegationGroups
}: UseGeneralGoogleSyncParams) {
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);

  const handleGoogleSync = async () => {
    if (currentUser.role !== 'admin') return;
    if (!useAppStore.getState().isDelegationsRequested) {
      useAppStore.getState().setIsDelegationsRequested(true);
    }
    const WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbwPAZcZ8wGQ1CVPYn_5h4arJ2dqX7T9rpWPMAmEuc3Q2G4BB7l59Mbxb8Ep0aQE6GA1/exec';
    
    setIsSyncing(true);
    
    const projectsData = projects.map(p => {
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year) : '';
      const status = getStatus(p.computedTakStart, p.computedTakCompl, p.computedOpening || p.opening, p.code);
      
      const projectDelegations = delegationGroups.filter(g => g.projectCode === p.code);
      let delegationTaskStr = '';
      if (projectDelegations.length > 0) {
        delegationTaskStr = projectDelegations.map(g => {
          let st = g.subTasks?.[0]?.delegationStatus || g.subTasks?.[0]?.status || 'Assigned';
          if (st === 'Accepted') st = 'On process';
          return `${g.name} (${st})`;
        }).join('; ');
      }
      
      const now = new Date();
      let activeTaskNames: string[] = [];
      if (p.taskSummaries) {
        const activeTasks = p.taskSummaries.filter(t => {
          if (!t.start || !t.finish) return false;
          const start = new Date(t.start);
          const finish = new Date(t.finish);
          return now >= start && now <= finish;
        });
        activeTaskNames = activeTasks.map(t => t.name);
      }
      let taskLines: string[] = [];
      activeTaskNames.forEach(name => {
        taskLines.push(...name.split('\n').filter((l: string) => l.trim() !== ''));
      });
      let detailString = taskLines.length > 0 ? taskLines.join('\n') : (p.detail || '');
      
      return {
        projectId: p.id || p.no, // Unique ID for upsert
        year: pYear,
        store: p.store || '',
        location: p.location || '',
        code: p.code || '',
        linkCodes: p.linkCodes || p.code || '',
        projectName: p.name || '',
        status: status,
        detail: detailString,
        startPlan: p.computedTakStart ? formatDisplayDateMain(p.computedTakStart) : '',
        finishPlan: p.computedTakCompl ? formatDisplayDateMain(p.computedTakCompl) : '',
        delegationTask: delegationTaskStr
      };
    });

    try {
      await fetch(WEBHOOK_URL, {
        method: 'POST',
        mode: 'no-cors',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'upsert', projects: projectsData })
      });
      
      useAppStore.getState().setToast({ title: "Notice", message: "Sent project sync request to Google Sheets!" });
    } catch(err) {
      console.error(err);
      useAppStore.getState().setToast({ title: "Error", message: (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)) });
    } finally {
      setIsSyncing(false);
    }
  };

  return {
    isSyncing,
    handleGoogleSync
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/components/GeneralTabParts/useGeneralData.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { useDeferredValue, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Project, Task, AppUser } from '../../types';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { 
  getStatus, 
  getProjectDatesAndStatus,
  matchProjectStatusFilter,
  parseDateTime, 
  getUserAllowedStores, 
  getEffectiveStoreFilter, 
  matchesAdvancedSearch 
} from '../../lib/utils';
import { useLocationOptions } from '../../hooks/useLocationOptions';
import { safeGetItemSession, safeSetItemSession } from '../../lib/storage';
import { SortConfig } from './generalTypes';

interface UseGeneralDataParams {
  projects: Project[];
  tasks: Task[];
  currentUser: AppUser;
}

const statusWeight: Record<string, number> = {
  'On process': 1,
  'Upcoming': 2,
  'Completed': 3,
  'Handover': 4,
  'Canceled': 5
};

export function useGeneralData({
  projects,
  tasks,
  currentUser
}: UseGeneralDataParams) {
  const globalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalYearFilter);
  const setGlobalYearFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalYearFilter);
  const globalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalStoreFilter);
  const setGlobalStoreFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalStoreFilter);
  const effectiveStore = getEffectiveStoreFilter(globalStoreFilter, currentUser);
  const globalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.globalLocationFilter);
  const setGlobalLocationFilter = useAppStore(state => state.setGlobalLocationFilter);
  const pinnedProjects = useAppStore(state => state.pinnedProjects);
  const setPinnedProjects = useAppStore(state => state.setPinnedProjects);
  const theme = useAppStore(state => state.theme);

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const deferredSearchTerm = useDeferredValue(searchTerm);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState(() => safeGetItemSession("taka_general_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days');
  const lastSelectedStatusFilterRef = useRef<string>(safeGetItemSession("taka_general_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days');
  const lastSelectedLocationFilterRef = useRef<string[]>(globalLocationFilter);
  const wasSearchingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  const [flashToggle, setFlashToggle] = useState(true);
  const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState<SortConfig | null>(null);
  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState(20);

  // Status and Location Filter Preservation when Searching
  useEffect(() => {
    const isSearching = !!deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!wasSearchingRef.current && isSearching) {
      if (statusFilter !== 'all') {
        lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current = statusFilter;
        safeSetItemSession("taka_general_status_filter", statusFilter);
      }
      lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current = globalLocationFilter;
      setStatusFilter('all');
      wasSearchingRef.current = true;
    } else if (wasSearchingRef.current && !isSearching) {
      const restored = lastSelectedStatusFilterRef.current || safeGetItemSession("taka_general_status_filter") || 'On process + Upcoming 3 days';
      setStatusFilter(restored);
      setGlobalLocationFilter(lastSelectedLocationFilterRef.current || []);
      wasSearchingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [deferredSearchTerm]);

  // Auto-jump to matched project location(s) when searching (Debounced)
  useEffect(() => {
    const term = deferredSearchTerm.trim();
    if (!term) return;

    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const matchedProjects = projects.filter(p => {
        const status = getStatus(p.computedTakStart, p.computedTakCompl, p.computedOpening || p.opening, p.code);
        const searchFields = [p.no, p.store, p.code, p.linkCodes, p.name, p.detail, (p as any).description, p.note, p.party, status, p.year];
        const matchesSearch = matchesAdvancedSearch(term, searchFields);
        const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year) : null;
        const matchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || pYear === globalYearFilter;
        const matchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || p.store === effectiveStore;
        return matchesSearch && matchesYear && matchesStore;
      });
      const matchedLocations = Array.from(
        new Set(matchedProjects.map(p => p.location).filter((loc): loc is string => !!loc && loc.trim() !== ''))
      );
      if (matchedLocations.length > 0) {
        const currentLocs = globalLocationFilter || [];
        const isSame = currentLocs.length === matchedLocations.length && currentLocs.every(l => matchedLocations.includes(l));
        if (!isSame) {
          setGlobalLocationFilter(matchedLocations);
        }
      }
    }, 350);

    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [deferredSearchTerm, projects, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter]);

  // Handle Escape key globally to clear search when not in modal
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && searchTerm) {
        const state = useAppStore.getState();
        const hasOpenModal =
          state.isProjectModalOpen ||
          state.isTaskModalOpen ||
          state.isUserModalOpen ||
          state.isLoginModalOpen ||
          state.isCalendarSyncModalOpen;
        if (hasOpenModal) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        const activeElement = document.activeElement;
        if (activeElement && typeof (activeElement as HTMLElement).blur === "function") {
          (activeElement as HTMLElement).blur();
        }
        setSearchTerm("");
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [searchTerm]);

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const projYears = projects.map(p => {
      if (p.year) return String(p.year);
      if (p.computedTakStart) return String(p.computedTakStart).substring(0, 4);
      return null;
    }).filter(Boolean) as string[];
    const taskYears = tasks.map(t => {
      if (t.projectYear) return String(t.projectYear);
      if (t.start) return String(t.start).substring(0, 4);
      return null;
    }).filter(Boolean) as string[];
    const baseYears = ['2026', '2027'];
    const unique = Array.from(new Set([...projYears, ...taskYears, ...baseYears])).filter(Boolean).sort();
    return unique.length > 0 ? unique : ['2026', '2027'];
  }, [projects, tasks, currentUser?.role]);

  const allowedStores = useMemo(() => getUserAllowedStores(currentUser), [currentUser]);
  const stores = allowedStores;
  const locationOptions = useLocationOptions();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (locationOptions.length > 0 && globalLocationFilter.length > 0) {
      const valid = globalLocationFilter.filter(loc => locationOptions.includes(loc));
      if (valid.length !== globalLocationFilter.length) {
        setGlobalLocationFilter(valid);
      }
    }
  }, [locationOptions, globalLocationFilter, setGlobalLocationFilter]);

  const filteredProjects = useMemo(() => {
    // Pre-calculate project dates & status once (O(N)) to eliminate O(N log N) recalculations during sorting
    const infoMap = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof getProjectDatesAndStatus>>();
    projects.forEach(p => {
      const pKey = p.id || p.code || String(p.no || '');
      infoMap.set(pKey, getProjectDatesAndStatus(p, tasks));
    });

    const getMemoizedInfo = (p: Project) => {
      const pKey = p.id || p.code || String(p.no || '');
      return infoMap.get(pKey) || getProjectDatesAndStatus(p, tasks);
    };

    return projects.filter(p => {
      const { computedTakStart, computedTakCompl, computedOpening, status } = getMemoizedInfo(p);
      const searchFields = [p.no, p.store, p.code, p.linkCodes, p.name, p.detail, (p as any).description, p.note, p.party, status, p.year];
      const matchesSearch = matchesAdvancedSearch(deferredSearchTerm, searchFields);
      const pYear = p.year ? String(p.year).trim() : null;
      const matchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || (pYear !== null && pYear === String(globalYearFilter).trim());
      const matchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || (Boolean(p.store) && String(p.store).toUpperCase().trim() === effectiveStore.toUpperCase().trim());
      const matchesLocation = globalLocationFilter.length === 0 || (p.location && globalLocationFilter.includes(p.location));

      const matchesStatus = matchProjectStatusFilter(status, statusFilter, computedTakStart);

      return matchesSearch && matchesYear && matchesStore && matchesLocation && matchesStatus;
    }).sort((a, b) => {
      const isPinnedA = a.id && pinnedProjects.includes(a.id);
      const isPinnedB = b.id && pinnedProjects.includes(b.id);
      
      if (isPinnedA !== isPinnedB) {
        return isPinnedA ? -1 : 1;
      }
      if (isPinnedA && isPinnedB) {
        const orderA = pinnedProjects.indexOf(a.id as string);
        const orderB = pinnedProjects.indexOf(b.id as string);
        if (orderA !== orderB) return orderA - orderB;
      }

      const infoA = getMemoizedInfo(a);
      const infoB = getMemoizedInfo(b);

      if (sortConfig) {
        let valA = '';
        let valB = '';
        if (sortConfig.key === 'start') {
          valA = infoA.computedTakStart || '9999-12-31';
          valB = infoB.computedTakStart || '9999-12-31';
        } else if (sortConfig.key === 'handover') {
          valA = infoA.computedTakCompl || '9999-12-31';
          valB = infoB.computedTakCompl || '9999-12-31';
        } else if (sortConfig.key === 'opening') {
          valA = infoA.computedOpening || a.opening || '9999-12-31';
          valB = infoB.computedOpening || b.opening || '9999-12-31';
        }
        
        if (valA < valB) return sortConfig.direction === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
        if (valA > valB) return sortConfig.direction === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
        return 0;
      }

      const statusA = infoA.status;
      const statusB = infoB.status;
      
      const weightA = statusWeight[statusA] || 99;
      const weightB = statusWeight[statusB] || 99;
      if (weightA !== weightB) {
        return weightA - weightB;
      }

      // Special sorting for 'On process' : prioritize finish date proximity to today
      if (statusA === 'On process') {
        const today = new Date();
        today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
        
        const finishA = parseDateTime(a.computedTakCompl || a.handover || a.computedOpening || a.opening);
        const finishB = parseDateTime(b.computedTakCompl || b.handover || b.computedOpening || b.opening);
        
        if (finishA && finishB) {
          const diffA = Math.abs(finishA.getTime() - today.getTime());
          const diffB = Math.abs(finishB.getTime() - today.getTime());
          if (diffA !== diffB) {
            return diffA - diffB;
          }
        } else if (finishA) {
          return -1;
        } else if (finishB) {
          return 1;
        }
      }

      const startA = a.computedTakStart || '9999-12-31';
      const startB = b.computedTakStart || '9999-12-31';
      if (startA !== startB) {
        return startA.localeCompare(startB);
      }

      const complA = a.computedTakCompl || '9999-12-31';
      const complB = b.computedTakCompl || '9999-12-31';
      if (complA !== complB) {
        return complA.localeCompare(complB);
      }

      const openA = a.computedOpening || a.opening || '9999-12-31';
      const openB = b.computedOpening || b.opening || '9999-12-31';
      if (openA !== openB) {
        return openA.localeCompare(openB);
      }
      
      return a.code.localeCompare(b.code) || String(a.year || '').localeCompare(String(b.year || ''));
    });
  }, [projects, tasks, deferredSearchTerm, globalYearFilter, effectiveStore, globalLocationFilter, statusFilter, pinnedProjects, sortConfig]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => setFlashToggle(prev => !prev), 600);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const paginatedProjects = useMemo(() => {
    return filteredProjects.slice(0, visibleCount);
  }, [filteredProjects, visibleCount]);

  return {
    globalYearFilter,
    setGlobalYearFilter,
    globalStoreFilter,
    setGlobalStoreFilter,
    effectiveStore,
    globalLocationFilter,
    setGlobalLocationFilter,
    pinnedProjects,
    setPinnedProjects,
    theme,
    searchTerm,
    setSearchTerm,
    deferredSearchTerm,
    statusFilter,
    setStatusFilter,
    lastSelectedStatusFilterRef,
    flashToggle,
    sortConfig,
    setSortConfig,
    visibleCount,
    setVisibleCount,
    years,
    stores,
    locationOptions,
    filteredProjects,
    paginatedProjects
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/budgetTreeService.ts
// ================================================================================

import { 
  calculatePaidBreakdown, 
  calculateBudgetRemaining, 
  BudgetRollupChild, 
  BudgetRollupResult 
} from './financialService';

export function calculateParentBudgetRollup(children: BudgetRollupChild[]): BudgetRollupResult {
  const totalBudget = children.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.budget) || 0), 0);
  const totalActual = children.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.totalActual) || 0), 0);
  const totalSupportingFee = children.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.totalSupportingFee) || 0), 0);
  const hasForc = children.some(c => Boolean(c.hasForc));
  const remaining = calculateBudgetRemaining(totalBudget, totalActual, totalSupportingFee);

  return {
    totalBudget,
    totalActual,
    totalSupportingFee,
    hasForc,
    remaining
  };
}

export interface BudgetNode {
  id: string;
  isSelf: boolean;
  isParentNode: boolean;
  code: string;
  name?: string;
  year?: string | number;
  store?: string;
  location?: string;
  type?: string;
  note?: string;
  budget: number;
  totalBudget: number;
  ownActual: number;
  totalActual: number;
  ownSupportingFee: number;
  totalSupportingFee: number;
  hasForc: boolean;
  remaining: number;
  project: any;
  parentId?: string;
  children: BudgetNode[];
  filteredChildren?: BudgetNode[];
  isSearchMatch?: boolean;
}

export interface BudgetTreeResult {
  roots: BudgetNode[];
  projectMap: Map<string, any>;
  codeToProjects: Map<string, any[]>;
}

export function buildBudgetTree(projects: any[], costs: any[]): BudgetTreeResult {
  const projectMap = new Map<string, any>();
  const codeToProjects = new Map<string, any[]>();

  projects.forEach(p => {
    const key = `${String(p.code).trim().toLowerCase()}_${String(p.year || '')}_${String(p.store || '')}`;
    const projectCosts = costs.filter(c => {
      const costCode = String(c.projectCode || (c as any).code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const pCode = String(p.code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (costCode !== pCode) return false;

      const costYear = String(c.projectYear || (c as any).year || '').trim();
      const pYear = String(p.year || '').trim();
      const matchesYear = !costYear || !pYear || costYear === pYear;
      if (!matchesYear) return false;

      const costStore = String(c.projectStore || (c as any).store || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const pStore = String(p.store || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const matchesStore = !costStore || !pStore || costStore === pStore;
      if (!matchesStore) return false;

      return true;
    });

    const computedActual = projectCosts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.actual) || 0), 0);
    const computedForc = projectCosts.filter(c => c.costType === 'forc').reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.actual) || 0), 0);
    const computedSupportingFee = projectCosts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.supportingFee) || 0), 0);

    const fin = p.financial || { totalApproved: p.budget || 0, totalActualPaid: p.actual || 0, totalForecastPaid: 0 };
    const hasCosts = projectCosts.length > 0;

    const finalActual = hasCosts ? computedActual : (fin.totalActualPaid || p.actual || 0);
    const finalForc = hasCosts ? computedForc : (fin.totalForecastPaid || 0);
    const finalSupportingFee = hasCosts ? computedSupportingFee : (p.supportingFee || 0);

    const pData = {
      ...p,
      ownActual: finalActual,
      ownForc: finalForc,
      ownSupportingFee: finalSupportingFee,
      hasForc: finalForc > 0,
      ownBudget: fin.totalApproved || p.budget || 0,
    };
    projectMap.set(key, pData);

    const codeKey = String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!codeToProjects.has(codeKey)) {
      codeToProjects.set(codeKey, []);
    }
    codeToProjects.get(codeKey)!.push(pData);
  });

  const parentToChildrenMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
  const linkedChildIds = new Set<string>();

  projects.forEach(p => {
    if (p.parentId && String(p.parentId).trim()) {
      const parentCode = String(p.parentId).trim().toUpperCase();
      if (codeToProjects.has(parentCode)) {
        if (!parentToChildrenMap.has(parentCode)) {
          parentToChildrenMap.set(parentCode, []);
        }
        const key = `${String(p.code).trim().toLowerCase()}_${String(p.year || '')}_${String(p.store || '')}`;
        const pData = projectMap.get(key) || p;
        parentToChildrenMap.get(parentCode)!.push(pData);
        linkedChildIds.add(p.id || `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`);
      }
    }
  });

  const roots: BudgetNode[] = [];

  projects.forEach(p => {
    const pId = p.id || `${p.code}-${p.year}-${p.store}-${p.no}`;
    if (linkedChildIds.has(pId)) {
      return;
    }

    const key = `${String(p.code).trim().toLowerCase()}_${String(p.year || '')}_${String(p.store || '')}`;
    const pData = projectMap.get(key) || p;
    const parentCode = String(p.code).trim().toUpperCase();

    const selfBudget = pData.ownBudget ?? 0;
    const selfActual = pData.ownActual ?? 0;
    const selfSupportingFee = pData.ownSupportingFee ?? 0;
    const selfRemaining = selfBudget - selfActual + selfSupportingFee;

    const selfChild: BudgetNode = {
      id: `${pId}_self`,
      isSelf: true,
      isParentNode: false,
      code: p.code,
      name: p.name,
      year: p.year,
      store: p.store,
      location: p.location,
      type: p.type,
      note: p.note,
      budget: selfBudget,
      totalBudget: selfBudget,
      ownActual: selfActual,
      totalActual: selfActual,
      ownSupportingFee: selfSupportingFee,
      totalSupportingFee: selfSupportingFee,
      hasForc: pData.hasForc,
      remaining: selfRemaining,
      project: p,
      children: [],
    };

    const linkedProjects = (parentToChildrenMap.get(parentCode) || []).map((childP: any) => {
      const childId = childP.id || `${childP.code}-${childP.year}-${childP.store}-${childP.no}`;
      const cBudget = childP.ownBudget ?? 0;
      const cActual = childP.ownActual ?? 0;
      const cSupportingFee = childP.ownSupportingFee ?? 0;
      const cRemaining = cBudget - cActual + cSupportingFee;

      return {
        id: childId,
        isSelf: false,
        isParentNode: false,
        code: childP.code,
        name: childP.name,
        year: childP.year,
        store: childP.store,
        location: childP.location,
        type: childP.type,
        note: childP.note,
        budget: cBudget,
        totalBudget: cBudget,
        ownActual: cActual,
        totalActual: cActual,
        ownSupportingFee: cSupportingFee,
        totalSupportingFee: cSupportingFee,
        hasForc: childP.hasForc,
        remaining: cRemaining,
        parentId: childP.parentId,
        project: childP,
        children: [],
      };
    });

    const allChildren = [selfChild, ...linkedProjects];
    const rollup = calculateParentBudgetRollup(allChildren);

    const parentNode: BudgetNode = {
      ...pData,
      id: pId,
      isParentNode: true,
      isSelf: false,
      totalBudget: rollup.totalBudget,
      totalActual: rollup.totalActual,
      totalSupportingFee: rollup.totalSupportingFee,
      ownSupportingFee: selfSupportingFee,
      hasForc: rollup.hasForc,
      remaining: rollup.remaining,
      children: allChildren,
      project: p,
    };

    roots.push(parentNode);
  });

  return { roots, projectMap, codeToProjects };
}

export interface BudgetFilterOptions {
  globalYearFilter?: string;
  effectiveStore?: string;
  globalLocationFilter?: string[];
  codeFilter?: string;
  searchTerm?: string;
  moneyFilter?: 'all' | 'has_money' | 'has_budget' | 'has_act_forc';
}

export function filterBudgetRoots(roots: BudgetNode[], options: BudgetFilterOptions = {}): BudgetNode[] {
  const {
    globalYearFilter = 'all',
    effectiveStore = 'all',
    globalLocationFilter = [],
    codeFilter = '',
    searchTerm = '',
    moneyFilter = 'all'
  } = options;

  const cleanCode = (codeFilter || '').replace(/["']/g, '').trim().toLowerCase();

  return roots.filter(node => {
    const pYear = node.year ? String(node.year) : null;
    const matchesYear = globalYearFilter === 'all' || pYear === globalYearFilter;
    const matchesStore = effectiveStore === 'all' || node.store === effectiveStore;
    const matchesLocation = globalLocationFilter.length === 0 || (node.location && globalLocationFilter.includes(node.location));

    const hasBudget = (node.totalBudget || 0) !== 0;
    const hasActForc = (node.totalActual || 0) !== 0;
    let matchesMoney = true;
    if (moneyFilter === 'has_money') matchesMoney = hasBudget || hasActForc;
    else if (moneyFilter === 'has_budget') matchesMoney = hasBudget;
    else if (moneyFilter === 'has_act_forc') matchesMoney = hasActForc;

    const baseParentMatch = matchesYear && matchesStore && matchesLocation && matchesMoney;

    const matchesCode = !cleanCode || String(node.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanCode) ||
      (node.children && node.children.some(c => String(c.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(cleanCode)));
    if (!matchesCode) return false;

    if (searchTerm) {
      const lowerTerm = searchTerm.toLowerCase().trim();
      const isParentMatch = [
        node.code, node.name, node.store, node.location, node.type, node.note, node.year,
        node.totalBudget, node.totalActual, node.remaining
      ].some(val => String(val ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(lowerTerm));

      const anyChildMatch = node.children && node.children.some(child => [
        child.code, child.name, child.store, child.location, child.type, child.note, child.year,
        child.budget, child.totalActual, child.remaining
      ].some(val => String(val ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(lowerTerm)));

      if (!isParentMatch && !anyChildMatch) return false;
    }

    return baseParentMatch;
  });
}

export function calculateBudgetGlobalTotals(filteredRoots: BudgetNode[]) {
  const totalBudget = filteredRoots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalBudget || 0), 0);
  const totalActual = filteredRoots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalActual || 0), 0);
  const totalSupportingFee = filteredRoots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalSupportingFee || 0), 0);
  const totalRemaining = totalBudget - totalActual + totalSupportingFee;
  return { totalBudget, totalActual, totalSupportingFee, totalRemaining };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/storageBudgetService.ts
// ================================================================================

import { localDb } from './localDb';

/**
 * Storage Budget Service
 * Giám sát và quản trị trần bộ nhớ IndexedDB cho iOS Safari (40MB limit theo chuẩn AGENTS.md)
 * Ngăn ngừa WebKit kích hoạt Emergency Wipe khi iPhone ngủ 2-4-8 tiếng
 */

const IS_IOS = typeof window !== 'undefined' && (
  /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) ||
  (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1)
);

// Ngưỡng an toàn tối đa cho iOS (35MB để không chạm trần 40MB của WebKit)
const IOS_SAFE_QUOTA_BYTES = 35 * 1024 * 1024;

export interface StorageBudgetReport {
  usageBytes: number;
  quotaBytes: number;
  percentageUsed: number;
  isIOS: boolean;
  evictedCount: number;
  status: 'safe' | 'warning' | 'pruned';
}

/**
 * Kiểm tra và dọn dẹp các bản ghi năm cũ (LRU eviction) nếu chạm ngưỡng bộ nhớ Safari
 */
export async function enforceStorageBudget(): Promise<StorageBudgetReport> {
  const result: StorageBudgetReport = {
    usageBytes: 0,
    quotaBytes: IOS_SAFE_QUOTA_BYTES,
    percentageUsed: 0,
    isIOS: IS_IOS,
    evictedCount: 0,
    status: 'safe',
  };

  try {
    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.storage && navigator.storage.estimate) {
      const estimate = await navigator.storage.estimate();
      result.usageBytes = estimate.usage || 0;
      if (estimate.quota) {
        result.quotaBytes = IS_IOS ? Math.min(estimate.quota, IOS_SAFE_QUOTA_BYTES) : estimate.quota;
      }
      if (result.quotaBytes > 0) {
        result.percentageUsed = Math.round((result.usageBytes / result.quotaBytes) * 100);
      }
    }

    // Nếu là môi trường iOS và dung lượng vượt 80% ngưỡng an toàn
    const needsPruning = IS_IOS && (result.percentageUsed > 80 || result.usageBytes > IOS_SAFE_QUOTA_BYTES * 0.85);

    if (needsPruning) {
      const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
      const cutoffYear = String(currentYear - 2); // Giữ các năm hiện hành (2025, 2026); dọn <= 2024

      // Chỉ dọn dẹp các task đã hoàn thành thuộc các năm lưu trữ cũ khỏi Dexie nội bộ (không chạm vào Firestore)
      const oldTasks = await localDb.tasks
        .filter((t: any) => {
          const taskYear = String(t.projectYear || t.year || '');
          const status = String(t.runningStatus || t.status || '').toLowerCase();
          return Boolean(taskYear && taskYear <= cutoffYear && status === 'completed');
        })
        .toArray();

      if (oldTasks.length > 0) {
        const oldIds = oldTasks.map((t) => t.id);
        await localDb.tasks.bulkDelete(oldIds);
        result.evictedCount += oldIds.length;
        result.status = 'pruned';
        console.info(`[storageBudgetService] Evicted ${oldIds.length} completed tasks of <= ${cutoffYear} to preserve iOS 40MB limit.`);
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[storageBudgetService] Storage budget check completed safely:', err);
  }

  return result;
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/lib/deltaInvalidateService.ts
// ================================================================================

import { doc, getDocFromServer, getDoc } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db, APP_ID } from './firebase';
import { useAppStore } from '../store/useAppStore';
import { usePerformanceStore } from '../store/usePerformanceStore';
import type { TakaDelta, TakaDeltaItem } from '../types';

const PHYSICAL_COLLECTION: Record<TakaDeltaItem['collectionName'], string> = {
  taka_projects: 'taka_projects',
  taka_tasks: 'taka_tasks',
  taka_costs: 'taka_costs',
  taka_delegations: 'taka_delegation_groups',
};

/**
 * Với chế độ DELTA_FIRST: Mọi tín hiệu invalidate gửi qua taka_deltas đều là thay đổi thực tế
 * từ thiết bị khác (chỉ 1 doc/lần sửa). Luôn cho phép resolve để cập nhật ngay vào Dexie + Zustand,
 * đảm bảo khi người dùng chuyển sang bất kỳ tab nào thì dữ liệu ở tab đó cũng đã mới nhất 100%.
 */
function isRelevant(item: TakaDeltaItem): boolean {
  if (!item || !item.docId || !item.collectionName) return false;
  return true;
}

/**
 * Đọc lại đúng các document vừa nhận tín hiệu invalidate từ thiết bị khác (1 Read / 1 Doc sửa đổi)
 * và chuyển thành synthetic delta để applyIncomingDeltas cập nhật nguyên tử vào Zustand + Dexie.
 */
export async function resolveInvalidations(items: TakaDeltaItem[]): Promise<TakaDelta[]> {
  // Khử trùng lặp theo collectionName + docId
  const unique = new Map<string, TakaDeltaItem>();
  for (const it of items) {
    if (!isRelevant(it)) continue;
    unique.set(`${it.collectionName}:${it.docId}`, it);
  }
  if (unique.size === 0) return [];

  const now = Date.now();
  const nowIso = new Date(now).toISOString();

  const results = await Promise.all(
    Array.from(unique.values()).map(async (it): Promise<TakaDeltaItem | null> => {
      const physical = PHYSICAL_COLLECTION[it.collectionName];
      if (!physical) return null;
      try {
        const ref = doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', physical, it.docId);
        let snap;
        try {
          snap = await getDocFromServer(ref);
        } catch {
          snap = await getDoc(ref);
        }
        usePerformanceStore
          .getState()
          .trackGetDoc(physical, false, 1, `Delta invalidate re-read: ${it.docId}`);

        if (!snap.exists()) {
          return {
            docId: it.docId,
            collectionName: it.collectionName,
            action: 'delete',
            isPatch: false,
            data: { id: it.docId, isDeleted: true },
          };
        }
        const data = snap.data() as Record<string, any>;
        if (data?.isDeleted) {
          return {
            docId: it.docId,
            collectionName: it.collectionName,
            action: 'delete',
            isPatch: false,
            data: { id: it.docId, isDeleted: true, ...data },
          };
        }
        return {
          docId: it.docId,
          collectionName: it.collectionName,
          action: 'create',
          isPatch: false,
          data: { id: it.docId, ...data },
          year: String(data.year || data.projectYear || it.year || ''),
        };
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[deltaSync] Failed to re-read invalidated doc:', it.collectionName, it.docId, err);
        return null;
      }
    })
  );

  const resolved = results.filter((x): x is TakaDeltaItem => x !== null);
  if (resolved.length === 0) return [];

  return [
    {
      id: `delta_resolved_${now}`,
      action: 'batch_update',
      batchItems: resolved,
      updatedBy: 'delta-invalidate',
      sessionId: '__resolved__',
      timestamp: nowIso,
      numericTime: now,
    },
  ];
}

// FILE: src/components/DetailTabParts/useDetailTaskMutations.ts
// ================================================================================

import React, { Dispatch, SetStateAction } from 'react';
import { Project, Task, DelegationGroup, AppUser, TaskNote } from '../../types';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { doc } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { writeBatch } from '../../lib/firestoreWrites';
import { db, APP_ID } from '../../lib/firebase';
import { undoManager } from '../../lib/undoManager';
import { extractStorageUrls, deleteStorageFiles } from '../../lib/storageUtils';
import { 
  buildTaskNoMap, 
  splitPredecessors, 
  parsePredecessors, 
  computeTaskCode, 
  updatePredecessorsStrings, 
  calculateTaskDatesFromPredecessors, 
  scheduleDependentTasks 
} from '../../lib/ganttUtils';
import { parseDateTime, formatToDatetimeLocal, generateId } from '../../lib/utils';
import { cleanDurationString, rollupParentTaskDates } from '../../lib/taskImportService';
import { normalizeTaskNameForComparison } from '../../lib/taskDeduplicationService';
import { ensureProjectTasksLoaded, saveTaskStateUnified, deleteTaskStateUnified } from '../../lib/taskStateService';
import { recordBatchDeltas } from '../../lib/deltaSyncService';
import { createPatchDeltaItem } from '../../lib/deltaPatchService';
import { TakaDeltaItem } from '../../types';

interface UseDetailTaskMutationsParams {
  tasks: Task[];
  projects: Project[];
  delegationGroups: DelegationGroup[];
  currentUser: AppUser;
  setConfirmModal: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<{
    isOpen: boolean;
    title: string;
    message: string;
    onConfirm: () => Promise<void>;
  }>>;
}

export function useDetailTaskMutations({
  tasks,
  projects,
  delegationGroups,
  currentUser,
  setConfirmModal,
}: UseDetailTaskMutationsParams) {
  const handleDeleteTask = async (t: Task) => {
    setConfirmModal({
      isOpen: true,
      title: 'Delete Task',
      message: 'Delete this Task?',
      onConfirm: async () => {
        try {
          // Xóa tức thì khỏi Zustand store & đồng bộ nguyên tử Batch Firestore + Delta Sync
          // Hàm này đã bao gồm: Xóa task, Cập nhật Project Summary, Xóa Dexie, Ghi Delta Batch.
          await deleteTaskStateUnified([t]);
          
          undoManager.push({
            description: `Delete Task: ${t.name}`,
            revert: async () => {
              const { doc } = await import('firebase/firestore');
              const { writeBatch } = await import('../../lib/firestoreWrites');
              const { db, APP_ID } = await import('../../lib/firebase');
              const revertBatch = writeBatch(db);
              revertBatch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', t.id), t);
              
              // Restore related delegations if any
              const storeDelegations = useAppStore.getState().delegationGroups || [];
              const relatedDelGroups = storeDelegations.filter(d => d.parentTaskId === t.id || d.id === `del_${t.id}`);
              relatedDelGroups.forEach(d => {
                revertBatch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_delegation_groups', d.id), d);
              });

              revertBatch.commit().catch(console.error);
            }
          });
        } catch (e) {
          console.error(e);
        }
      }
    });
  };

  const handleUpdateTask = async (
    t: Task, 
    updates: Partial<Task>, 
    _options?: { skipPredecessorRecalc?: boolean; skipProjectRollup?: boolean }
  ) => {
    try {
      if (updates.name && updates.name !== t.name) {
        const currentProject = projects.find(p => 
          p.code === t.projectCode &&
          (String(p.year || '') === String(t.projectYear || '') || (!p.year && !t.projectYear)) &&
          (String(p.store || '') === String(t.projectStore || '') || (!p.store && !t.projectStore))
        );
        if (currentProject) {
          const normUpdateName = normalizeTaskNameForComparison(updates.name, currentProject.code);
          const isDuplicate = tasks.some(tsk => {
            if (tsk.id === t.id || tsk.isDeleted) return false;
            const sameProject = tsk.projectCode === currentProject.code &&
                   (String(tsk.projectYear || '') === String(currentProject.year || '') || (!tsk.projectYear && !currentProject.year)) &&
                   (String(tsk.projectStore || '') === String(currentProject.store || '') || (!tsk.projectStore && !currentProject.store));
            if (!sameProject) return false;

            if (tsk.name.trim().toLowerCase() === (updates.name || '').trim().toLowerCase()) return true;
            const tNorm = normalizeTaskNameForComparison(tsk.name, tsk.projectCode);
            return !!(normUpdateName && tNorm && normUpdateName === tNorm);
          });
          if (isDuplicate) {
            useAppStore.getState().setToast({ title: "Notice", message: `Task name "${updates.name}" already exists in project ${currentProject.code} (${currentProject.year} - ${currentProject.store}). Please enter a different name.` });
            return;
          }
        }
      }

      if (updates.predecessor !== undefined && updates.predecessor !== t.predecessor) {
        let val = updates.predecessor;
        const taskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, tasks);
        let parts = splitPredecessors(val, taskNoMap);
        if (parts.length > 2) parts = parts.slice(0, 2);
        
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
          updates.durationManual = false;
          if (!/FF|SF/i.test(parts[1])) {
            if (!/SS|FS/i.test(parts[1])) parts[1] += "FF";
            else parts[1] = parts[1].replace(/SS|FS/ig, "FF");
          }
          updates.predecessor = parts.join(', ');
        }
        parsePredecessors(updates.predecessor, taskNoMap, t);
      }

      let predecessorUpdates: { id: string; predecessor: string; updatedAt: string }[] = [];
      if (updates.taskCodePhu !== undefined && updates.taskCodePhu !== t.taskCodePhu) {
        const oldCode = (t.taskCodePhu || computeTaskCode(projects, tasks, t)).trim();
        const newCode = (updates.taskCodePhu || computeTaskCode(projects, tasks.map(item => item.id === t.id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item), { ...t, ...updates })).trim();
        if (oldCode && newCode && oldCode.toLowerCase() !== newCode.toLowerCase()) {
          const map = new Map<string, string>();
          map.set(oldCode.toLowerCase(), newCode);
          predecessorUpdates = updatePredecessorsStrings(map, tasks, projects);
        }
      }

      let targetTaskId = t.id;
      if (targetTaskId && targetTaskId.startsWith('ts_')) {
        // Cùng mã dự án chưa đủ: phải cùng store và year (mã phụ ảo `<mã>#<số>` trùng giữa các store/năm)
        const sameProjectIdentity = (x: any) =>
          String(x.projectStore || '').trim().toLowerCase() === String(t.projectStore || '').trim().toLowerCase() &&
          String(x.projectYear ?? '').trim() === String(t.projectYear ?? '').trim();
        let realTask = tasks.find(tsk => 
          !tsk.id.startsWith('ts_') &&
          tsk.projectCode === t.projectCode &&
          sameProjectIdentity(tsk) &&
          ((t.taskCodePhu && tsk.taskCodePhu === t.taskCodePhu) ||
           (t.name && tsk.name && tsk.name.trim().toLowerCase() === t.name.trim().toLowerCase()))
        );
        if (!realTask && t.projectCode) {
          try {
            const loaded = await ensureProjectTasksLoaded(t.projectCode);
            const loadedSame = loaded.filter(sameProjectIdentity);
            if (loadedSame.length > 0) {
              realTask = loadedSame.find(tsk =>
                (t.taskCodePhu && tsk.taskCodePhu === t.taskCodePhu) ||
                (t.name && tsk.name && tsk.name.trim().toLowerCase() === t.name.trim().toLowerCase())
              );
            }
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn("Failed to resolve ts_ task from local/cache:", err);
          }
        }
        if (realTask) {
          targetTaskId = realTask.id;
        } else {
          targetTaskId = `t_${Date.now()}_${generateId().substring(0, 8)}`;
        }
      }
      const tRef = doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', targetTaskId);
      
      let newT = { ...t, id: targetTaskId, ...updates, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
      if (updates.predecessor !== undefined) {
        let currentTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || tasks;
        let taskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, currentTasks);
        let parts = splitPredecessors(updates.predecessor, taskNoMap);
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
          newT.durationManual = false;
        }
        newT.realtimeUpdate = Boolean(updates.predecessor.trim());
        let parsed = parsePredecessors(updates.predecessor, taskNoMap, newT);
        
        if (updates.predecessor.trim() && (!parsed.length || parsed.some(p => !p.task))) {
          for (const token of parts) {
            const cleanToken = token.replace(/(fs|ss|ff|sf|[+-]\s*\d+|\s+\d+)$/i, '').trim();
            const matchedProj = projects.find(p => p.code && cleanToken.toLowerCase().startsWith(p.code.toLowerCase()));
            if (matchedProj && matchedProj.code !== t.projectCode) {
              const loaded = await ensureProjectTasksLoaded(matchedProj.code);
              if (loaded.length > 0) {
                currentTasks = useAppStore.getState().tasks || currentTasks;
                taskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, currentTasks);
                parsed = parsePredecessors(updates.predecessor, taskNoMap, newT);
              }
            }
          }
        }
        const calcResult = calculateTaskDatesFromPredecessors(newT, parsed);
        if (calcResult) {
          newT.start = calcResult.start;
          newT.finish = calcResult.finish;
          newT.duration = calcResult.duration;
          newT.durationManual = calcResult.durationManual;
        }
      } else if (newT.predecessor) {
        newT.realtimeUpdate = Boolean(newT.predecessor.trim());
      }
      
      if (updates.finish !== undefined) {
        if (newT.start && newT.finish) {
          const s = parseDateTime(newT.start);
          const f = parseDateTime(newT.finish);
          if (s && f && !isNaN(s.getTime()) && !isNaN(f.getTime())) {
            const diffDays = (f.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
            newT.duration = String(diffDays >= 0 ? Number(diffDays.toFixed(2)) : 0);
          }
        }
      } else if (updates.start !== undefined) {
        if (newT.durationManual) {
          if (newT.start && newT.duration !== undefined && newT.duration !== '') {
            const s = parseDateTime(newT.start);
            if (s && !isNaN(s.getTime())) {
              const days = parseFloat(String(newT.duration));
              if (!isNaN(days) && days >= 0) {
                const f = new Date(s.getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
                newT.finish = formatToDatetimeLocal(f);
              }
            }
          }
        } else {
          if (newT.start && newT.finish) {
            const s = parseDateTime(newT.start);
            const f = parseDateTime(newT.finish);
            if (s && f && !isNaN(s.getTime()) && !isNaN(f.getTime())) {
              const diffDays = (f.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
              newT.duration = String(diffDays >= 0 ? Number(diffDays.toFixed(2)) : 0);
            }
          }
        }
      } else if (updates.duration !== undefined) {
        newT.duration = cleanDurationString(updates.duration);
        newT.durationManual = true;
        if (newT.start && newT.duration !== '') {
          const s = parseDateTime(newT.start);
          if (s && !isNaN(s.getTime())) {
            const days = parseFloat(newT.duration);
            if (!isNaN(days) && days >= 0) {
              const f = new Date(s.getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
              newT.finish = formatToDatetimeLocal(f);
            }
          }
        }
      } else if (updates.predecessor === undefined) {
        if (newT.durationManual) {
          if (newT.start && newT.duration !== undefined && newT.duration !== '') {
            const s = parseDateTime(newT.start);
            if (s && !isNaN(s.getTime())) {
              const days = parseFloat(String(newT.duration));
              if (!isNaN(days) && days >= 0) {
                const f = new Date(s.getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
                newT.finish = formatToDatetimeLocal(f);
              }
            }
          }
        } else {
          if (newT.start && newT.finish) {
            const s = parseDateTime(newT.start);
            const f = parseDateTime(newT.finish);
            if (s && f && !isNaN(s.getTime()) && !isNaN(f.getTime())) {
              const diffDays = (f.getTime() - s.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
              newT.duration = String(diffDays >= 0 ? Number(diffDays.toFixed(2)) : 0);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      const updatesToDependents = scheduleDependentTasks(newT, tasks, projects);
      const batch = writeBatch(db);

      const scheduledUpdatedTask = updatesToDependents.find(d => d.id === newT.id);
      if (scheduledUpdatedTask) {
        newT = scheduledUpdatedTask as any;
      }

      const allCurrentTasksWithScheduled = tasks.map(tsk => {
        if (tsk.id === newT.id) return newT;
        const dep = updatesToDependents.find(d => d.id === tsk.id);
        return dep || tsk;
      });
      if (!allCurrentTasksWithScheduled.some(tsk => tsk.id === newT.id)) {
        allCurrentTasksWithScheduled.push(newT);
      }
      const { updatedParents } = rollupParentTaskDates(allCurrentTasksWithScheduled);

      batch.set(tRef, newT, { merge: true });

      // Rule 9 Mandate: Parent task rollups are computed 100% in-memory on client.
      // We keep updatedParents in memory via saveTaskStateUnified below, but do NOT issue Firestore writeBatch for parent tasks.

      if (updates.delegation === true) {
         const deterministicGroupId = `del_${t.id}`;
         const existingGroup = delegationGroups.find(g => g.parentTaskId === t.id || g.id === deterministicGroupId);
         if (!existingGroup) {
            const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10);
            const rawParty = (t.party && t.party.trim()) ? t.party.trim() : (currentUser.username || '');
            const parsedParty = rawParty.split(/[,;/+]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)[0] || rawParty;
            const newGroup: DelegationGroup = {
              id: deterministicGroupId,
              parentTaskId: t.id,
              name: t.name || 'Delegated Task',
              projectCode: t.projectCode,
              projectStore: t.projectStore,
              projectYear: (t.projectYear !== undefined && t.projectYear !== null && !isNaN(Number(t.projectYear))) ? Number(t.projectYear) : (Number(projects.find(p => p.code === t.projectCode)?.year) || new Date().getFullYear()),
              subTasks: [{ 
                 id: generateId(), 
                 name: '', 
                 comments: '', 
                 status: 'Assigned', 
                 delegationStatus: 'Assigned', 
                 priority: 'Mid', 
                 party: parsedParty, 
                 startAct: dateStr, 
                 finishAct: dateStr, 
                 updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() 
              }],
              updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
              createdBy: currentUser.id
            };
            const delRef = doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_delegation_groups', deterministicGroupId);
            batch.set(delRef, newGroup, { merge: true });
         }
      }

      const depUpdates = updatesToDependents.filter(d => d.id !== newT.id);
      depUpdates.forEach(depTask => {
        const depRef = doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', depTask.id);
        batch.set(depRef, { ...depTask, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }, { merge: true });
      });

      predecessorUpdates.forEach(pu => {
        if (pu.id !== t.id && !updatesToDependents.some(d => d.id === pu.id)) {
          const pRef = doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', pu.id);
          batch.update(pRef, { predecessor: pu.predecessor, updatedAt: pu.updatedAt });
        }
      });
      
      const allUpdatesToSave = [
        newT,
        ...depUpdates,
        ...updatedParents.filter(pt => pt.id !== newT.id && !depUpdates.some(d => d.id === pt.id)),
        ...predecessorUpdates.filter(pu => pu.id !== t.id && !depUpdates.some(d => d.id === pu.id))
      ];
      const deltaItems: TakaDeltaItem[] = allUpdatesToSave.map((item) => {
        const prev = tasks.find((tsk) => tsk.id === item.id);
        return createPatchDeltaItem({
          docId: item.id,
          collectionName: 'taka_tasks',
          previous: prev,
          updated: item,
          action: 'update',
          year: (item as any).projectYear ? String((item as any).projectYear) : (t.projectYear ? String(t.projectYear) : undefined),
        });
      });

      saveTaskStateUnified(allUpdatesToSave);

      try {
        const { markExplicitDelta } = await import('../../lib/autoDeltaQueue');
        markExplicitDelta(deltaItems);
        await batch.commit();
        recordBatchDeltas(deltaItems, currentUser.username || currentUser.id).catch(() => {});
      } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("Batch commit failed", error);
        useAppStore.getState().setToast({ title: "Save Failed", message: "Network error or permission denied. Data was not saved.", type: "error" });
        saveTaskStateUnified([t, ...depUpdates.map(d => tasks.find(tsk => tsk.id === d.id) || d)]);
        return;
      }
      
      undoManager.push({
        description: `Update Task Schedule: ${t.name}`,
        revert: async () => {
          const revertBatch = writeBatch(db);
          revertBatch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', t.id), t);
          updatesToDependents.forEach(depTask => {
            const originalDep = tasks.find(tsk => tsk.id === depTask.id);
            if (originalDep) {
              revertBatch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', depTask.id), originalDep);
            }
          });
          revertBatch.commit().catch(console.error);
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveNotes = async (taskId: string, notes: TaskNote[]) => {
    const t = tasks.find(item => item.id === taskId);
    if (t) {
      await handleUpdateTask(t, { notes });
    }
  };

  return {
    handleDeleteTask,
    handleUpdateTask,
    handleSaveNotes,
  };
}


// ================================================================================
// FILE: src/hooks/taskModal/taskModalSaveService.ts
// ================================================================================

import { doc, deleteField } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db, APP_ID, writeBatch } from '../../lib/firebase';
import { addPendingUpdate } from '../../lib/syncQueue';
import { undoManager } from '../../lib/undoManager';
import {
  getPredecessorCodeUpdates,
  updatePredecessorsStrings,
  parsePredecessors,
  computeTaskCode,
  buildTaskNoMap,
  scheduleDependentTasks,
  splitPredecessors
} from '../../lib/ganttUtils';
import { resolvePredecessorString, PredecessorContext } from '../../lib/predecessorMatchUtils';
import { generateId, getDefaultDatetimeLocal, formatToDatetimeLocal, parseDateTime } from '../../lib/utils';
import { syncProjectDatesByCode } from '../../lib/projectDatesService';
import { saveTaskStateUnified } from '../../lib/taskStateService';
import { useAppStore } from '../../store/useAppStore';
import { Project, Task, AppUser } from '../../types';
import { buildCanonicalProjectKey, normalizeTaskNameForComparison } from '../../lib/taskDeduplicationService';
import { recordBatchDeltas } from '../../lib/deltaSyncService';
import { createPatchDeltaItem } from '../../lib/deltaPatchService';

export interface TaskSaveParams {
  formData: Partial<Task>;
  task: Task | null;
  projects: Project[];
  tasks: Task[];
  cloudStatus: string;
  currentUser: AppUser;
  t: (key: string) => string;
  setToast: (toast: any) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  setIsSubmitting: (submitting: boolean) => void;
}

export async function executeTaskSave({
  formData,
  task,
  projects,
  tasks,
  cloudStatus,
  currentUser,
  setToast,
  onClose,
  setIsSubmitting
}: TaskSaveParams): Promise<boolean> {
  const currentProject = projects.find(p => 
    p.code === formData.projectCode &&
    (String(p.year || '') === String(formData.projectYear || '') || (!p.year && !formData.projectYear)) &&
    (String(p.store || '') === String(formData.projectStore || '') || (!p.store && !formData.projectStore))
  );

  if (!currentProject) {
    useAppStore.getState().setToast({ title: "Notice", message: "Please select a valid project." });
    setIsSubmitting(false);
    return false;
  }

  const currentYear = (!isNaN(Number(currentProject?.year)) && currentProject?.year !== null && currentProject?.year !== '') 
    ? Number(currentProject.year) 
    : (formData.projectYear && !isNaN(Number(formData.projectYear)) ? Number(formData.projectYear) : new Date().getFullYear());

  const targetPrjKey = buildCanonicalProjectKey(
    currentProject.code || formData.projectCode,
    currentProject.year || formData.projectYear,
    currentProject.store || formData.projectStore,
    projects
  );

  // Check for duplicate task name or code in the same project
  const normFormName = normalizeTaskNameForComparison(formData.name, formData.projectCode || currentProject?.code);
  const formTaskCode = formData.taskCodePhu ? String(formData.taskCodePhu).trim().toLowerCase() : '';

  const isDuplicate = tasks.some(t => {
    if (t.id === task?.id || t.isDeleted) return false;
    
    const tPrjKey = buildCanonicalProjectKey(t.projectCode, t.projectYear, t.projectStore, projects);
    const sameProject = (tPrjKey === targetPrjKey);
    if (!sameProject) return false;

    // Check exact name
    if (t.name && String(t.name).trim().toLowerCase() === String(formData.name || '').trim().toLowerCase()) {
      return true;
    }
    // Check normalized name
    const tNorm = normalizeTaskNameForComparison(t.name, t.projectCode);
    if (normFormName && tNorm && normFormName === tNorm) {
      return true;
    }
    // Check taskCodePhu
    if (formTaskCode && t.taskCodePhu && String(t.taskCodePhu).trim().toLowerCase() === formTaskCode) {
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  });

  if (isDuplicate) {
    setToast({ title: "Duplicate Task", message: `Task "${formData.name}" already exists in this project. Duplicate sub-tasks are not allowed.` });
    setIsSubmitting(false);
    return false;
  }

  let id = task ? task.id : `t_${Date.now()}_${generateId().substring(0, 8)}`;
  if (id && id.startsWith('ts_')) {
    let realTask = tasks.find(t => 
      !t.id.startsWith('ts_') &&
      t.projectCode === currentProject.code &&
      (String(t.projectStore || '') === String(currentProject.store || '')) &&
      (String(t.projectYear || '') === String(currentProject.year || '')) &&
      ((task?.taskCodePhu && t.taskCodePhu === task.taskCodePhu) ||
       (task?.name && t.name && t.name.trim().toLowerCase() === task.name.trim().toLowerCase()))
    );
    if (!realTask && currentProject?.code) {
      try {
        const { ensureProjectTasksLoaded } = await import('../../lib/taskStateService');
        const sameIdentity = (x: Task) =>
          String(x.projectStore || '').trim().toLowerCase() === String(currentProject.store || '').trim().toLowerCase() &&
          String(x.projectYear ?? '').trim() === String(currentProject.year ?? '').trim();
        const loaded: Task[] = await ensureProjectTasksLoaded(currentProject.code);
        const loadedSame = loaded.filter(sameIdentity);
        if (loadedSame.length > 0) {
          realTask = loadedSame.find(t =>
            (task?.taskCodePhu && t.taskCodePhu === task.taskCodePhu) ||
            (task?.name && t.name && t.name.trim().toLowerCase() === task.name.trim().toLowerCase())
          );
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Failed to find real task for ts_ in local/cache:", err);
      }
    }
    if (realTask) {
      id = realTask.id;
    } else {
      id = `t_${Date.now()}_${generateId().substring(0, 8)}`;
    }
  }
  let newOrderIndex = task?.orderIndex;
  
  if (!task) {
    const existingProjectTasks = tasks.filter(t => 
      t.projectCode === currentProject.code && 
      (String(t.projectYear || '') === String(currentProject.year || '') || (!t.projectYear && !currentProject.year)) &&
      (String(t.projectStore || '') === String(currentProject.store || '') || (!t.projectStore && !currentProject.store))
    );
    let maxOrder = 0;
    existingProjectTasks.forEach(t => {
      const order = t.orderIndex !== undefined ? t.orderIndex : 0;
      if (order > maxOrder) maxOrder = order;
    });
    newOrderIndex = maxOrder + 1;
  }

  if (formData.predecessor !== undefined && (!task || formData.predecessor !== task.predecessor)) {
    if (formData.predecessor) {
      const predContext: PredecessorContext = {
        projectId: formData.projectId || task?.projectId || currentProject?.id,
        projectCode: formData.projectCode || task?.projectCode || currentProject?.code,
        projectYear: formData.projectYear || task?.projectYear || currentProject?.year,
        projectStore: formData.projectStore || task?.projectStore || currentProject?.store,
        projectLocation: formData.location || task?.location || currentProject?.location
      };
      formData.predecessor = resolvePredecessorString(formData.predecessor, tasks, projects, formData.projectCode, predContext, formData.predecessorTaskIds);
      const taskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, tasks);
      let parts = splitPredecessors(formData.predecessor, taskNoMap);
      if (parts.length >= 2) {
        if (!/FF|SF/i.test(parts[1])) {
          if (!/SS|FS/i.test(parts[1])) parts[1] += "FF";
          else parts[1] = parts[1].replace(/SS|FS/ig, "FF");
        }
        formData.predecessor = parts.join(", ");
      }
    }
    const taskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, tasks);
    const dummyTask: any = {
      projectId: formData.projectId || task?.projectId || currentProject?.id,
      projectCode: formData.projectCode || task?.projectCode || currentProject?.code,
      projectYear: formData.projectYear || task?.projectYear || currentProject?.year,
      projectStore: formData.projectStore || task?.projectStore || currentProject?.store,
      location: formData.location || task?.location || currentProject?.location
    };
    parsePredecessors(formData.predecessor, taskNoMap, dummyTask);
  }

  try {
    const todayDate = getDefaultDatetimeLocal();
    let finalFormData = { ...formData };
    if (!finalFormData.start) finalFormData.start = todayDate;
    if (!finalFormData.finish) finalFormData.finish = finalFormData.start || todayDate;
    if (!finalFormData.baselineStart) finalFormData.baselineStart = finalFormData.start || todayDate;
    if (!finalFormData.baselineFinish) finalFormData.baselineFinish = finalFormData.finish || todayDate;
    if (finalFormData.duration === undefined || finalFormData.duration === null || String(finalFormData.duration).trim() === '') {
      finalFormData.duration = "0";
    }
    let durationVal = parseFloat(String(finalFormData.duration || "0"));
    if (durationVal < 0) {
      finalFormData.duration = "0";
      finalFormData.finish = finalFormData.start;
    }
    if (finalFormData.durationManual ?? true) {
      finalFormData.durationManual = true;
      if (finalFormData.start && finalFormData.duration !== undefined && finalFormData.duration !== '') {
        const s = parseDateTime(finalFormData.start);
        const days = parseFloat(String(finalFormData.duration));
        if (s && !isNaN(s.getTime()) && !isNaN(days)) {
          const f = new Date(s.getTime() + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
          finalFormData.finish = formatToDatetimeLocal(f);
        }
      }
    }
    if (!finalFormData.party) {
      finalFormData.party = currentProject?.party || 'Takashimaya';
    }

    if (cloudStatus === 'offline') {
      let payload = {
        ...finalFormData,
        id,
        projectYear: Number(currentYear) || new Date().getFullYear(),
        comments: task?.comments || finalFormData.comments || [],
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        orderIndex: newOrderIndex
      };
      addPendingUpdate({ collectionPath: `artifacts/${APP_ID}/public/data/taka_tasks`, action: 'set', docId: id, data: payload } as any);
      setToast({ title: 'Draft Saved', message: 'Task saved offline. Will sync when connection returns.' });
      onClose();
      setIsSubmitting(false);
      return true;
    }
    
    let dataToSave: any = {
      ...finalFormData,
      id,
      projectYear: Number(currentYear) || new Date().getFullYear(),
      comments: task?.comments || finalFormData.comments || [],
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      orderIndex: newOrderIndex
    };

    if (dataToSave.predecessor && dataToSave.predecessor.trim()) {
      dataToSave.realtimeUpdate = true;
    } else {
      dataToSave.realtimeUpdate = false;
    }

    // Explicitly delete fields from Firestore if unlinked or cleared
    if (task?.parentTaskId && !finalFormData.parentTaskId) {
      dataToSave.parentTaskId = deleteField();
      dataToSave.parentId = null;
    }
    if (task?.taskCodePhu && (!finalFormData.taskCodePhu || !finalFormData.taskCodePhu.trim())) {
      dataToSave.taskCodePhu = deleteField();
    }

    Object.keys(dataToSave).forEach(key => {
      if ((dataToSave as any)[key] === undefined) {
        delete (dataToSave as any)[key];
      }
    });

    const oldTaskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, tasks);
    const newTasksForMap = task 
       ? tasks.map(t => t.id === id ? dataToSave as Task : t) 
       : [...tasks, dataToSave as Task];
    const newTaskNoMap = buildTaskNoMap(projects, newTasksForMap);
    const oldCodeToNewCode = getPredecessorCodeUpdates(oldTaskNoMap, newTaskNoMap, projects, newTasksForMap);

    if (task) {
      const oldCode = (task.taskCodePhu || computeTaskCode(projects, tasks, task)).trim();
      const newCode = (dataToSave.taskCodePhu || computeTaskCode(projects, newTasksForMap, dataToSave as Task)).trim();
      if (oldCode && newCode && oldCode.toLowerCase() !== newCode.toLowerCase()) {
        oldCodeToNewCode.set(oldCode.toLowerCase(), newCode);
      }
    }

    const predecessorUpdatesOrig = updatePredecessorsStrings(oldCodeToNewCode, tasks, projects, oldTaskNoMap);
    const predecessorUpdates = predecessorUpdatesOrig.filter(pu => pu.id !== id);
    const predecessorUpdatesMap = new Map(predecessorUpdates.map(pu => [pu.id, pu]));

    const updatesToDependents = scheduleDependentTasks(dataToSave as Task, tasks, projects);
    
    const scheduledUpdatedTask = updatesToDependents.find(d => d.id === dataToSave.id);
    if (scheduledUpdatedTask) {
      dataToSave = scheduledUpdatedTask as any;
    }

    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    if (task) {
      batch.update(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', id), dataToSave);
    } else {
      batch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', id), dataToSave);
    }

    const depUpdates = updatesToDependents.filter(d => d.id !== id);
    const mappedDepUpdates = new Set<string>();

    depUpdates.forEach(depTask => {
      const depProj = projects.find(p => p.code === depTask.projectCode);
      const depYear = (depTask.projectYear !== undefined && depTask.projectYear !== null && !isNaN(Number(depTask.projectYear)))
        ? Number(depTask.projectYear)
        : (depProj?.year && !isNaN(Number(depProj.year)) ? Number(depProj.year) : new Date().getFullYear());
      const pu = predecessorUpdatesMap.get(depTask.id);
      const data = pu 
        ? { ...depTask, projectYear: depYear, predecessor: pu.predecessor, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } 
        : { ...depTask, projectYear: depYear, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
      Object.keys(data).forEach(key => {
        if ((data as any)[key] === undefined) {
          delete (data as any)[key];
        }
      });
      batch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', depTask.id), data, { merge: true });
      mappedDepUpdates.add(depTask.id);
    });

    predecessorUpdates.forEach(pu => {
      if (!mappedDepUpdates.has(pu.id)) {
         batch.set(doc(db, 'artifacts', APP_ID, 'public', 'data', 'taka_tasks', pu.id), { predecessor: pu.predecessor, updatedAt: pu.updatedAt }, { merge: true });
      }
    });

    const optimisticList: any[] = [dataToSave];
    depUpdates.forEach(depTask => {
      const pu = predecessorUpdatesMap.get(depTask.id);
      if (pu) {
        optimisticList.push({ ...depTask, predecessor: pu.predecessor, updatedAt: pu.updatedAt });
      } else {
        optimisticList.push(depTask);
      }
    });
    predecessorUpdates.forEach(pu => {
      if (!mappedDepUpdates.has(pu.id)) {
        optimisticList.push({ id: pu.id, predecessor: pu.predecessor, updatedAt: pu.updatedAt });
      }
    });
    
    saveTaskStateUnified(optimisticList);

    const isNewTask = !task;
    const oldAny = (task || {}) as any;
    const newAny = dataToSave as any;
    const datesOrStatusChanged =
      isNewTask ||
      task?.start !== dataToSave.start ||
      task?.finish !== dataToSave.finish ||
      task?.runningStatus !== dataToSave.runningStatus ||
      task?.approval !== dataToSave.approval ||
      Boolean(oldAny.isCompleted) !== Boolean(newAny.isCompleted) ||
      (oldAny.status || '') !== (newAny.status || '') ||
      task?.projectCode !== dataToSave.projectCode ||
      task?.projectStore !== dataToSave.projectStore ||
      task?.projectYear !== dataToSave.projectYear ||
      depUpdates.length > 0;

    // Close modal INSTANTLY (0ms UI lag)
    onClose();
    setIsSubmitting(false);

    // Offload network commit, delta emission, and project date rollups to non-blocking background microtask
    setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const deltaItems = optimisticList.map(item => {
          const prev = tasks.find(t => t.id === item.id);
          return createPatchDeltaItem({
            docId: item.id,
            collectionName: 'taka_tasks',
            previous: prev,
            updated: item,
            action: prev ? 'update' : 'create',
            year: item.projectYear ? String(item.projectYear) : (task?.projectYear ? String(task.projectYear) : undefined),
          });
        });
        const { markExplicitDelta } = await import('../../lib/autoDeltaQueue');
        markExplicitDelta(deltaItems);
        await batch.commit();
        recordBatchDeltas(deltaItems, currentUser?.email || currentUser?.username).catch(() => {});

        if (datesOrStatusChanged) {
          const allTasksCurrent = useAppStore.getState().tasks;
